## Description

<!-- Thank you for contributing! Check CONTRIBUTING.md for details. -->

#### What type of PR is this?

/kind feature

#### What this PR does / why we need it?

Adds `kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta`, a guidance-only skill (one `SKILL.md`, no scripts). The PR author's coding agent picks the user-facing surfaces this PR can affect (API and CRD, install config, webhook messages, condition and event reasons, metrics, feature gates, logs, kueuectl), reads them at the merge base and the head, and writes a short before/after table under "Useful notes for your reviewer".

For API and CRD changes it adds a static compatibility check, and when a change likely needs an ACTION REQUIRED release note it says so and points to `kueue-release-notes`. Publishing follows `kueue-pr-evidence-common` (own PR, dry run first, `gh pr edit` only after approval).

Fixes #18144
Part of #18141

#### Useful notes for your reviewer

Reworked to match the guidance-only approach agreed in #18178: the earlier scripts, tests and fixtures are gone, and the agent chooses the surfaces case by case. The diff is now the `SKILL.md` plus a README row. `make verify-skills-lint` passes.

Example: the skill run as a dry run on #16632 (merged); the block was not posted there.

**kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta** base 60996403d464 -> head eb8fd70bbb86

1 user-facing contract change (kueuectl error text 1).

Runner: static · surfaces: kueuectl error text, flags, command help text, output columns

| Surface | Change | Before (merge base) | After (head) |
|---|---|---|---|
| kueuectl | create clusterqueue resource group overlap error | `invalid resource group` | `%w: flavor %q covers %v but flavor %q covers %v; flavors that share a resource must cover the same resources` |

Unchanged: kueuectl flags, command help text, output columns, other error strings.

<details><summary>Locations</summary>

| Surface | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| kueuectl | resource group overlap error | base `cmd/kueuectl/app/create/create_clusterqueue.go:528`; head `cmd/kueuectl/app/create/create_clusterqueue.go:522` |

`%w` wraps `errInvalidResourceGroup`, defined at line 84 as `invalid resource group`. `parseResourceGroups` returns the error at line 308.

</details>

## AI summary

Adds the `kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta` skill as a guidance-only `SKILL.md` (155 lines). The skill tells a PR author's coding agent to compare user-facing contract surfaces at the merge base and the PR head. It reads both sides with `git show <merge-base>:<path>` and `git show <head>:<path>`, and it does not use a worktree.

The skill covers these surfaces: API and CRD fields, defaults, validation, and CEL; Helm CRD copies and controller flags; webhook messages; condition, event, and constant strings; metrics; feature gates; log verbosity and keys; and `cmd/kueuectl/` flags, columns, and error text. The agent chooses surfaces case by case. It keeps only changed rows and shows at most 12 changed rows in a visible table within a 25-line budget. Extra rows go in a `<details>` block.

For API and CRD changes, the skill adds a Compatibility table with static findings or `unverified` results. It flags a likely ACTION REQUIRED when a metric, label, annotation, or log key is renamed or removed, a default changes, a gate default flips or locks, or a removal forces a user change. It refers the author to `kueue-release-notes` for note text.

The skill does not require every listed surface to be checked on each run. It also does not require a fixed `Unchanged:` list beyond the surfaces the agent checked.

The example block is labeled as shape only.

Publishing follows `kueue-pr-evidence-common`. The agent fetches the PR body, shows the diff, and runs `gh pr edit` only after explicit user approval.

The skills README adds a trigger-table row and an `@` path reference for the new skill. No scripts or code change.

### Suggested release note

NONE

**Explanation**

[`#18144`] The head `SKILL.md` still contains two unmet requirements from the previous review. The base-read defect is fixed: "How to compare" reads both sides with `git show &lt;merge-base&gt;:&lt;path&gt;` and `git show &lt;head&gt;:&lt;path&gt;`.

1. Surface coverage (unmet). The issue requires every listed surface to be checked. `SKILL.md` still says "Pick them case by case" and "Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it." An agent can skip listed surfaces such as feature gates, kueuectl, or log keys. The PR description cites `#18178` as the reason, but `#18178` does not change the issue acceptance criteria.
2. Rewording (unmet). The issue states that a reworded message counts as one change. `SKILL.md` still says "A reworded message you still recognize is one changed row. Otherwise record one removal and one addition." The Logs bullet repeats the recognition exception: "A recognized reworded log line counts as one change."
3. ACTION REQUIRED (met). The block-shape section flags metric, label, annotation, log key, default, gate, and flag cases, and points to `kueue-release-notes`.
4. Validation (unconfirmed). The acceptance criteria require the `#17747` log counts and the `#16632` kueuectl message list. The PR reports a `#16632` dry run, but its output is not in the diff. No `#17747` evidence is present.

**Resolution**

Revise `cmd/experimental/skills/kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta/SKILL.md` so that:
- Every listed surface (CRD fields and CEL, webhook messages, reason, label, and annotation constants and event reasons, metrics, feature gates, logs, kueuectl flags, columns, and error strings) is checked on each PR. Remove "Pick them case by case" and "Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it." Name unchanged surfaces on the `Unchanged:` line.
- Each reworded message counts as one changed row. Remove the "you still recognize" exception in "How to compare" and the "recognized reworded log line" exception in the Logs bullet.
After the revision, run the skill on `#17747` and `#16632`. Record the log counts and the changed kueuectl messages in the PR description or a linked comment.

</dd>
</dl>

</dd>

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.

+# limitations under the License.
+
+"""Idempotently place one pr-evidence block in a PR body's reviewer-notes section.
+
+Conventions: cmd/experimental/skills/kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md.
+Python 3 standard library only.
+
+    inject-pr-notes --pr <num|url> --skill kueue-pr-evidence-<name> --file <block.md> \
+        --base <sha> --head <sha> [--repo kubernetes-sigs/kueue] [--dry-run]
+
+Exit codes: 0 ok / no change, 1 gh failure, 2 usage or malformed body, 3 refused.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import difflib
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+
+SECTION_TITLE = "Useful notes for your reviewer"
+SECTION_HEADING = "#### " + SECTION_TITLE
+DEFAULT_REPO = "kubernetes-sigs/kueue"
+DEFAULT_MAX_VISIBLE_LINES = 25
+GITHUB_BODY_LIMIT = 65536
+
+SKILL_RE = re.compile(r"^kueue-pr-evidence-[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$")
+SHA_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{7,40}$")
+HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^ {0,3}(#{1,6})[ \t]+(.*?)[ \t#]*$")
+FENCE_RE = re.compile(r"^ {0,3}(`{3,}|~{3,})(.*)$")
+MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*pr-evidence:([a-z0-9-]+):(start|end)\s*-->")
+RELEASE_NOTE_TITLE_RE = re.compile(r"^release[ -]?notes?:?$", re.IGNORECASE)
+PR_URL_RE = re.compile(r"^https?://github\.com/([^/]+/[^/]+)/pull/(\d+)(?:[/?#].*)?$")
+
+
+class InjectError(Exception):
+    """Malformed input or body; exit 2."""
+
+
+class Refused(Exception):
+    """Safety refusal (author / stale head); exit 3."""
+
+
+def markers(skill: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
+    return f"<!-- pr-evidence:{skill}:start -->", f"<!-- pr-evidence:{skill}:end -->"
+
+
+def header_line(skill: str, base: str, head: str) -> str:
+    # Plain (non-code) SHAs so GitHub autolinks them to commits.
+    return f"**{skill}** base {base[:12]} -> head {head[:12]}"
+
+
+def visible_lines(content: str) -> int:
+    """Non-blank lines a reviewer sees without expanding <details>.
+
+    The <details> opening line (summary) counts once; its body does not.
+    """
+    depth = 0
+    count = 0
+    for line in content.split("\n"):
+        opens = len(re.findall(r"<details\b", line, re.IGNORECASE))
+        closes = len(re.findall(r"</details\s*>", line, re.IGNORECASE))
+        if depth == 0 and line.strip():
+            count += 1
+        depth = max(0, depth + opens - closes)
```

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at

+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+"""Idempotently place one pr-evidence block in a PR body's reviewer-notes section.
+
+Conventions: cmd/experimental/skills/kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md.
+Python 3 standard library only.
+
+    inject-pr-notes --pr <num|url> --skill kueue-pr-evidence-<name> --file <block.md> \
+        --base <sha> --head <sha> [--repo kubernetes-sigs/kueue] [--dry-run]
+
+Exit codes: 0 ok / no change, 1 gh failure, 2 usage or malformed body, 3 refused.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import difflib
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+
+SECTION_TITLE = "Useful notes for your reviewer"
+SECTION_HEADING = "#### " + SECTION_TITLE
+DEFAULT_REPO = "kubernetes-sigs/kueue"
+DEFAULT_MAX_VISIBLE_LINES = 25
+GITHUB_BODY_LIMIT = 65536
+
+SKILL_RE = re.compile(r"^kueue-pr-evidence-[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$")
+SHA_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{7,40}$")
+HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^ {0,3}(#{1,6})[ \t]+(.*?)[ \t#]*$")
+FENCE_RE = re.compile(r"^ {0,3}(`{3,}|~{3,})(.*)$")
+MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*pr-evidence:([a-z0-9-]+):(start|end)\s*-->")
+RELEASE_NOTE_TITLE_RE = re.compile(r"^release[ -]?notes?:?$", re.IGNORECASE)
+PR_URL_RE = re.compile(r"^https?://github\.com/([^/]+/[^/]+)/pull/(\d+)(?:[/?#].*)?$")
+
+
+class InjectError(Exception):
+    """Malformed input or body; exit 2."""
+
+
+class Refused(Exception):
+    """Safety refusal (author / stale head); exit 3."""
+
+
+def markers(skill: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
+    return f"<!-- pr-evidence:{skill}:start -->", f"<!-- pr-evidence:{skill}:end -->"
+
+
+def header_line(skill: str, base: str, head: str) -> str:
+    # Plain (non-code) SHAs so GitHub autolinks them to commits.
+    return f"**{skill}** base {base[:12]} -> head {head[:12]}"
+
+
+def visible_lines(content: str) -> int:
+    """Non-blank lines a reviewer sees without expanding <details>.
+
+    The <details> opening line (summary) counts once; its body does not.
+    """
+    depth = 0
+    count = 0
+    for line in content.split("\n"):
+        opens = len(re.findall(r"<details\b", line, re.IGNORECASE))
+        closes = len(re.findall(r"</details\s*>", line, re.IGNORECASE))
+        if depth == 0 and line.strip():
+            count += 1
+        depth = max(0, depth + opens - closes)
+    return count
+
+
+def render_block(skill: str, content: str, base: str, head: str) -> list[str]:
+    start, end = markers(skill)
+    body = content.replace("\r\n", "\n").strip("\n")
+    lines = [start, header_line(skill, base, head)]
+    if body:
+        lines += [""] + body.split("\n")
+    lines.append(end)
+    return lines
+
+
+def _validate_markers(lines: list[str]) -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
+    """Return {skill: (start_idx, end_idx)}; raise on unbalanced/nested/inline markers."""
+    spans: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
+    open_skill: str | None = None
+    open_idx = -1
+    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
+        found = MARKER_RE.findall(line)
+        if not found:
+            continue
```

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,102 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+# Throwaway detached kueue worktrees for generating the "before" side.
+#
+# Usage:
+#   kueue-worktree.sh add <sha> [--repo DIR]   # prints the new worktree path
+#   kueue-worktree.sh remove <path>            # only paths under the managed root
+#   kueue-worktree.sh list                     # managed worktrees
+#   kueue-worktree.sh gc [--repo DIR]          # remove all managed worktrees, prune
+#
+# Managed root: $PR_EVIDENCE_WORKTREE_ROOT, else
+#   ${XDG_CACHE_HOME:-~/.cache}/kueue-pr-evidence/worktrees
+#
+# `add` runs `git worktree add --detach` with hooks disabled. It never checks
+# out, resets, or switches branches in --repo (default: current directory); it
+# only adds an entry under the shared .git/worktrees/ that `remove` and `gc`
+# delete. The new tree has its own bin/; nothing is shared with the PR tree.
+
+set -o errexit
+set -o nounset
+set -o pipefail
+
+root="${PR_EVIDENCE_WORKTREE_ROOT:-${XDG_CACHE_HOME:-$HOME/.cache}/kueue-pr-evidence/worktrees}"
+
+die() { echo "kueue-worktree.sh: $*" >&2; exit 2; }
+usage() { sed -n '17,31p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; }
+
+abs() { (cd "$1" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P); }
+
+under_root() {
+  local p r
+  p="$(abs "$1")" || return 1
+  r="$(abs "$root")" || return 1
+  [[ "$p" == "$r"/* ]]
+}
+
+remove_one() {
+  local path="$1" common
+  under_root "$path" || die "refusing: $path is not under $root"
+  common="$(git -C "$path" rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir 2>/dev/null || true)"
+  if [[ -n "$common" ]]; then
+    git --git-dir="$common" worktree remove --force "$path" 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$path"
+    git --git-dir="$common" worktree prune
+  else
+    rm -rf "$path"
+  fi
+  echo "removed $path" >&2
+}
+
+cmd="${1:-}"
+[[ $# -gt 0 ]] && shift
+case "$cmd" in
+  add)
+    [[ $# -ge 1 ]] || die "add needs <sha>"
+    sha="$1"; shift
+    repo=.
+    while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
+      case "$1" in
+        --repo) repo="$2"; shift 2 ;;
+        *) die "unknown arg: $1" ;;
+      esac
+    done
+    full="$(git -C "$repo" rev-parse --verify --quiet "$sha^{commit}")" || die "unknown commit: $sha"
+    mkdir -p "$root"
+    path="$(mktemp -d "$root/${full:0:12}.XXXXXX")"
+    rmdir "$path"
+    git -C "$repo" -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null worktree add --quiet --detach "$path" "$full" >&2
+    echo "$path"
```

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,89 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+# Print the upstream merge base and HEAD SHAs of a kueue worktree.
+#
+# Usage: merge-base.sh [--worktree DIR] [--branch main] [--no-fetch]
+#
+# Prints eval-able KEY=VALUE lines on stdout:
+#   UPSTREAM_REMOTE=upstream
+#   UPSTREAM_REF=refs/remotes/upstream/main
+#   MERGE_BASE=<40-hex>
+#   HEAD_SHA=<40-hex>
+#
+# The remote is $PR_EVIDENCE_UPSTREAM_REMOTE if set, else `upstream` when it
+# points at kubernetes-sigs/kueue, else the first remote that does. The script
+# only fetches; it never pushes or changes the worktree.
+
+set -o errexit
+set -o nounset
+set -o pipefail
+
+worktree=.
+branch=main
+fetch=1
+while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
+  case "$1" in
+    --worktree) worktree="$2"; shift 2 ;;
+    --branch) branch="$2"; shift 2 ;;
+    --no-fetch) fetch=0; shift ;;
+    -h | --help) sed -n '17,29p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; exit 0 ;;
+    *) echo "merge-base.sh: unknown arg: $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+  esac
+done
+
+g() { git -C "$worktree" "$@"; }
+
+g rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
+  echo "merge-base.sh: not a git worktree: $worktree" >&2
+  exit 2
+}
+
+is_kueue_url() { [[ "$1" =~ github\.com[:/]kubernetes-sigs/kueue(\.git)?/?$ ]]; }
```

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,89 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+# Print the upstream merge base and HEAD SHAs of a kueue worktree.
+#
+# Usage: merge-base.sh [--worktree DIR] [--branch main] [--no-fetch]
+#
+# Prints eval-able KEY=VALUE lines on stdout:
+#   UPSTREAM_REMOTE=upstream
+#   UPSTREAM_REF=refs/remotes/upstream/main
+#   MERGE_BASE=<40-hex>
+#   HEAD_SHA=<40-hex>
+#
+# The remote is $PR_EVIDENCE_UPSTREAM_REMOTE if set, else `upstream` when it
+# points at kubernetes-sigs/kueue, else the first remote that does. The script
+# only fetches; it never pushes or changes the worktree.
+
+set -o errexit
+set -o nounset
+set -o pipefail
+
+worktree=.
+branch=main
+fetch=1
+while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
+  case "$1" in
+    --worktree) worktree="$2"; shift 2 ;;
+    --branch) branch="$2"; shift 2 ;;
+    --no-fetch) fetch=0; shift ;;
+    -h | --help) sed -n '17,29p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; exit 0 ;;
+    *) echo "merge-base.sh: unknown arg: $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+  esac
+done
+
+g() { git -C "$worktree" "$@"; }
+
+g rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
+  echo "merge-base.sh: not a git worktree: $worktree" >&2
+  exit 2
+}
+
+is_kueue_url() { [[ "$1" =~ github\.com[:/]kubernetes-sigs/kueue(\.git)?/?$ ]]; }
+
+remote="${PR_EVIDENCE_UPSTREAM_REMOTE:-}"
+if [[ -z "$remote" ]]; then
+  if is_kueue_url "$(g remote get-url upstream 2>/dev/null || true)"; then
+    remote=upstream
+  else
+    while read -r r; do
+      if is_kueue_url "$(g remote get-url "$r")"; then remote="$r"; break; fi
+    done < <(g remote)
+  fi
+fi
+if [[ -z "$remote" ]]; then
+  echo "merge-base.sh: no remote points at kubernetes-sigs/kueue; add one:" >&2
+  echo "  git remote add upstream https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue.git" >&2
+  exit 2
+fi
+
+if [[ "$fetch" == 1 ]]; then
+  g fetch --quiet "$remote" "$branch" >&2
+fi
+ref="refs/remotes/$remote/$branch"
+if ! g rev-parse --verify --quiet "$ref^{commit}" >/dev/null; then
+  ref=FETCH_HEAD
```

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+# Print the upstream merge base and HEAD SHAs of a kueue worktree.
+#
+# Usage: merge-base.sh [--worktree DIR] [--branch main] [--no-fetch]
+#
+# Prints eval-able KEY=VALUE lines on stdout:
+#   UPSTREAM_REMOTE=upstream
+#   UPSTREAM_REF=refs/remotes/upstream/main
+#   MERGE_BASE=<40-hex>
+#   HEAD_SHA=<40-hex>
+#
+# The remote is $PR_EVIDENCE_UPSTREAM_REMOTE if set, else `upstream` when it
+# points at kubernetes-sigs/kueue, else the first remote that does. The script
+# only fetches; it never pushes or changes the worktree.
+
+set -o errexit
+set -o nounset
+set -o pipefail
+
+worktree=.
+branch=main
+fetch=1
+while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
+  case "$1" in
+    --worktree) worktree="$2"; shift 2 ;;
+    --branch) branch="$2"; shift 2 ;;
+    --no-fetch) fetch=0; shift ;;
+    -h | --help) sed -n '17,29p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; exit 0 ;;
+    *) echo "merge-base.sh: unknown arg: $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+  esac
+done
+
+g() { git -C "$worktree" "$@"; }
+
+g rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
+  echo "merge-base.sh: not a git worktree: $worktree" >&2
+  exit 2
+}
+
+is_kueue_url() { [[ "$1" =~ github\.com[:/]kubernetes-sigs/kueue(\.git)?/?$ ]]; }
+
+remote="${PR_EVIDENCE_UPSTREAM_REMOTE:-}"
+if [[ -z "$remote" ]]; then
+  if is_kueue_url "$(g remote get-url upstream 2>/dev/null || true)"; then
+    remote=upstream
+  else
+    while read -r r; do
+      if is_kueue_url "$(g remote get-url "$r")"; then remote="$r"; break; fi
+    done < <(g remote)
+  fi
+fi
+if [[ -z "$remote" ]]; then
+  echo "merge-base.sh: no remote points at kubernetes-sigs/kueue; add one:" >&2
+  echo "  git remote add upstream https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue.git" >&2
+  exit 2
+fi
+
+if [[ "$fetch" == 1 ]]; then
+  g fetch --quiet "$remote" "$branch" >&2
+fi
+ref="refs/remotes/$remote/$branch"
+if ! g rev-parse --verify --quiet "$ref^{commit}" >/dev/null; then
+  ref=FETCH_HEAD
+fi
+
+merge_base="$(g merge-base HEAD "$ref")"
+head_sha="$(g rev-parse HEAD)"
+
+if [[ -n "$(g status --porcelain --untracked-files=no)" ]]; then
+  echo "merge-base.sh: warning: uncommitted changes are not part of HEAD_SHA" >&2
+fi
+
+printf 'UPSTREAM_REMOTE=%s\nUPSTREAM_REF=%s\nMERGE_BASE=%s\nHEAD_SHA=%s\n' \
+  "$remote" "$ref" "$merge_base" "$head_sha"
```

@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+---
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-common
+description: Shared scripts and conventions for the kueue-pr-evidence-* skills. Resolves the merge base and head SHAs, creates a throwaway worktree at the merge base, and writes one marked evidence block into the "Useful notes for your reviewer" section of the user's own PR, dry-run first. Use when an evidence skill publishes its block, or when a user asks to preview or refresh one.
+license: Apache-2.0
+metadata:
+  copyright: The Kubernetes Authors
+---
+
+# Skill: Publish PR reviewer evidence
+
+Library skill for the `kueue-pr-evidence-*` family
+([#18141](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/18141)). Each
+evidence skill generates its own content and calls the scripts here to resolve
+SHAs, build the base side, and publish the block. This skill adds no evidence
+itself.
+
+## When to use
+
+- An evidence skill has a block ready to publish.
+- A user asks to preview, refresh, or repair an evidence block in their PR
+  description.
+
+## Conventions
+
+1. **One block per skill.** Each skill owns one block under
+   `#### Useful notes for your reviewer`, between
+   `<!-- pr-evidence:<skill>:start -->` and `<!-- pr-evidence:<skill>:end -->`.
+   A re-run replaces the block in place and keeps all other text.
+2. **About 25 visible lines.** The injector rejects a block with more than 25
+   visible lines, header included. Lines inside `<details>` do not count; the
+   `<details>` line does. Start with a one-line verdict, then a small table.
+   Use bold text instead of `####` headings inside a block.
+3. **Generated base.** Build the "before" side from the merge base, never
+   from memory. The header line pins the base and head SHAs.
+4. **envtest by default.** Use kind only when the behavior needs real Pods
+   (PodsReady), DRA, TAS node-fit, or MultiKueue, and name the runner in the
+   block.
+5. **Own PRs, dry-run first.** Publish only to a PR the `gh` user opened.
+   Always run `--dry-run`, show the diff, and wait for the user's approval.
+
+## Steps
+
+`PR_WORKTREE` is a checkout of the PR head. Run the scripts from the kueue
+checkout that contains this skill; it can be the same tree.
+
+1. Resolve the SHAs and an output directory outside the repository:
+
+   ```bash
+   C="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)/cmd/experimental/skills/kueue-pr-evidence-common/scripts"
+   eval "$("$C/merge-base.sh" --worktree "$PR_WORKTREE")"  # UPSTREAM_REMOTE UPSTREAM_REF MERGE_BASE HEAD_SHA
```

+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+"""Field-level CRD schema delta (no OpenAPI dumps)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import re
+
+from . import miniyaml
+from .model import ABSENT, API, ACTION, BREAKING, TIGHTEN, Change, short
+
+ATTRS = [
+    "type", "format", "default", "enum", "minimum", "maximum", "exclusiveMinimum",
+    "exclusiveMaximum", "multipleOf", "minLength", "maxLength", "minItems", "maxItems",
+    "minProperties", "maxProperties", "uniqueItems", "pattern", "nullable",
+    "x-kubernetes-int-or-string", "x-kubernetes-preserve-unknown-fields",
+    "x-kubernetes-list-type", "x-kubernetes-list-map-keys", "x-kubernetes-map-type",
+    "x-kubernetes-embedded-resource",
+]
+# attr -> +1 when a larger value is stricter, -1 when a smaller one is.
+STRICTER = {"minimum": 1, "exclusiveMinimum": 1, "minLength": 1, "minItems": 1,
+            "minProperties": 1, "maximum": -1, "exclusiveMaximum": -1, "maxLength": -1,
+            "maxItems": -1, "maxProperties": -1}
+OMIT_RE = re.compile(
+    r"[^.\n]*\b(?:if (?:unspecified|not specified|not set|unset|omitted|empty|nil|absent)|"
+    r"when (?:unspecified|not specified|not set|unset|omitted|empty|absent)|"
+    r"defaults? to|by default)\b[^.\n]*\.?", re.IGNORECASE)
+
+
+def parse(text: str | None) -> dict | None:
+    """{'kind', 'versions': {name: {'served','storage','columns','fields'}}} or None."""
+    if not text:
+        return None
+    for doc in miniyaml.load_all(text):
+        if isinstance(doc, dict) and doc.get("kind") == "CustomResourceDefinition":
+            return _flatten_crd(doc)
+    return None
+
+
+def _flatten_crd(doc: dict) -> dict:
+    spec = doc.get("spec") or {}
+    out = {"kind": (spec.get("names") or {}).get("kind", "?"), "versions": {}}
+    for v in spec.get("versions") or []:
+        fields: dict = {}
+        schema = ((v.get("schema") or {}).get("openAPIV3Schema")) or {}
+        _walk(schema, "", False, fields)
+        cols = {c.get("name"): {k: c.get(k) for k in ("jsonPath", "type", "priority")}
+                for c in v.get("additionalPrinterColumns") or []}
+        out["versions"][v.get("name")] = {
+            "served": v.get("served"), "storage": v.get("storage"),
+            "columns": cols, "fields": fields,
+        }
+    return out
+
+
+def _walk(schema: dict, path: str, required: bool, out: dict) -> None:
+    if not isinstance(schema, dict):
+        return
+    if path:
+        attrs = {k: schema[k] for k in ATTRS if k in schema}
+        attrs["required"] = required
+        cel = schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations") or []
+        out[path] = {
+            "attrs": attrs,
+            "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message") or r.get("messageExpression") or "")
+                    for r in cel if isinstance(r, dict)],
+            "description": schema.get("description") or "",
+        }
+    elif schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations"):
+        out["(root)"] = {"attrs": {}, "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message", ""))
+                                               for r in schema["x-kubernetes-validations"]],
```

+"""Go-source contract surfaces: logs, events, validation/CLI messages, API
+constants, metrics, feature gates, kueuectl flags and columns."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from collections import Counter
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+from .gosrc import GoFile, literals_in, string_consts, string_value
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, API, TIGHTEN, Change, pair, short
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Rec:
+    path: str
+    line: int
+    func: str
+    kind: str
+    text: str
+    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+
+    @property
+    def where(self) -> str:
+        return f"{self.path}:{self.line}"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
+
+def is_go_source(path: str) -> bool:
+    return (path.endswith(".go") and not path.endswith("_test.go") and "zz_generated" not in path
+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)
+
+
+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("pkg/webhooks/") or "webhook" in path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs
+
+LOG_RE = re.compile(
+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")
+LOGGER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|\.)(log\w*|\w*logger\w*|l|lg|klog)$|LoggerFrom\(")
+
+
+def log_calls(gf: GoFile) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(LOG_RE):
+        recv = m.group(1)
+        last = re.sub(r"\(.*\)$", "", recv)
+        if not (LOGGER_NAME_RE.search(last) or "LoggerFrom(" in recv):
+            continue
+        method = m.group(4)
+        is_err = method.startswith("Error")
+        if is_err:
+            if len(args) < 2:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[1], args[2:]
+            level = "Error"
+        else:
+            if not args:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[0], args[1:]
+            if m.group(3) is not None:
+                level = f"V({m.group(3).strip()})"
+            else:
+                level = _alias_level(gf, last, m.start()) or "V(0)"
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr)
+        keys = []
+        for i in range(0, len(kv), 2):
+            k = kv[i]
+            if k.endswith("..."):
+                keys.append("…")
+                break
+            keys.append(string_value(k) or f"<{short(k, 20)}>")
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "log",
+                       msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 40)}>",
+                       {"level": level, "keys": tuple(keys)}))
+    return out
+
+
+_V_IN_RHS_RE = re.compile(r"\.V\(([^()]*)\)")
+
+
+def _alias_level(gf: GoFile, ident: str, pos: int) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve `logV := log.V(5)` style aliases assigned earlier in the same func.
+
+    Walks the ident's assignments backwards: an RHS with `.V(n)` gives the level,
+    a self-derived RHS (`logV = logV.WithValues(...)`) keeps looking, anything
+    else ends the search (plain logger, V(0)).
+    """
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*", ident):
+        return None
+    region = gf.src[gf.func_start(pos):pos]
+    assign = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])" + re.escape(ident) + r"\s*:?=\s*([^\n;]+)")
+    for m in reversed(list(assign.finditer(region))):
+        rhs = m.group(1)
+        if rhs.startswith("="):  # comparison, not assignment
+            continue
+        v = _V_IN_RHS_RE.search(rhs)
+        if v:
+            return f"V({v.group(1).strip()})"
+        if re.match(re.escape(ident) + r"\b", rhs.strip()):
+            continue
+        return None
+    return None
+
+
+def diff_logs(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["level"], r.attrs["keys"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.6)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.8)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        bk, hk = set(b.attrs["keys"]), set(h.attrs["keys"])
+        gone, new = sorted(bk - hk), sorted(hk - bk)
+        notes = []
+        if gone and new:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"log key renamed ({', '.join(gone)} → {', '.join(new)})"
+            notes.append("key renamed")
+        elif gone:
+            notes.append("key removed")
+        elif new:
+            notes.append("key added")
+        if b.attrs["level"] != h.attrs["level"]:
+            notes.insert(0, f"{b.attrs['level']}→{h.attrs['level']}")
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            notes.append("reworded")
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), _log_cell(b), _log_cell(h), h.where, "changed", fl,
+                          "; ".join(notes), group=h.path,
+                          meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(b.text, 50), _log_cell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, "removed",
+                          group=b.path, meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": ABSENT}))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), ABSENT, _log_cell(h), h.where, "added", {}, "added",
+                          group=h.path, meta={"level_before": ABSENT, "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _log_cell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    keys = ", ".join(r.attrs["keys"])
+    return f'{r.attrs["level"]} "{r.text}"' + (f" [{keys}]" if keys else "")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- events
+
+EVENT_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Eventf|Event|AnnotatedEventf)\(")
+EVENT_TYPE_RE = re.compile(r'^(?:[\w.]+\.)?EventType(Normal|Warning)$|^"(Normal|Warning)"$')
+
+
+def events(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(EVENT_RE):
+        t = next((i for i, a in enumerate(args) if EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(a)), None)
+        if t is None or t + 1 >= len(args):
+            continue
+        tm = EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(args[t])
+        etype = tm.group(1) or tm.group(2)
+        reason_expr = args[t + 1]
+        reason = string_value(reason_expr, env) or reason_expr
+        new_api = t == 2 and len(args) >= t + 4
+        msg_expr = args[t + 3] if new_api else (args[t + 2] if len(args) > t + 2 else "")
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr, env)
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "event", reason,
+                       {"type": etype, "msg": msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 30)}>"}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_events(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["type"], r.attrs["msg"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.attrs["msg"], 0.6)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"event reason {b.text} → {h.text}"
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", _ev(b), _ev(h), h.where, "changed", fl, group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {b.text}", _ev(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", ABSENT, _ev(h), h.where, "added", {}, group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+def _ev(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f'{r.attrs["type"]} {r.text}: "{r.attrs["msg"]}"'
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- messages
+
+FIELD_RE = re.compile(r"\bfield\.(Invalid|Forbidden|Required|NotSupported|Duplicate|TooMany|TooLong|"
+                      r"TooLongMaxLength|NotFound)\(")
+ERR_RE = re.compile(r"\b(errors\.New|fmt\.Errorf)\(")
+VERB_RE = re.compile(r"%(?:%|[-+# 0]*(?:\*|\d+)?(?:\.(?:\*|\d+))?[a-zA-Z])")
+FIELD_MSG_ARG = {"Invalid": 2, "Forbidden": 1, "Required": 1, "NotSupported": 2}
+
+
+def messages(gf: GoFile, sentinels: dict[str, str], *, field_calls: bool, errors_too: bool) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    consumed: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()
+    if field_calls:
+        for m, args, _ in gf.calls(FIELD_RE):
+            fn = m.group(1)
+            k = FIELD_MSG_ARG.get(fn)
+            if k is None or len(args) <= k:
+                continue
+            if fn == "NotSupported":
+                vals = literals_in(args[k])
+                if not vals:
+                    continue
+                text = "supported: " + ", ".join(vals)
+            else:
+                text = string_value(args[k])
+                if text is None:
+                    continue
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), f"field.{fn}", text))
+    if errors_too:
+        for m, args, end in gf.calls(ERR_RE):
+            func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+            if not func or not args:
+                continue  # package-level sentinels are reported via their uses
+            text = string_value(args[0])
+            if text is None:
+                continue
+            if m.group(1) == "fmt.Errorf" and "%w" in text:
+                verbs = [v for v in VERB_RE.findall(text) if v != "%%"]
+                for idx, v in enumerate(verbs):
+                    if v == "%w" and 1 + idx < len(args) and args[1 + idx] in sentinels:
+                        text = text.replace("%w", sentinels[args[1 + idx]], 1)
+                        consumed.add((gf.line(m.start()), args[1 + idx]))
+                        break
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), func, m.group(1), text))
+        if sentinels:
+            names = "|".join(map(re.escape, sorted(sentinels, key=len, reverse=True)))
+            for m in re.finditer(rf"^[ \t]*return\b[^\n]*\b({names})\b", gf.src, re.M):
+                func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+                if not func:
+                    continue
+                ln = gf.line(m.start())
+                if (ln, m.group(1)) in consumed:
+                    continue
+                out.append(Rec(gf.path, ln, func, "sentinel", sentinels[m.group(1)], {"var": m.group(1)}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_messages(surface: str, base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec],
+                  base_files: dict, head_files: dict) -> list[Change]:
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.45)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.75)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+
+    def logic_note(func: str, bpath: str | None, hpath: str | None) -> str:
+        bf = base_files.get(bpath) if bpath else None
+        hf = head_files.get(hpath) if hpath else None
+        if not func:
+            return ""
+        bs = bf.logic_signature(func) if bf else None
+        hs = hf.logic_signature(func) if hf else None
+        if bs is None and hs is not None:
+            return f"new func `{func}`"
+        if bs is not None and hs is not None and bs != hs:
+            return f"logic in `{func}` also changed"
+        return ""
+
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if b.text == h.text:
+            continue
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", b.text, h.text, h.where, "changed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {},
+                          logic_note(h.func, b.path, h.path), group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{b.kind} in `{b.func or '?'}`", b.text, ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {}, logic_note(b.func, b.path, None),
+                          group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        fl = {API: 1, TIGHTEN: f"new validation in `{h.func}`"} if surface == "Webhook" else {}
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", ABSENT, h.text, h.where, "added", fl,
+                          logic_note(h.func, None, h.path), group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- constants
+
+CONTRACT_CONST_RE = re.compile(r"(Reason|Label|Annotation|Condition|Finalizer)")
+
+
+def contract_consts(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    """Exported string constants that are part of the user contract."""
+    api = gf.path.startswith("apis/")
+    events_file = gf.path.endswith("/events.go")
+    out = {}
+    for name, value, line in string_consts(gf, env):
+        if not name[:1].isupper():
+            continue
+        if not (api or events_file or CONTRACT_CONST_RE.search(name)):
+            continue
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, line, "", _const_kind(name, value), value)
+    return out
+
+
+def _const_kind(name: str, value: str) -> str:
+    if re.search(r"Label|Annotation|Finalizer", name) or ("/" in value and "." in value.split("/")[0]):
+        return "key"
+    if "Reason" in name:
+        return "reason"
+    if "Condition" in name:
+        return "condition"
+    return "const"
+
+
+def diff_consts(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    gone = {n: r for n, r in base.items() if n not in head}
+    new = {n: r for n, r in head.items() if n not in base}
+    by_val_new = {}
+    for n, r in new.items():
+        by_val_new.setdefault(r.text, []).append(n)
+    for n, r in sorted(gone.items()):
+        if by_val_new.get(r.text):
+            n2 = by_val_new[r.text].pop(0)
+            new.pop(n2)
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}` → `{n2}`", f'{n} = "{r.text}"', f'{n2} = "{r.text}"',
+                              head[n2].where, "changed", {API: 1} if r.path.startswith("apis/") else {},
+                              "Go identifier renamed; value unchanged"))
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if r.kind == "key":
+            fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(r.kind)} `{r.text}` removed"
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{r.text}"', ABSENT, r.where, "removed", fl,
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n, r in sorted(new.items()):
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, f'"{r.text}"', r.where, "added", {},
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n in sorted(set(base) & set(head)):
+        b, h = base[n], head[n]
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl = {}
+            if h.kind in ("key", "reason", "condition"):
+                fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(h.kind)} `{b.text}` renamed to `{h.text}`"
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{b.text}"', f'"{h.text}"', h.where, "changed", fl,
+                              _kind_word(h.kind)))
+    return out
+
+
+def _kind_word(k: str) -> str:
+    return {"key": "label/annotation key", "reason": "reason", "condition": "condition type"}.get(k, "constant")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- metrics
+
+METRIC_RE = re.compile(r"\bprometheus\.New(Counter|Gauge|Histogram|Summary)(Vec)?\(")
+
+
+def metrics(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    out = {}
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(METRIC_RE):
+        if not args:
+            continue
+        opts = _composite(args[0])
+        name = string_value(opts.get("Name", ""), env)
+        if not name:
+            continue
+        ns = string_value(opts.get("Namespace", '""'), env) or ""
+        sub = string_value(opts.get("Subsystem", '""'), env) or ""
+        full = "_".join(x for x in (ns, sub, name) if x)
+        labels: list[str] = []
+        dynamic = False
+        if m.group(2) and len(args) > 1:
+            labels = literals_in(args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r'"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`', "", args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r"\[\]string|[{},\s]", "", rest)
+            dynamic = bool(rest)
+        help_text = " ".join((string_value(opts.get("Help", '""'), env) or "").split())
+        out[full] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), m.group(1).lower(), full,
+                        {"labels": labels, "dynamic": dynamic, "help": help_text,
+                         "buckets": " ".join(opts.get("Buckets", "").split())})
+    return out
+
+
+def _composite(expr: str) -> dict[str, str]:
+    from .gosrc import composite_fields
+    return composite_fields(expr)
+
+
+def diff_metrics(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", _mcell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` removed"}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, _mcell(h), h.where, "added", {}))
+            continue
+        if b.kind != h.kind:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` type", b.kind, h.kind, h.where, "changed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` type changed"}))
+        bl, hl = b.attrs["labels"], h.attrs["labels"]
+        if bl != hl or b.attrs["dynamic"] != h.attrs["dynamic"]:
+            gone = [x for x in bl if x not in hl]
+            fl = {}
+            if gone:
+                new = [x for x in hl if x not in bl]
+                what = "renamed" if new else "removed"
+                fl[ACTION] = f"metric `{n}` label {what} ({', '.join(gone)}" + (
+                    f" → {', '.join(new)})" if new else ")")
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` labels", _labels(b), _labels(h), h.where, "changed", fl))
+        if b.attrs["help"] != h.attrs["help"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` help", b.attrs["help"], h.attrs["help"], h.where, "changed", {}))
+        if b.attrs["buckets"] != h.attrs["buckets"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` buckets", b.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT,
+                              h.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT, h.where, "changed", {}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _labels(r: Rec) -> str:
+    s = ", ".join(r.attrs["labels"]) or "(none)"
+    return s + (" + dynamic" if r.attrs["dynamic"] else "")
+
+
+def _mcell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f"{r.kind} [{_labels(r)}]"
+
+
+DOC_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\|\s*`([a-z0-9_:]+)`\s*\|\s*([^|]*)\|(.*)\|\s*(.*?)\s*\|\s*$", re.M)
+
+
+def metrics_doc(text: str | None) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
+    out = {}
+    for m in DOC_ROW_RE.finditer(text or ""):
+        labels = re.findall(r"(?:^|<br>)\s*`([a-z0-9_]+)`", m.group(4))
+        out[m.group(1)] = labels
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- feature gates
+
+SPEC_RE = re.compile(r'\{\s*Version:\s*version\.MustParse\("([^"]+)"\)\s*,\s*Default:\s*(true|false)\s*,'
+                     r'\s*PreRelease:\s*(?:featuregate\.)?(\w+)(?:\s*,\s*LockToDefault:\s*(true|false))?\s*\}')
+
+
+def feature_gates(gf: GoFile) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    m = re.search(r"defaultVersionedFeatureGates\s*=\s*map\[[^\]]+\][\w.]+\{", gf.src)
+    if not m:
+        return {}
+    items, _ = _group_from(gf.src, m.end() - 1)
+    out = {}
+    for it in items:
+        km = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_][\w.]*)\s*:\s*(\{.*\})\s*\Z", it, re.S)
+        if not km:
+            continue
+        specs = [(v, d == "true", pre, lock == "true") for v, d, pre, lock in SPEC_RE.findall(km.group(2))]
+        if not specs:
+            continue
+        name = km.group(1).rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
+        pos = m.end() + gf.src[m.end():].find(km.group(1) + ":")
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(pos), "", "gate", name, {"specs": specs})
+    return out
+
+
+def _group_from(src: str, i: int):
+    from .gosrc import group_args
+    return group_args(src, i)
+
+
+def gate_cell(specs) -> str:
+    v, d, pre, lock = specs[-1]
+    return f"{pre} {'on' if d else 'off'} @v{v}" + (" (locked)" if lock else "")
+
+
+def diff_gates(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", gate_cell(b.attrs["specs"]), "removed", b.where,
+                              "removed", {API: 1, ACTION: f"feature gate `{n}` removed"} if not
+                              b.attrs["specs"][-1][3] else {API: 1}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, gate_cell(h.attrs["specs"]), h.where,
+                              "added", {API: 1}, meta={"gate": n, "specs": h.attrs["specs"]}))
+            continue
+        bs, hs = b.attrs["specs"][-1], h.attrs["specs"][-1]
+        if (bs[1], bs[2], bs[3]) == (hs[1], hs[2], hs[3]) and b.attrs["specs"] == h.attrs["specs"]:
+            continue
+        if (bs[1], bs[2], bs[3]) == (hs[1], hs[2], hs[3]):
+            continue  # only historical entries changed
+        fl = {API: 1}
+        if bs[1] != hs[1]:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"feature gate `{n}` default {'on' if hs[1] else 'off'}"
+        out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", gate_cell(b.attrs["specs"]), gate_cell(h.attrs["specs"]),
+                          h.where, "changed", fl, meta={"gate": n, "specs": h.attrs["specs"],
+                                                        "default_before": bs[1], "default_after": hs[1],
+                                                        "locked_now": bool(hs[3] and not bs[3])}))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- kueuectl
+
+FLAG_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Flags|PersistentFlags)\(\)\.([A-Z]\w*)\(")
```

  **Detect command-level required flags.**
  
  If a PR adds `cmd.MarkFlagRequired("name")` without changing the flag declaration, the tool can report no contract change even though omitting the flag now fails. Capture command-level required marks and associate them with the registered flags.

+---
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta
+description: Lists the user-facing contract changes in a Kueue PR as a before/after table built from the merge base and the head, with no cluster and no checkout. Covers CRD fields, defaults and validation, CEL and webhook messages, condition and event reasons, metrics, feature gates, log verbosity and keys, and kueuectl flags, columns and errors. Adds a compatibility check for API changes and flags a likely ACTION REQUIRED release note. Use when a reviewer asks what a PR changes for users, or before marking an API or UX change ready for review.
+license: Apache-2.0

@@ -0,0 +1,95 @@
+---
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta
+description: Lists the user-facing contract changes in a Kueue PR as a before/after table built from the merge base and the head, with no cluster and no checkout. Covers CRD fields, defaults and validation, CEL and webhook messages, condition and event reasons, metrics, feature gates, log verbosity and keys, and kueuectl flags, columns and errors. Adds a compatibility check for API changes and flags a likely ACTION REQUIRED release note. Use when a reviewer asks what a PR changes for users, or before marking an API or UX change ready for review.
+license: Apache-2.0
+metadata:
+  copyright: The Kubernetes Authors
+---
+
+# Skill: List user-facing contract changes
+
+Member of the `kueue-pr-evidence-*` family
+([#18141](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/18141)). It reads
+both sides of every changed file with `git show <sha>:<path>` and publishes
+one block through
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+- The PR touches `apis/`, `config/components/crd/`, `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  `pkg/webhooks/`, `pkg/metrics/`, `pkg/features/`, `cmd/kueuectl/`, or log calls.
+- A reviewer asks "what changes for users?" or "does this need ACTION REQUIRED?"
+- For runtime behavior, use `kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff`. For the release
+  note text, use [kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md).
+
+## Steps
+
+`PR_WORKTREE` is a checkout of the PR head.
+
+1. Resolve the SHAs and an output directory:
+
+   ```bash
+   S="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)/cmd/experimental/skills"
+   C="$S/kueue-pr-evidence-common/scripts"
+   eval "$("$C/merge-base.sh" --worktree "$PR_WORKTREE")"  # MERGE_BASE HEAD_SHA
+   OUT="${XDG_CACHE_HOME:-$HOME/.cache}/kueue-pr-evidence/pr-<number>"
+   mkdir -p "$OUT"
+   ```
+
+2. Generate the block. The script only reads git objects, so it needs no base
+   worktree:
+
+   ```bash
+   "$S/kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta/scripts/contract-delta" --repo "$PR_WORKTREE" \
+     --base "$MERGE_BASE" --head "$HEAD_SHA" --out "$OUT/kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta.md"
+   ```
+
+   `--format json` prints every change with its flags. For a merged PR, pass
+   `--base <merge>^1 --head <merge>`.
```

+"""Collect contract changes between two revisions of a kueue tree (read-only git)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import posixpath
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+from . import crd, goextract as gx
+from .gosrc import GoFile, sentinel_errors, string_consts
+from .model import ACTION, API, BREAKING, SURFACE_ORDER, TIGHTEN, Change, short
+
+CRD_PREFIX = "config/components/crd/"
+CHART_CRD_PREFIX = "charts/kueue/templates/crd/"
+METRICS_DOC = "site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md"
+FEATURES_FILE = "pkg/features/kube_features.go"
+CONSTANTS_FILE = "pkg/constants/constants.go"
+
+SURFACES_CHECKED = ("CRD schema/defaults/validation", "CEL", "webhook messages",
+                    "condition/event reasons + API constants", "metrics (+ docs)", "feature gates",
+                    "log V-levels/keys", "kueuectl flags/columns/errors")
+
+
+class GitError(RuntimeError):
+    pass
+
+
+class Rev:
+    """Read-only view of one commit (git cat-file; never touches the worktree)."""
+
+    def __init__(self, repo: str, rev: str):
+        self.repo = repo
+        self.sha = _git(repo, "rev-parse", "--verify", f"{rev}^{{commit}}").strip()
+        self._blobs: dict = {}
+        self._pkg: dict = {}
+
+    def read(self, path: str | None) -> str | None:
+        if not path:
+            return None
+        if path not in self._blobs:
+            try:
+                self._blobs[path] = _git(self.repo, "show", f"{self.sha}:{path}")
+            except GitError:
+                self._blobs[path] = None
+        return self._blobs[path]
+
+    def gofile(self, path: str | None) -> GoFile | None:
+        src = self.read(path)
+        return GoFile(path, src) if src is not None else None
+
+    def package(self, d: str) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, str]]:
+        """(string consts, sentinel errors) across non-test .go files in dir d."""
+        if d not in self._pkg:
+            consts: dict[str, str] = {}
+            sentinels: dict[str, str] = {}
+            try:
+                names = _git(self.repo, "ls-tree", "--name-only", f"{self.sha}:{d}" if d else self.sha).split()
+            except GitError:
+                names = []
+            files = [GoFile(posixpath.join(d, n), self.read(posixpath.join(d, n)) or "")
+                     for n in names if n.endswith(".go") and not n.endswith("_test.go")]
+            for _ in range(2):  # two passes resolve cross-file `A + "-x"` constants
+                for gf in files:
+                    consts.update({n: v for n, v, _ in string_consts(gf, consts)})
```

+        if path not in self._blobs:
+            try:
+                self._blobs[path] = _git(self.repo, "show", f"{self.sha}:{path}")
+            except GitError:
+                self._blobs[path] = None
+        return self._blobs[path]
+
+    def gofile(self, path: str | None) -> GoFile | None:
+        src = self.read(path)
+        return GoFile(path, src) if src is not None else None
+
+    def package(self, d: str) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, str]]:
+        """(string consts, sentinel errors) across non-test .go files in dir d."""
+        if d not in self._pkg:
+            consts: dict[str, str] = {}
+            sentinels: dict[str, str] = {}
+            try:
+                names = _git(self.repo, "ls-tree", "--name-only", f"{self.sha}:{d}" if d else self.sha).split()
+            except GitError:
+                names = []
+            files = [GoFile(posixpath.join(d, n), self.read(posixpath.join(d, n)) or "")
+                     for n in names if n.endswith(".go") and not n.endswith("_test.go")]
+            for _ in range(2):  # two passes resolve cross-file `A + "-x"` constants
+                for gf in files:
+                    consts.update({n: v for n, v, _ in string_consts(gf, consts)})
+            for gf in files:
+                sentinels.update(sentinel_errors(gf))
+            self._pkg[d] = (consts, sentinels)
+        return self._pkg[d]
+
+
+def _git(repo: str, *args: str) -> str:
+    try:
+        res = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, *args], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
+        raise GitError(f"git {' '.join(args[:2])}: {exc.stderr.strip()}") from exc
+    return res.stdout
+
+
+def changed_files(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> list[tuple[str | None, str | None]]:
+    """(base_path, head_path) per changed file of `git diff base...head` (renames kept)."""
+    out = _git(repo, "diff", "--name-status", "-z", "-M", "--no-ext-diff", f"{base}...{head}")
+    toks = out.split("\0")
+    res = []
+    i = 0
+    while i < len(toks) and toks[i]:
+        st = toks[i]
+        if st[0] in "RC":
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], toks[i + 2]))
+            i += 3
+        elif st[0] == "A":
+            res.append((None, toks[i + 1]))
+            i += 2
+        elif st[0] == "D":
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], None))
+            i += 2
+        else:
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], toks[i + 1]))
+            i += 2
+    return res
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Report:
+    base: str
+    head: str
+    changes: list[Change] = field(default_factory=list)
+    compat: list[tuple[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)
+    actions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    files: int = 0
+
+    def to_json(self) -> dict:
+        return {"base": self.base, "head": self.head, "files_changed": self.files,
+                "surfaces_checked": list(SURFACES_CHECKED),
+                "changes": [c.to_json() for c in self.changes],
+                "compat": [{"check": a, "result": b} for a, b in self.compat],
+                "action_required": self.actions, "notes": self.notes}
+
+
+def collect(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> Report:
+    b, h = Rev(repo, base), Rev(repo, head)
+    files = changed_files(repo, b.sha, h.sha)
+    rep = Report(b.sha, h.sha, files=len(files))
+    changes: list[Change] = []
+    head_gates = set(gx.feature_gates(h.gofile(FEATURES_FILE)).keys()) if h.read(FEATURES_FILE) else set()
+
+    log_b, log_h, wh_b, wh_h, cli_b, cli_h = [], [], [], [], [], []
+    gofiles_b: dict = {}
+    gofiles_h: dict = {}
+    metrics_touched = False
+    chart_touched = set()
+    crd_touched = set()
+
+    for bp, hp in files:
+        path = hp or bp
```

  **Check rename eligibility on each side.**
  
  When Git renames a production Go file into an excluded test directory, the head path causes the collector to skip both files. Removed logs and other contracts can disappear from the report. Evaluate each side independently so the eligible base file contributes removals.

+"""Field-level CRD schema delta (no OpenAPI dumps)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import re
+
+from . import miniyaml
+from .model import ABSENT, API, ACTION, BREAKING, TIGHTEN, Change, short
+
+ATTRS = [
+    "type", "format", "default", "enum", "minimum", "maximum", "exclusiveMinimum",
+    "exclusiveMaximum", "multipleOf", "minLength", "maxLength", "minItems", "maxItems",
+    "minProperties", "maxProperties", "uniqueItems", "pattern", "nullable",
+    "x-kubernetes-int-or-string", "x-kubernetes-preserve-unknown-fields",
+    "x-kubernetes-list-type", "x-kubernetes-list-map-keys", "x-kubernetes-map-type",
+    "x-kubernetes-embedded-resource",
+]
+# attr -> +1 when a larger value is stricter, -1 when a smaller one is.
+STRICTER = {"minimum": 1, "exclusiveMinimum": 1, "minLength": 1, "minItems": 1,
+            "minProperties": 1, "maximum": -1, "exclusiveMaximum": -1, "maxLength": -1,
+            "maxItems": -1, "maxProperties": -1}
+OMIT_RE = re.compile(
+    r"[^.\n]*\b(?:if (?:unspecified|not specified|not set|unset|omitted|empty|nil|absent)|"
+    r"when (?:unspecified|not specified|not set|unset|omitted|empty|absent)|"
+    r"defaults? to|by default)\b[^.\n]*\.?", re.IGNORECASE)
+
+
+def parse(text: str | None) -> dict | None:
+    """{'kind', 'versions': {name: {'served','storage','columns','fields'}}} or None."""
+    if not text:
+        return None
+    for doc in miniyaml.load_all(text):
+        if isinstance(doc, dict) and doc.get("kind") == "CustomResourceDefinition":
+            return _flatten_crd(doc)
+    return None
+
+
+def _flatten_crd(doc: dict) -> dict:
+    spec = doc.get("spec") or {}
+    out = {"kind": (spec.get("names") or {}).get("kind", "?"), "versions": {}}
+    for v in spec.get("versions") or []:
+        fields: dict = {}
+        schema = ((v.get("schema") or {}).get("openAPIV3Schema")) or {}
+        _walk(schema, "", False, fields)
+        cols = {c.get("name"): {k: c.get(k) for k in ("jsonPath", "type", "priority")}
+                for c in v.get("additionalPrinterColumns") or []}
+        out["versions"][v.get("name")] = {
+            "served": v.get("served"), "storage": v.get("storage"),
+            "columns": cols, "fields": fields,
+        }
+    return out
+
+
+def _walk(schema: dict, path: str, required: bool, out: dict) -> None:
+    if not isinstance(schema, dict):
+        return
+    if path:
+        attrs = {k: schema[k] for k in ATTRS if k in schema}
+        attrs["required"] = required
+        cel = schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations") or []
+        out[path] = {
+            "attrs": attrs,
+            "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message") or r.get("messageExpression") or "")
+                    for r in cel if isinstance(r, dict)],
+            "description": schema.get("description") or "",
+        }
+    elif schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations"):
+        out["(root)"] = {"attrs": {}, "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message", ""))
+                                               for r in schema["x-kubernetes-validations"]],
+                         "description": ""}
```

+    """{'kind', 'versions': {name: {'served','storage','columns','fields'}}} or None."""
+    if not text:
+        return None
+    for doc in miniyaml.load_all(text):

+    return None

+def _flatten_crd(doc: dict) -> dict:
+    spec = doc.get("spec") or {}
+    out = {"kind": (spec.get("names") or {}).get("kind", "?"), "versions": {}}
+    for v in spec.get("versions") or []:
+        fields: dict = {}
+        schema = ((v.get("schema") or {}).get("openAPIV3Schema")) or {}
+        _walk(schema, "", False, fields)
+        cols = {c.get("name"): {k: c.get(k) for k in ("jsonPath", "type", "priority")}
+                for c in v.get("additionalPrinterColumns") or []}
+        out["versions"][v.get("name")] = {
+            "served": v.get("served"), "storage": v.get("storage"),
+            "columns": cols, "fields": fields,
+        }
+    return out
+
+
+def _walk(schema: dict, path: str, required: bool, out: dict) -> None:
+    if not isinstance(schema, dict):
+        return
+    if path:
+        attrs = {k: schema[k] for k in ATTRS if k in schema}
+        attrs["required"] = required
+        cel = schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations") or []
+        out[path] = {
+            "attrs": attrs,
+            "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message") or r.get("messageExpression") or "")
+                    for r in cel if isinstance(r, dict)],
+            "description": schema.get("description") or "",
+        }
+    elif schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations"):
+        out["(root)"] = {"attrs": {}, "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message", ""))
+                                               for r in schema["x-kubernetes-validations"]],
+                         "description": ""}
+    req = set(schema.get("required") or [])
+    for name, sub in (schema.get("properties") or {}).items():
+        _walk(sub, f"{path}.{name}" if path else name, name in req, out)
+    if isinstance(schema.get("items"), dict):
+        _walk(schema["items"], f"{path}[]", False, out)
+    if isinstance(schema.get("additionalProperties"), dict):
+        _walk(schema["additionalProperties"], f"{path}.*", False, out)

  **Report changes inside CRD validation combinators**
  
  When an existing object gains an `allOf` requirement for an optional property, the tool omits the validation change. It can report no change or affirm manifest compatibility even though manifests omitting that property are rejected. Record combinator changes and classify their compatibility conservatively.

+"""Field-level CRD schema delta (no OpenAPI dumps)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import re
+
+from . import miniyaml
+from .model import ABSENT, API, ACTION, BREAKING, TIGHTEN, Change, short
+
+ATTRS = [
+    "type", "format", "default", "enum", "minimum", "maximum", "exclusiveMinimum",
+    "exclusiveMaximum", "multipleOf", "minLength", "maxLength", "minItems", "maxItems",
+    "minProperties", "maxProperties", "uniqueItems", "pattern", "nullable",
+    "x-kubernetes-int-or-string", "x-kubernetes-preserve-unknown-fields",
+    "x-kubernetes-list-type", "x-kubernetes-list-map-keys", "x-kubernetes-map-type",
+    "x-kubernetes-embedded-resource",
+]
+# attr -> +1 when a larger value is stricter, -1 when a smaller one is.
+STRICTER = {"minimum": 1, "exclusiveMinimum": 1, "minLength": 1, "minItems": 1,
+            "minProperties": 1, "maximum": -1, "exclusiveMaximum": -1, "maxLength": -1,
+            "maxItems": -1, "maxProperties": -1}
+OMIT_RE = re.compile(
+    r"[^.\n]*\b(?:if (?:unspecified|not specified|not set|unset|omitted|empty|nil|absent)|"
+    r"when (?:unspecified|not specified|not set|unset|omitted|empty|absent)|"
+    r"defaults? to|by default)\b[^.\n]*\.?", re.IGNORECASE)
+
+
+def parse(text: str | None) -> dict | None:
+    """{'kind', 'versions': {name: {'served','storage','columns','fields'}}} or None."""
+    if not text:
+        return None
+    for doc in miniyaml.load_all(text):
+        if isinstance(doc, dict) and doc.get("kind") == "CustomResourceDefinition":
+            return _flatten_crd(doc)
+    return None
+
+
+def _flatten_crd(doc: dict) -> dict:
+    spec = doc.get("spec") or {}
+    out = {"kind": (spec.get("names") or {}).get("kind", "?"), "versions": {}}
+    for v in spec.get("versions") or []:
+        fields: dict = {}
+        schema = ((v.get("schema") or {}).get("openAPIV3Schema")) or {}
+        _walk(schema, "", False, fields)
+        cols = {c.get("name"): {k: c.get(k) for k in ("jsonPath", "type", "priority")}
+                for c in v.get("additionalPrinterColumns") or []}
+        out["versions"][v.get("name")] = {
+            "served": v.get("served"), "storage": v.get("storage"),
+            "columns": cols, "fields": fields,
+        }
+    return out
+
+
+def _walk(schema: dict, path: str, required: bool, out: dict) -> None:
+    if not isinstance(schema, dict):
+        return
+    if path:
+        attrs = {k: schema[k] for k in ATTRS if k in schema}
+        attrs["required"] = required
+        cel = schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations") or []
+        out[path] = {
+            "attrs": attrs,
+            "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message") or r.get("messageExpression") or "")
+                    for r in cel if isinstance(r, dict)],
+            "description": schema.get("description") or "",
+        }
+    elif schema.get("x-kubernetes-validations"):
+        out["(root)"] = {"attrs": {}, "cel": [(r.get("rule", ""), r.get("message", ""))
+                                               for r in schema["x-kubernetes-validations"]],
+                         "description": ""}
+    req = set(schema.get("required") or [])
+    for name, sub in (schema.get("properties") or {}).items():
+        _walk(sub, f"{path}.{name}" if path else name, name in req, out)
+    if isinstance(schema.get("items"), dict):
+        _walk(schema["items"], f"{path}[]", False, out)
+    if isinstance(schema.get("additionalProperties"), dict):
+        _walk(schema["additionalProperties"], f"{path}.*", False, out)
+
+
+def _fmt(v) -> str:
+    if v is None:
+        return ABSENT
+    if isinstance(v, (dict, list)):
+        return json.dumps(v, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True)
+    if isinstance(v, bool):
+        return "true" if v else "false"
+    return str(v)
+
+
+def describe(f: dict) -> str:
+    """Short field summary, e.g. `integer/int64, required, min 1`."""
+    a = f["attrs"]
+    t = a.get("type", "any")
+    if a.get("format"):
+        t += "/" + a["format"]
+    bits = [t, "required" if a.get("required") else "optional"]
+    if "default" in a:
+        bits.append(f"default {_fmt(a['default'])}")
+    if "enum" in a:
+        bits.append("enum " + "|".join(_fmt(x) for x in a["enum"]))
+    for k, lbl in (("minimum", "min"), ("maximum", "max"), ("minLength", "minLen"),
+                   ("maxLength", "maxLen"), ("minItems", "minItems"), ("maxItems", "maxItems")):
+        if k in a:
+            bits.append(f"{lbl} {_fmt(a[k])}")
+    if f["cel"]:
+        bits.append(f"{len(f['cel'])} CEL")
+    return ", ".join(bits)
+
+
+def _is_status(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path == "status" or path.startswith("status.") or path.startswith("status[")
+
+
+def _children(path: str, fields: dict) -> list[str]:
+    return [p for p in fields if p != path and (p.startswith(path + ".") or p.startswith(path + "["))]
+
+
+def gate_mentions(text: str, gates: set[str]) -> list[str]:
+    return sorted({g for g in gates if re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(g)}\b", text or "")})
+
+
+def omit_sentence(text: str) -> str:
+    m = OMIT_RE.search(text or "")
+    return " ".join(m.group(0).split()) if m else ""
+
+
+def diff(path: str, base: dict | None, head: dict | None, gates: set[str]) -> list[Change]:
+    if base is None and head is None:
+        return []
+    kind = (head or base)["kind"]
+    changes: list[Change] = []
+    bv = (base or {}).get("versions", {})
+    hv = (head or {}).get("versions", {})
+    for v in sorted(set(bv) | set(hv)):
+        if v not in hv:
+            changes.append(Change("CRD", f"{kind} version {v}", "served" if bv[v]["served"] else "not served",
+                                  ABSENT, path, "removed", {BREAKING: "CRD version removed", API: 1,
+                                                            ACTION: "CRD version removed"}))
+            continue
+        if v not in bv:
+            changes.append(Change("CRD", f"{kind} version {v}", ABSENT,
+                                  "served" + (", storage" if hv[v]["storage"] else ""), path, "added", {API: 1}))
+            continue
+        b, h = bv[v], hv[v]
+        for k in ("served", "storage"):
+            if b[k] != h[k]:
+                fl = {API: 1}
+                if k == "served" and not h[k]:
+                    fl.update({BREAKING: f"{v} no longer served", ACTION: f"{kind} {v} no longer served"})
+                changes.append(Change("CRD", f"{kind} {v} {k}", _fmt(b[k]), _fmt(h[k]), path, "changed", fl))
+        changes += _diff_columns(kind, v, path, b["columns"], h["columns"])
+        changes += _diff_fields(kind, v, path, b["fields"], h["fields"], gates)
+    return _merge_versions(changes)
+
+
+def _diff_columns(kind, v, path, b, h) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for name in sorted(set(b) | set(h)):
+        if b.get(name) == h.get(name):
+            continue
+        before = b[name]["jsonPath"] if name in b else ABSENT
+        after = h[name]["jsonPath"] if name in h else ABSENT
+        if name in b and name in h and before == after:
+            before, after = _fmt(b[name]), _fmt(h[name])
+        out.append(Change("CRD columns", f"{kind} column `{name}`", before, after, path,
+                          "added" if name not in b else "removed" if name not in h else "changed",
+                          meta={"version": v}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _diff_fields(kind, v, path, b: dict, h: dict, gates: set[str]) -> list[Change]:
+    out: list[Change] = []
+    removed = [p for p in b if p not in h]
+    added = [p for p in h if p not in b]
+    top_removed = [p for p in removed if not any(p != q and (p.startswith(q + ".") or p.startswith(q + "["))
+                                                  for q in removed)]
+    top_added = [p for p in added if not any(p != q and (p.startswith(q + ".") or p.startswith(q + "["))
+                                              for q in added)]
+    for p in top_removed:
+        n = len(_children(p, b))
+        fl = {API: 1}
+        if not _is_status(p):
+            fl[BREAKING] = f"field `{p}` removed"
+        fl[ACTION] = f"{kind} field `{p}` removed"
+        out.append(Change("CRD", f"{kind} `{p}`", describe(b[p]) + (f" (+{n} subfields)" if n else ""),
+                          ABSENT, path, "removed", fl, meta={"version": v}))
+    for p in top_added:
+        f = h[p]
+        kids = _children(p, h)
+        parent = p.rsplit(".", 1)[0] if "." in p else ""
+        fl = {API: 1}

+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
