+"""Go-source contract surfaces: logs, events, validation/CLI messages, API
+constants, metrics, feature gates, kueuectl flags and columns."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from collections import Counter
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+from .gosrc import GoFile, literals_in, string_consts, string_value
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, API, TIGHTEN, Change, pair, short
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Rec:
+    path: str
+    line: int
+    func: str
+    kind: str
+    text: str
+    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+
+    @property
+    def where(self) -> str:
+        return f"{self.path}:{self.line}"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
+
+def is_go_source(path: str) -> bool:
+    return (path.endswith(".go") and not path.endswith("_test.go") and "zz_generated" not in path
+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)
+
+
+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("pkg/webhooks/") or "webhook" in path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs
+
+LOG_RE = re.compile(
+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")
+LOGGER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|\.)(log\w*|\w*logger\w*|l|lg|klog)$|LoggerFrom\(")
+
+
+def log_calls(gf: GoFile) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(LOG_RE):
+        recv = m.group(1)
+        last = re.sub(r"\(.*\)$", "", recv)
+        if not (LOGGER_NAME_RE.search(last) or "LoggerFrom(" in recv):
+            continue
+        method = m.group(4)
+        is_err = method.startswith("Error")
+        if is_err:
+            if len(args) < 2:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[1], args[2:]
+            level = "Error"
+        else:
+            if not args:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[0], args[1:]
+            if m.group(3) is not None:
+                level = f"V({m.group(3).strip()})"
+            else:
+                level = _alias_level(gf, last, m.start()) or "V(0)"
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr)
+        keys = []
+        for i in range(0, len(kv), 2):
+            k = kv[i]
+            if k.endswith("..."):
+                keys.append("…")
+                break
+            keys.append(string_value(k) or f"<{short(k, 20)}>")
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "log",
+                       msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 40)}>",
+                       {"level": level, "keys": tuple(keys)}))
+    return out
+
+
+_V_IN_RHS_RE = re.compile(r"\.V\(([^()]*)\)")
+
+
+def _alias_level(gf: GoFile, ident: str, pos: int) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve `logV := log.V(5)` style aliases assigned earlier in the same func.
+
+    Walks the ident's assignments backwards: an RHS with `.V(n)` gives the level,
+    a self-derived RHS (`logV = logV.WithValues(...)`) keeps looking, anything
+    else ends the search (plain logger, V(0)).
+    """
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*", ident):
+        return None
+    region = gf.src[gf.func_start(pos):pos]
+    assign = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])" + re.escape(ident) + r"\s*:?=\s*([^\n;]+)")
+    for m in reversed(list(assign.finditer(region))):
+        rhs = m.group(1)
+        if rhs.startswith("="):  # comparison, not assignment
+            continue
+        v = _V_IN_RHS_RE.search(rhs)
+        if v:
+            return f"V({v.group(1).strip()})"
+        if re.match(re.escape(ident) + r"\b", rhs.strip()):
+            continue
+        return None
+    return None
+
+
+def diff_logs(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["level"], r.attrs["keys"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.6)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.8)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        bk, hk = set(b.attrs["keys"]), set(h.attrs["keys"])
+        gone, new = sorted(bk - hk), sorted(hk - bk)
+        notes = []
+        if gone and new:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"log key renamed ({', '.join(gone)} → {', '.join(new)})"
+            notes.append("key renamed")
+        elif gone:
+            notes.append("key removed")
+        elif new:
+            notes.append("key added")
+        if b.attrs["level"] != h.attrs["level"]:
+            notes.insert(0, f"{b.attrs['level']}→{h.attrs['level']}")
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            notes.append("reworded")
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), _log_cell(b), _log_cell(h), h.where, "changed", fl,
+                          "; ".join(notes), group=h.path,
+                          meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(b.text, 50), _log_cell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, "removed",
+                          group=b.path, meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": ABSENT}))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), ABSENT, _log_cell(h), h.where, "added", {}, "added",
+                          group=h.path, meta={"level_before": ABSENT, "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _log_cell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    keys = ", ".join(r.attrs["keys"])
+    return f'{r.attrs["level"]} "{r.text}"' + (f" [{keys}]" if keys else "")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- events
+
+EVENT_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Eventf|Event|AnnotatedEventf)\(")
+EVENT_TYPE_RE = re.compile(r'^(?:[\w.]+\.)?EventType(Normal|Warning)$|^"(Normal|Warning)"$')
+
+
+def events(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(EVENT_RE):
+        t = next((i for i, a in enumerate(args) if EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(a)), None)
+        if t is None or t + 1 >= len(args):
+            continue
+        tm = EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(args[t])
+        etype = tm.group(1) or tm.group(2)
+        reason_expr = args[t + 1]
+        reason = string_value(reason_expr, env) or reason_expr
+        new_api = t == 2 and len(args) >= t + 4
+        msg_expr = args[t + 3] if new_api else (args[t + 2] if len(args) > t + 2 else "")
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr, env)
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "event", reason,
+                       {"type": etype, "msg": msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 30)}>"}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_events(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["type"], r.attrs["msg"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.attrs["msg"], 0.6)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"event reason {b.text} → {h.text}"
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", _ev(b), _ev(h), h.where, "changed", fl, group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {b.text}", _ev(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", ABSENT, _ev(h), h.where, "added", {}, group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+def _ev(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f'{r.attrs["type"]} {r.text}: "{r.attrs["msg"]}"'
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- messages
+
+FIELD_RE = re.compile(r"\bfield\.(Invalid|Forbidden|Required|NotSupported|Duplicate|TooMany|TooLong|"
+                      r"TooLongMaxLength|NotFound)\(")
+ERR_RE = re.compile(r"\b(errors\.New|fmt\.Errorf)\(")
+VERB_RE = re.compile(r"%(?:%|[-+# 0]*(?:\*|\d+)?(?:\.(?:\*|\d+))?[a-zA-Z])")
+FIELD_MSG_ARG = {"Invalid": 2, "Forbidden": 1, "Required": 1, "NotSupported": 2}
+
+
+def messages(gf: GoFile, sentinels: dict[str, str], *, field_calls: bool, errors_too: bool) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    consumed: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()
+    if field_calls:
+        for m, args, _ in gf.calls(FIELD_RE):
+            fn = m.group(1)
+            k = FIELD_MSG_ARG.get(fn)
+            if k is None or len(args) <= k:
+                continue
+            if fn == "NotSupported":
+                vals = literals_in(args[k])
+                if not vals:
+                    continue
+                text = "supported: " + ", ".join(vals)
+            else:
+                text = string_value(args[k])
+                if text is None:
+                    continue
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), f"field.{fn}", text))
+    if errors_too:
+        for m, args, end in gf.calls(ERR_RE):
+            func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+            if not func or not args:
+                continue  # package-level sentinels are reported via their uses
+            text = string_value(args[0])
+            if text is None:
+                continue
+            if m.group(1) == "fmt.Errorf" and "%w" in text:
+                verbs = [v for v in VERB_RE.findall(text) if v != "%%"]
+                for idx, v in enumerate(verbs):
+                    if v == "%w" and 1 + idx < len(args) and args[1 + idx] in sentinels:
+                        text = text.replace("%w", sentinels[args[1 + idx]], 1)
+                        consumed.add((gf.line(m.start()), args[1 + idx]))
+                        break
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), func, m.group(1), text))
+        if sentinels:
+            names = "|".join(map(re.escape, sorted(sentinels, key=len, reverse=True)))
+            for m in re.finditer(rf"^[ \t]*return\b[^\n]*\b({names})\b", gf.src, re.M):
+                func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+                if not func:
+                    continue
+                ln = gf.line(m.start())
+                if (ln, m.group(1)) in consumed:
+                    continue
+                out.append(Rec(gf.path, ln, func, "sentinel", sentinels[m.group(1)], {"var": m.group(1)}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_messages(surface: str, base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec],
+                  base_files: dict, head_files: dict) -> list[Change]:
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.45)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.75)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+
+    def logic_note(func: str, bpath: str | None, hpath: str | None) -> str:
+        bf = base_files.get(bpath) if bpath else None
+        hf = head_files.get(hpath) if hpath else None
+        if not func:
+            return ""
+        bs = bf.logic_signature(func) if bf else None
+        hs = hf.logic_signature(func) if hf else None
+        if bs is None and hs is not None:
+            return f"new func `{func}`"
+        if bs is not None and hs is not None and bs != hs:
+            return f"logic in `{func}` also changed"
+        return ""
+
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if b.text == h.text:
+            continue
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", b.text, h.text, h.where, "changed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {},
+                          logic_note(h.func, b.path, h.path), group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{b.kind} in `{b.func or '?'}`", b.text, ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {}, logic_note(b.func, b.path, None),
+                          group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        fl = {API: 1, TIGHTEN: f"new validation in `{h.func}`"} if surface == "Webhook" else {}
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", ABSENT, h.text, h.where, "added", fl,
+                          logic_note(h.func, None, h.path), group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- constants
+
+CONTRACT_CONST_RE = re.compile(r"(Reason|Label|Annotation|Condition|Finalizer)")
+
+
+def contract_consts(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    """Exported string constants that are part of the user contract."""
+    api = gf.path.startswith("apis/")
+    events_file = gf.path.endswith("/events.go")
+    out = {}
+    for name, value, line in string_consts(gf, env):
+        if not name[:1].isupper():
+            continue
+        if not (api or events_file or CONTRACT_CONST_RE.search(name)):
+            continue
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, line, "", _const_kind(name, value), value)
+    return out
+
+
+def _const_kind(name: str, value: str) -> str:
+    if re.search(r"Label|Annotation|Finalizer", name) or ("/" in value and "." in value.split("/")[0]):
+        return "key"
+    if "Reason" in name:
+        return "reason"
+    if "Condition" in name:
+        return "condition"
+    return "const"
+
+
+def diff_consts(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    gone = {n: r for n, r in base.items() if n not in head}
+    new = {n: r for n, r in head.items() if n not in base}
+    by_val_new = {}
+    for n, r in new.items():
+        by_val_new.setdefault(r.text, []).append(n)
+    for n, r in sorted(gone.items()):
+        if by_val_new.get(r.text):
+            n2 = by_val_new[r.text].pop(0)
+            new.pop(n2)
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}` → `{n2}`", f'{n} = "{r.text}"', f'{n2} = "{r.text}"',
+                              head[n2].where, "changed", {API: 1} if r.path.startswith("apis/") else {},
+                              "Go identifier renamed; value unchanged"))
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if r.kind == "key":
+            fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(r.kind)} `{r.text}` removed"
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{r.text}"', ABSENT, r.where, "removed", fl,
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n, r in sorted(new.items()):
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, f'"{r.text}"', r.where, "added", {},
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n in sorted(set(base) & set(head)):
+        b, h = base[n], head[n]
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl = {}
+            if h.kind in ("key", "reason", "condition"):
+                fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(h.kind)} `{b.text}` renamed to `{h.text}`"
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{b.text}"', f'"{h.text}"', h.where, "changed", fl,
+                              _kind_word(h.kind)))
+    return out
+
+
+def _kind_word(k: str) -> str:
+    return {"key": "label/annotation key", "reason": "reason", "condition": "condition type"}.get(k, "constant")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- metrics
+
+METRIC_RE = re.compile(r"\bprometheus\.New(Counter|Gauge|Histogram|Summary)(Vec)?\(")
+
+
+def metrics(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    out = {}
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(METRIC_RE):
+        if not args:
+            continue
+        opts = _composite(args[0])
+        name = string_value(opts.get("Name", ""), env)
+        if not name:
+            continue
+        ns = string_value(opts.get("Namespace", '""'), env) or ""
+        sub = string_value(opts.get("Subsystem", '""'), env) or ""
+        full = "_".join(x for x in (ns, sub, name) if x)
+        labels: list[str] = []
+        dynamic = False
+        if m.group(2) and len(args) > 1:
+            labels = literals_in(args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r'"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`', "", args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r"\[\]string|[{},\s]", "", rest)
+            dynamic = bool(rest)
+        help_text = " ".join((string_value(opts.get("Help", '""'), env) or "").split())
+        out[full] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), m.group(1).lower(), full,
+                        {"labels": labels, "dynamic": dynamic, "help": help_text,
+                         "buckets": " ".join(opts.get("Buckets", "").split())})
+    return out
+
+
+def _composite(expr: str) -> dict[str, str]:
+    from .gosrc import composite_fields
+    return composite_fields(expr)
+
+
+def diff_metrics(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", _mcell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` removed"}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, _mcell(h), h.where, "added", {}))
+            continue
+        if b.kind != h.kind:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` type", b.kind, h.kind, h.where, "changed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` type changed"}))
+        bl, hl = b.attrs["labels"], h.attrs["labels"]
+        if bl != hl or b.attrs["dynamic"] != h.attrs["dynamic"]:
+            gone = [x for x in bl if x not in hl]
+            fl = {}
+            if gone:
+                new = [x for x in hl if x not in bl]
+                what = "renamed" if new else "removed"
+                fl[ACTION] = f"metric `{n}` label {what} ({', '.join(gone)}" + (
+                    f" → {', '.join(new)})" if new else ")")
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` labels", _labels(b), _labels(h), h.where, "changed", fl))
+        if b.attrs["help"] != h.attrs["help"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` help", b.attrs["help"], h.attrs["help"], h.where, "changed", {}))
+        if b.attrs["buckets"] != h.attrs["buckets"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` buckets", b.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT,
+                              h.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT, h.where, "changed", {}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _labels(r: Rec) -> str:
+    s = ", ".join(r.attrs["labels"]) or "(none)"
+    return s + (" + dynamic" if r.attrs["dynamic"] else "")
+
+
+def _mcell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f"{r.kind} [{_labels(r)}]"
+
+
+DOC_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\|\s*`([a-z0-9_:]+)`\s*\|\s*([^|]*)\|(.*)\|\s*(.*?)\s*\|\s*$", re.M)
+
+
+def metrics_doc(text: str | None) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
+    out = {}
+    for m in DOC_ROW_RE.finditer(text or ""):
+        labels = re.findall(r"(?:^|<br>)\s*`([a-z0-9_]+)`", m.group(4))
+        out[m.group(1)] = labels
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- feature gates
+
+SPEC_RE = re.compile(r'\{\s*Version:\s*version\.MustParse\("([^"]+)"\)\s*,\s*Default:\s*(true|false)\s*,'
+                     r'\s*PreRelease:\s*(?:featuregate\.)?(\w+)(?:\s*,\s*LockToDefault:\s*(true|false))?\s*\}')
+
+
+def feature_gates(gf: GoFile) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    m = re.search(r"defaultVersionedFeatureGates\s*=\s*map\[[^\]]+\][\w.]+\{", gf.src)
+    if not m:
+        return {}
+    items, _ = _group_from(gf.src, m.end() - 1)
+    out = {}
+    for it in items:
+        km = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_][\w.]*)\s*:\s*(\{.*\})\s*\Z", it, re.S)
+        if not km:
+            continue
+        specs = [(v, d == "true", pre, lock == "true") for v, d, pre, lock in SPEC_RE.findall(km.group(2))]
+        if not specs:
+            continue
+        name = km.group(1).rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
+        pos = m.end() + gf.src[m.end():].find(km.group(1) + ":")
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(pos), "", "gate", name, {"specs": specs})
+    return out
+
+
+def _group_from(src: str, i: int):
+    from .gosrc import group_args
+    return group_args(src, i)
+
+
+def gate_cell(specs) -> str:
+    v, d, pre, lock = specs[-1]
+    return f"{pre} {'on' if d else 'off'} @v{v}" + (" (locked)" if lock else "")
+
+
+def diff_gates(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", gate_cell(b.attrs["specs"]), "removed", b.where,
+                              "removed", {API: 1, ACTION: f"feature gate `{n}` removed"} if not
+                              b.attrs["specs"][-1][3] else {API: 1}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, gate_cell(h.attrs["specs"]), h.where,
+                              "added", {API: 1}, meta={"gate": n, "specs": h.attrs["specs"]}))
+            continue
+        bs, hs = b.attrs["specs"][-1], h.attrs["specs"][-1]
+        if (bs[1], bs[2], bs[3]) == (hs[1], hs[2], hs[3]) and b.attrs["specs"] == h.attrs["specs"]:
+            continue
+        if (bs[1], bs[2], bs[3]) == (hs[1], hs[2], hs[3]):
+            continue  # only historical entries changed
+        fl = {API: 1}
+        if bs[1] != hs[1]:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"feature gate `{n}` default {'on' if hs[1] else 'off'}"
+        out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", gate_cell(b.attrs["specs"]), gate_cell(h.attrs["specs"]),
+                          h.where, "changed", fl, meta={"gate": n, "specs": h.attrs["specs"],
+                                                        "default_before": bs[1], "default_after": hs[1],
+                                                        "locked_now": bool(hs[3] and not bs[3])}))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- kueuectl
+
+FLAG_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Flags|PersistentFlags)\(\)\.([A-Z]\w*)\(")
+COLS_RE = re.compile(r"\[\]metav1\.TableColumnDefinition\{")
+
+
+def kueuectl_flags(gf: GoFile) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    out = {}
+    marks = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(FLAG_RE):
+        meth = m.group(2)
+        if meth.startswith("Mark"):
+            if args:
+                marks.append((string_value(args[0]), meth, string_value(args[1]) if len(args) > 1 else ""))
+            continue
+        has_var = "Var" in meth
+        a = args[1:] if has_var else args
+        if not a:
+            continue
+        name = string_value(a[0])
+        if not name:
+            continue
+        p = meth.endswith("P")
+        short_ = string_value(a[1]) if p and len(a) > 1 else ""
+        rest = a[2:] if p else a[1:]
+        typ = re.sub(r"(VarP|Var|P)$", "", meth) or meth
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "flag", name,
+                        {"type": typ, "short": short_ or "", "default": rest[0] if rest else "",
+                         "usage": (string_value(rest[1]) if len(rest) > 1 else "") or "",
+                         "persistent": m.group(1) == "PersistentFlags", "marks": []})
+    for name, meth, msg in marks:
+        if name in out:
+            out[name].attrs["marks"].append(meth.replace("Mark", "").lower() + (f": {msg}" if msg else ""))
+    return out
+
+
+def _flag_cell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    a = r.attrs
+    s = f"--{r.text}" + (f"/-{a['short']}" if a["short"] else "") + f" {a['type']}={a['default']}"
+    if a["marks"]:
+        s += " (" + "; ".join(a["marks"]) + ")"
+    return s
+
+
+def diff_flags(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec], path: str) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []

+            elif b.attrs["usage"] != h.attrs["usage"]:

+"""Minimal scanning helpers for gofmt'd Go source (no Go toolchain needed).
+
+Everything works on the output of ``blank_comments`` so offsets stay aligned
+with the original text while comments can never be mistaken for code.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import bisect
+import re
+
+LIT_RE = re.compile(r'"(?:[^"\\\n]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`')
+IDENT_RE = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\.[A-Za-z_]\w*)*")
+FUNC_RE = re.compile(r"^func\s+(?:\(\s*(?:\w+\s+)?\*?([\w.\[\], ]+?)\s*\)\s*)?([A-Za-z_]\w*)", re.M)
+_ESCAPES = {'"': '"', "\\": "\\", "'": "'"}
+_OPEN = {"(": ")", "{": "}", "[": "]"}
+
+
+def skip_literal(src: str, i: int) -> int:
+    """Return the index just past the string/rune literal starting at src[i]."""
+    q = src[i]
+    n = len(src)
+    if q == "`":
+        k = src.find("`", i + 1)
+        return n if k < 0 else k + 1
+    j = i + 1
+    while j < n:
+        ch = src[j]
+        if ch == "\\":
+            j += 2
+            continue
+        if ch == q:
+            return j + 1
+        if ch == "\n":
+            return j
+        j += 1
+    return n
+
+
+def blank_comments(src: str) -> str:
+    """Replace // and /* */ comments with spaces (newlines kept)."""
+    out = list(src)
+    i, n = 0, len(src)
+    while i < n:
+        c = src[i]
+        if c == "/" and i + 1 < n and src[i + 1] == "/":
+            j = src.find("\n", i)
+            j = n if j < 0 else j
+            out[i:j] = " " * (j - i)
+            i = j
+        elif c == "/" and i + 1 < n and src[i + 1] == "*":
+            j = src.find("*/", i + 2)
+            j = n if j < 0 else j + 2
+            for k in range(i, j):
+                if out[k] != "\n":
+                    out[k] = " "
+            i = j
+        elif c in "\"`'":
+            i = skip_literal(src, i)
+        else:
+            i += 1
+    return "".join(out)
+
+
+def decode(lit: str) -> str:
+    """Decode a Go string literal for display (only quote/backslash escapes)."""
+    if lit.startswith("`"):
+        return lit[1:-1]
+    body = lit[1:-1]
+    return re.sub(r"\\(.)", lambda m: _ESCAPES.get(m.group(1), m.group(0)), body)
+
+
+def split_top(expr: str, sep: str) -> list[str]:
+    """Split expr on sep at nesting depth 0, skipping literals."""
+    parts, depth, start, i = [], 0, 0, 0
+    n = len(expr)
+    while i < n:
+        c = expr[i]
+        if c in "\"`'":
+            i = skip_literal(expr, i)
+            continue
+        if c in _OPEN:
+            depth += 1
+        elif c in ")}]":
+            depth -= 1
+        elif c == sep and depth == 0:
+            parts.append(expr[start:i])
+            start = i + 1
+        i += 1
+    parts.append(expr[start:])
+    return [p.strip() for p in parts]
+
+
+def string_value(expr: str, env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> str | None:
+    """Value of a string literal, a `+` concatenation of literals, or (with env)
+    identifiers resolving to string constants. None when not static."""
+    expr = (expr or "").strip()
+    if not expr:
+        return None
+    out = []
+    for tok in split_top(expr, "+"):
+        m = LIT_RE.fullmatch(tok)
+        if m:
+            out.append(decode(tok))
+            continue
+        if env is not None and IDENT_RE.fullmatch(tok):
+            name = tok.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
+            if tok in env:
+                out.append(env[tok])
+                continue
+            if name in env:
+                out.append(env[name])
+                continue

  **Preserve package identity when resolving constants.**
  
  If a changed metrics package defines `KueueName` with a different value, `constants.KueueName` resolves to the local value. The report can then show unchanged metrics as removed and added, with a false ACTION REQUIRED hint. Resolve qualified names using their package-qualified bindings.

+"""Minimal scanning helpers for gofmt'd Go source (no Go toolchain needed).
+
+Everything works on the output of ``blank_comments`` so offsets stay aligned
+with the original text while comments can never be mistaken for code.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import bisect
+import re
+
+LIT_RE = re.compile(r'"(?:[^"\\\n]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`')
+IDENT_RE = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\.[A-Za-z_]\w*)*")
+FUNC_RE = re.compile(r"^func\s+(?:\(\s*(?:\w+\s+)?\*?([\w.\[\], ]+?)\s*\)\s*)?([A-Za-z_]\w*)", re.M)
+_ESCAPES = {'"': '"', "\\": "\\", "'": "'"}
+_OPEN = {"(": ")", "{": "}", "[": "]"}
+
+
+def skip_literal(src: str, i: int) -> int:
+    """Return the index just past the string/rune literal starting at src[i]."""
+    q = src[i]
+    n = len(src)
+    if q == "`":
+        k = src.find("`", i + 1)
+        return n if k < 0 else k + 1
+    j = i + 1
+    while j < n:
+        ch = src[j]
+        if ch == "\\":
+            j += 2
+            continue
+        if ch == q:
+            return j + 1
+        if ch == "\n":
+            return j
+        j += 1
+    return n
+
+
+def blank_comments(src: str) -> str:
+    """Replace // and /* */ comments with spaces (newlines kept)."""
+    out = list(src)
+    i, n = 0, len(src)
+    while i < n:
+        c = src[i]
+        if c == "/" and i + 1 < n and src[i + 1] == "/":
+            j = src.find("\n", i)
+            j = n if j < 0 else j
+            out[i:j] = " " * (j - i)
+            i = j
+        elif c == "/" and i + 1 < n and src[i + 1] == "*":
+            j = src.find("*/", i + 2)
+            j = n if j < 0 else j + 2
+            for k in range(i, j):
+                if out[k] != "\n":
+                    out[k] = " "
+            i = j
+        elif c in "\"`'":
+            i = skip_literal(src, i)
+        else:
+            i += 1
+    return "".join(out)
+
+
+def decode(lit: str) -> str:
+    """Decode a Go string literal for display (only quote/backslash escapes)."""
+    if lit.startswith("`"):
+        return lit[1:-1]
+    body = lit[1:-1]
+    return re.sub(r"\\(.)", lambda m: _ESCAPES.get(m.group(1), m.group(0)), body)
+
+
+def split_top(expr: str, sep: str) -> list[str]:
+    """Split expr on sep at nesting depth 0, skipping literals."""
+    parts, depth, start, i = [], 0, 0, 0
+    n = len(expr)
+    while i < n:
+        c = expr[i]
+        if c in "\"`'":
+            i = skip_literal(expr, i)
+            continue
+        if c in _OPEN:
+            depth += 1
+        elif c in ")}]":
+            depth -= 1
+        elif c == sep and depth == 0:
+            parts.append(expr[start:i])
+            start = i + 1
+        i += 1
+    parts.append(expr[start:])
+    return [p.strip() for p in parts]
+
+
+def string_value(expr: str, env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> str | None:
+    """Value of a string literal, a `+` concatenation of literals, or (with env)
+    identifiers resolving to string constants. None when not static."""
+    expr = (expr or "").strip()
+    if not expr:
+        return None
+    out = []
+    for tok in split_top(expr, "+"):
+        m = LIT_RE.fullmatch(tok)
+        if m:
+            out.append(decode(tok))
+            continue
+        if env is not None and IDENT_RE.fullmatch(tok):
+            name = tok.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
+            if tok in env:
+                out.append(env[tok])
+                continue
+            if name in env:
+                out.append(env[name])
+                continue
+        return None
+    return "".join(out)
+
+
+def literals_in(expr: str) -> list[str]:
+    return [decode(m.group(0)) for m in LIT_RE.finditer(expr or "")]
+
+
+def group_args(src: str, i: int) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
+    """src[i] is an opening ( or {; return top-level comma-separated items and
+    the index just past the matching closer."""
+    closer = _OPEN[src[i]]
+    depth, start, j, n = 0, i + 1, i, len(src)
+    args: list[str] = []
+    while j < n:
+        c = src[j]
+        if c in "\"`'":
+            j = skip_literal(src, j)
+            continue
+        if c in _OPEN:
+            depth += 1
+        elif c in ")}]":
+            depth -= 1
+            if depth == 0:
+                last = src[start:j].strip()
+                if last:
+                    args.append(last)
+                return args, j + 1
+        elif c == "," and depth == 1:
+            args.append(src[start:j].strip())
+            start = j + 1
+        j += 1
+    return args, n
+
+
+def composite_fields(expr: str) -> dict[str, str]:
+    """Key: value fields of a composite literal such as `prometheus.GaugeOpts{...}`."""
+    k = expr.find("{")
+    if k < 0:
+        return {}
+    items, _ = group_args(expr, k)
+    out = {}
+    for it in items:
+        m = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*:\s*(.*)\Z", it, re.S)
+        if m:
+            out[m.group(1)] = m.group(2).strip()
+    return out
+
+
+class GoFile:
+    """A parsed view of one Go source file at one revision."""
+
+    def __init__(self, path: str, src: str):
+        self.path = path
+        self.raw = src
+        self.src = blank_comments(src)
+        self._line_starts = [0] + [m.end() for m in re.finditer("\n", self.src)]
+        self.funcs = []  # (start, end, name)
+        for m in FUNC_RE.finditer(self.src):
+            end = self.src.find("\n}", m.end())
+            end = len(self.src) if end < 0 else end + 2
+            recv = (m.group(1) or "").split("[")[0].strip()
+            name = f"{recv}.{m.group(2)}" if recv else m.group(2)
+            self.funcs.append((m.start(), end, name))
+        self._func_starts = [f[0] for f in self.funcs]
+
+    def line(self, pos: int) -> int:
+        return bisect.bisect_right(self._line_starts, pos)
+
+    def func_at(self, pos: int) -> str:
+        k = bisect.bisect_right(self._func_starts, pos) - 1
+        if k >= 0 and self.funcs[k][0] <= pos < self.funcs[k][1]:
+            return self.funcs[k][2]
+        return ""
+
+    def func_start(self, pos: int) -> int:
+        """Start offset of the function enclosing pos (0 at package level)."""
+        k = bisect.bisect_right(self._func_starts, pos) - 1
+        if k >= 0 and self.funcs[k][0] <= pos < self.funcs[k][1]:
+            return self.funcs[k][0]
+        return 0
+
+    def func_body(self, name: str) -> str | None:
+        for s, e, n in self.funcs:
+            if n == name:
+                return self.src[s:e]
+        return None
+
+    def logic_signature(self, name: str) -> str | None:
+        """Function code with literals and whitespace normalized away."""
+        body = self.func_body(name)
+        if body is None:
+            return None
+        return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", LIT_RE.sub('""', body)).strip()
+
+    def calls(self, pattern: re.Pattern):
+        """Yield (match, args, end) for each pattern match ending at '('."""
+        for m in pattern.finditer(self.src):
+            open_idx = m.end() - 1
+            if self.src[open_idx] != "(":
+                continue
+            args, end = group_args(self.src, open_idx)
+            yield m, args, end

@@ -0,0 +1,370 @@
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+"""Tiny YAML subset loader for controller-gen CRD output (python3 stdlib only).
+
+Supports block mappings and sequences (including `- key: v` items and
+sequences at the parent key's indent), plain multi-line scalars, single and
+double quoted (multi-line) scalars, literal/folded block scalars (|, |-, |+,
+>, >-), empty flow collections and simple flow sequences, comments on their
+own line, and multiple documents. That is everything controller-gen and
+sigs.k8s.io/yaml emit; anchors, tags and complex keys are out of scope.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+
+_INT_RE = re.compile(r"^[-+]?(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*)$")
+_FLOAT_RE = re.compile(r"^[-+]?(?:\.[0-9]+|[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]*)?)(?:[eE][-+]?[0-9]+)?$")
+
+
+class YAMLError(ValueError):
+    pass
+
+
+def _indent(line: str) -> int:
+    return len(line) - len(line.lstrip(" "))
+
+
+def _is_blank(line: str) -> bool:
+    s = line.strip()
+    return not s or s.startswith("#")
+
+
+def _strip_comment(s: str) -> str:
+    return re.sub(r"\s+#.*$", "", s)
+
+
+def _plain(s: str):
+    if s in ("", "~", "null", "Null", "NULL"):
+        return None
+    if s in ("true", "True", "TRUE"):
+        return True
+    if s in ("false", "False", "FALSE"):
+        return False
+    if _INT_RE.match(s):
+        return int(s)
+    if _FLOAT_RE.match(s) and any(c.isdigit() for c in s):
+        return float(s)
+    return s
+
+
+def _split_key(text: str):
+    """Return (key, rest) if text is `key: rest` / `key:`; else None."""
+    if text[:1] in ("'", '"'):
+        q = text[0]
+        i = 1
+        while i < len(text):
+            if text[i] == q:
+                if q == "'" and text[i + 1:i + 2] == "'":
+                    i += 2
+                    continue
+                break
+            if q == '"' and text[i] == "\\":
+                i += 1
+            i += 1
+        key = _unquote(text[:i + 1])
+        rest = text[i + 1:]
+        if rest.startswith(":") and (len(rest) == 1 or rest[1] == " "):
+            return key, rest[1:].strip()
+        return None
+    m = re.match(r"^([^#'\"{\[][^:]*?|[^:#\s][^:]*?):(?: (.*)|$)", text)
+    if not m:
+        return None
+    return m.group(1).strip(), (m.group(2) or "").strip()
+
+
+def _unquote(s: str) -> str:
+    if s.startswith("'"):
+        return s[1:-1].replace("''", "'")
+    body = s[1:-1]
+    out, i = [], 0
+    esc = {"n": "\n", "t": "\t", '"': '"', "\\": "\\", "/": "/", "r": "\r", "0": "\0", " ": " "}
+    while i < len(body):
+        c = body[i]
+        if c == "\\" and i + 1 < len(body):
+            n = body[i + 1]
+            if n == "u":
+                out.append(chr(int(body[i + 2:i + 6], 16)))
+                i += 6
+                continue
+            if n == "x":
+                out.append(chr(int(body[i + 2:i + 4], 16)))
+                i += 4
+                continue
+            out.append(esc.get(n, n))
+            i += 2
+            continue
+        out.append(c)
+        i += 1
+    return "".join(out)
+
+
+def _fold(parts: list[str]) -> str:
+    """Join flow-scalar continuation lines: single break -> space, blank -> \\n."""
+    out = ""
+    pending_nl = 0
+    for k, p in enumerate(parts):
+        if p == "":
+            pending_nl += 1
+            continue
+        if k == 0 or not out:
+            out += ("\n" * pending_nl) + p if out else p
+        elif pending_nl:
+            out += "\n" * pending_nl + p
+        else:
+            out += " " + p
+        pending_nl = 0
+    return out
+
+
+class _Parser:
+    def __init__(self, text: str):
+        self.lines = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n")
+        self.n = len(self.lines)
+
+    def _next(self, i: int) -> int:
+        while i < self.n and _is_blank(self.lines[i]):
+            i += 1
+        return i
+
+    def documents(self):
+        docs, i = [], 0
+        while True:
+            i = self._next(i)
+            if i >= self.n:
+                break
+            if self.lines[i].startswith("---"):
+                i += 1
+                continue
+            if self.lines[i].startswith("..."):
+                i += 1
+                continue
+            val, i = self.node(i, _indent(self.lines[i]))
+            docs.append(val)
+        return docs
+
+    def node(self, i: int, indent: int):
+        i = self._next(i)
+        if i >= self.n:
+            return None, i
+        line = self.lines[i]
+        text = line[indent:]
+        if text == "-" or text.startswith("- "):
+            return self.seq(i, indent)
+        return self.mapping(i, indent)
+
+    def seq(self, i: int, indent: int):
+        out = []
+        while True:
+            i = self._next(i)
+            if i >= self.n:
+                break
+            line = self.lines[i]
+            if _indent(line) != indent or line.startswith("---"):
+                break
+            text = line[indent:]
+            if not (text == "-" or text.startswith("- ")):
+                break
+            rest = text[1:].lstrip(" ")
+            if not rest:
+                val, i = self.node(i + 1, self._child_indent(i + 1, indent))
+                out.append(val)
+                continue
+            inner = indent + (len(text) - len(rest))
+            if _split_key(rest) is not None and not rest.startswith(("'", '"')) or (
+                    rest[:1] in ("'", '"') and _split_key(rest) is not None):
+                self.lines[i] = " " * inner + rest
+                val, i = self.mapping(i, inner)
+                out.append(val)
+            else:
+                val, i = self.scalar(rest, i, indent)
+                out.append(val)
+        return out, i
+
+    def _child_indent(self, i: int, parent: int) -> int:
+        j = self._next(i)
+        if j >= self.n:
+            return parent + 2
+        return _indent(self.lines[j])
+
+    def mapping(self, i: int, indent: int):
+        out = {}
+        while True:
+            i = self._next(i)
+            if i >= self.n:
+                break
+            line = self.lines[i]
+            if _indent(line) != indent or line.startswith(("---", "...")):
+                if _indent(line) > indent:
+                    raise YAMLError(f"line {i + 1}: unexpected indentation")
+                break
+            text = line[indent:]
+            if text == "-" or text.startswith("- "):
+                break
+            kv = _split_key(text)
+            if kv is None:
+                raise YAMLError(f"line {i + 1}: expected 'key: value': {text!r}")
+            key, rest = kv
+            if rest == "":
+                j = self._next(i + 1)
+                if j < self.n and _indent(self.lines[j]) > indent:
+                    val, i = self.node(j, _indent(self.lines[j]))
+                elif j < self.n and _indent(self.lines[j]) == indent and (
+                        self.lines[j][indent:] == "-" or self.lines[j][indent:].startswith("- ")):
+                    val, i = self.seq(j, indent)
+                else:
+                    val, i = None, i + 1
+            elif rest[0] in "|>":
+                val, i = self.block(rest, i, indent)
+            else:
+                val, i = self.scalar(rest, i, indent)
+            out[key] = val
+        return out, i
+
+    def block(self, header: str, i: int, indent: int):
+        m = re.match(r"^([|>])([-+]?)([1-9]?)([-+]?)\s*(#.*)?$", header)
+        if not m:
+            raise YAMLError(f"line {i + 1}: bad block scalar header {header!r}")
+        style = m.group(1)
+        chomp = m.group(2) or m.group(4)
+        explicit = m.group(3)
+        j = i + 1
+        body: list[str] = []
+        block_indent = indent + int(explicit) if explicit else None
+        while j < self.n:
+            ln = self.lines[j]
+            if ln.strip() == "":
+                body.append("")
+                j += 1
+                continue
+            ind = _indent(ln)
+            if block_indent is None:
+                if ind <= indent:
+                    break
+                block_indent = ind
+            if ind < block_indent:
+                break
+            body.append(ln[block_indent:])
+            j += 1
+        trailing = 0
+        while body and body[-1] == "":
+            body.pop()
+            trailing += 1
+        if style == "|":
+            text = "\n".join(body)
+        else:
+            text = ""
+            prev_more = False
+            for k, ln in enumerate(body):
+                more = ln.startswith((" ", "\t"))
+                if k == 0:
+                    text = ln
+                elif ln == "":
+                    text += "\n"
+                elif more or prev_more or text.endswith("\n"):
+                    text += ("\n" if not text.endswith("\n") else "") + ln
+                else:
+                    text += " " + ln
+                prev_more = more
+        if chomp == "-":
+            pass
+        elif chomp == "+":
+            text += "\n" * (trailing + 1) if body else "\n" * trailing
+        elif body:
+            text += "\n"
+        return text, j
+
+    def scalar(self, rest: str, i: int, indent: int):
+        """Plain or quoted scalar starting on line i (possibly multi-line)."""
+        if rest[0] in ("'", '"'):
+            q = rest[0]
+            buf = [rest]
+            j = i
+            while not self._closed(" ".join(buf), q):
+                j += 1
+                if j >= self.n:
+                    raise YAMLError(f"line {i + 1}: unterminated quoted scalar")
+                buf.append(self.lines[j].strip())
+            raw = buf[0]
+            for p in buf[1:]:
+                raw = raw + ("\n" if p == "" else (" " if not raw.endswith("\n") else "")) + p
+            raw = raw.rstrip()
+            end = self._quote_end(raw, q)
+            return _unquote(raw[:end + 1]), j + 1
+        if rest.startswith("[") or rest.startswith("{"):
+            return self.flow(rest, i)
+        parts = [_strip_comment(rest)]
+        j = i + 1
+        while j < self.n:
+            ln = self.lines[j]
+            if ln.strip() == "":
+                parts.append("")
+                j += 1
+                continue
+            if _indent(ln) <= indent or ln.lstrip().startswith("#"):
+                break
+            parts.append(_strip_comment(ln.strip()))
+            j += 1
+        while parts and parts[-1] == "":
+            parts.pop()
+            j -= 1
+        while j > i + 1 and self.lines[j - 1].strip() == "":
+            j -= 1
+        text = _fold(parts)
+        return (_plain(text) if len(parts) == 1 else text), j
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _quote_end(s: str, q: str) -> int:
+        i = 1
+        while i < len(s):
+            if s[i] == q:
+                if q == "'" and s[i + 1:i + 2] == "'":
+                    i += 2
+                    continue
+                return i
+            if q == '"' and s[i] == "\\":
+                i += 1
+            i += 1
+        return -1
+
+    def _closed(self, s: str, q: str) -> bool:
+        return self._quote_end(s, q) >= 0
+
+    def flow(self, rest: str, i: int):
+        s = rest.strip()
+        if s in ("[]", "{}"):
+            return ([] if s == "[]" else {}), i + 1
+        if s.startswith("[") and s.endswith("]"):
+            items = [x.strip() for x in s[1:-1].split(",") if x.strip()]

  **Split flow sequences outside quoted scalars.**
  
  For `default: ["a,b"]`, the parser returns `["", "b\""]` instead of `["a,b"]`. Rewriting an equivalent block sequence in flow style can therefore produce a false default change and ACTION REQUIRED hint. Make tokenization quote-aware.

+"""Change records and before/after pairing shared by all surfaces."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import difflib
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+ABSENT = "—"
+
+# Visible ordering of surfaces (most consequential first).
+SURFACE_ORDER = [
+    "CRD", "CEL", "CRD columns", "Webhook", "Feature gate", "Metric",
+    "Constant", "Event", "kueuectl", "Log",
+]
+
+# Flags understood by the renderer / compat matrix.
+ACTION = "action"            # likely ACTION REQUIRED release note (value = reason)
+BREAKING = "breaking"        # an existing manifest/consumer may break (static)
+TIGHTEN = "tighten"          # new/changed validation; acceptance unverified
+API = "api"                  # counts as an API change (compat matrix)
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Change:
+    surface: str
+    item: str
+    before: str = ABSENT
+    after: str = ABSENT
+    where: str = ""
+    kind: str = "changed"    # added | removed | changed
+    flags: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+    note: str = ""
+    group: str = ""
+    meta: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+
+    def to_json(self) -> dict:
+        return {
+            "surface": self.surface, "item": self.item, "before": self.before,
+            "after": self.after, "where": self.where, "kind": self.kind,
+            "flags": self.flags, "note": self.note, "group": self.group,
+        }

  When an added CRD object has more than three direct children, JSON omits the remaining child names and summaries. Consumers must reread the CRD to recover them. Include the stored child details in each change.

+"""Markdown block for inject-pr-notes (content only: no markers, no header)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections import OrderedDict
+
+from .core import SURFACES_CHECKED, Report
+from .goextract import level_histogram
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, BREAKING, SURFACE_ORDER, Change, short
+
+RELEASE_NOTES_SKILL = "cmd/experimental/skills/kueue-release-notes"
+# Content budget: the injector adds one header line; the family budget is 25.
+DEFAULT_BUDGET = 24
+MAX_DETAIL_ROWS = 120
+COLLAPSE_AT = 3
+WIDE = 110  # before/after cell width when a surface is shown row by row
+
+
+def empty_line() -> str:
+    return f"No user-facing contract change detected (surfaces checked: {', '.join(SURFACES_CHECKED)})."
+
+
+def cell(s: str, n: int = 60) -> str:
+    s = short(s, n) if n else " ".join(str(s).split())
+    s = s.replace("|", "\\|")
+    if s in ("", ABSENT):
+        return ABSENT
+    return s
+
+
+def code(s: str, n: int = 60) -> str:
+    """Inline-code cell (backticks stripped from content to keep markdown sane)."""
+    c = cell(str(s).replace("`", "'"), n)
+    return c if c == ABSENT else f"`{c}`"
+
+
+def _counts(items: list[Change]) -> str:
+    n_add = sum(c.kind == "added" for c in items)
+    n_rm = sum(c.kind == "removed" for c in items)
+    bits = [(len(items) - n_add - n_rm, "changed"), (n_add, "added"), (n_rm, "removed")]
+    return ", ".join(f"{n} {w}" for n, w in bits if n)
+
+
+def _files(items: list[Change]) -> str:
+    return ", ".join(sorted({(c.group or c.where.split(":")[0]).rsplit("/", 1)[-1] for c in items}))
+
+
+def _important_first(items: list[Change]) -> list[Change]:
+    return sorted(items, key=lambda c: (0 if c.flags.get(BREAKING) else 1, 0 if c.flags.get(ACTION) else 1))
+
+
+def _log_row(items: list[Change]) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
+    moved = sum(1 for c in items if c.kind == "changed" and c.meta.get("level_before") != c.meta.get("level_after"))
+    renamed = sum(1 for c in items if c.flags.get(ACTION))
+    reworded = sum(1 for c in items if "reworded" in c.note)
+    n_add = sum(c.kind == "added" for c in items)
+    n_rm = sum(c.kind == "removed" for c in items)
+    what = (f"{len(items)} log calls in {short(_files(items), 40)}: {moved} V-level moves, {reworded} reworded, "
+            f"{n_add} added, {n_rm} removed" + (f", {renamed} key rename{'s' if renamed != 1 else ''}"
+                                                 if renamed else ""))
+    return ("Log", cell(what, 0), level_histogram(items, "level_before"), level_histogram(items, "level_after"))
+
+
+def _surface_rows(surface: str, items: list[Change], mode: str) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, str]]:
+    """mode: "full" (row per change up to COLLAPSE_AT, else 2 + summary) or "one" (single row)."""
+    if surface == "Log" and (len(items) > COLLAPSE_AT or mode == "one"):
+        return [_log_row(items)]
+    if len(items) <= COLLAPSE_AT and mode == "full":
+        return [(surface, cell(c.item + (f" ({c.note})" if c.note and surface != "Log" else ""), WIDE),
+                 code(c.before, WIDE), code(c.after, WIDE)) for c in items]
+    first = _important_first(items)
+    if mode == "one":
+        c = first[0]
+        more = f" (+{len(items) - 1} more; {len(items)} total: {_counts(items)})" if len(items) > 1 else ""
+        return [(surface, cell(c.item, 60) + more, code(c.before), code(c.after))]
+    rows = [(surface, cell(c.item, 70), code(c.before), code(c.after)) for c in first[:2]]
+    rows.append((surface, cell(f"… {len(items) - 2} more (of {len(items)}: {_counts(items)}) in {_files(items)}",
+                               0), "see details", ""))
+    return rows
+
+
+def render(rep: Report, budget: int = DEFAULT_BUDGET) -> str:
+    if not rep.changes:
+        return empty_line() + "\n"
+    by_surface: OrderedDict[str, list[Change]] = OrderedDict()
+    for s in SURFACE_ORDER:
+        xs = [c for c in rep.changes if c.surface == s]
+        if xs:
+            by_surface[s] = xs
+    for c in rep.changes:
+        if c.surface not in by_surface:
+            by_surface.setdefault(c.surface, []).append(c)
+
+    n = len(rep.changes)
+    verdict = (f"**{n} user-facing contract change{'s' if n != 1 else ''}** "
+               f"({', '.join(f'{s} {len(v)}' for s, v in by_surface.items())}) · "
+               "runner: static (git blobs at merge base vs head, no cluster)")
+
+
+    compat = []
+    if rep.compat:
+        compat = ["", "**Compatibility** (static; ⚠️ = unverified)", "", "| Check | Result |", "|---|---|"]
+        compat += [f"| {a} | {cell(b, 0)} |" for a, b in rep.compat]
+    release = []
+    if rep.actions:
+        reasons = "; ".join(rep.actions[:3]) + (f"; +{len(rep.actions) - 3} more" if len(rep.actions) > 3 else "")
+        release = ["", f"⚠️ Likely **ACTION REQUIRED** release note: {reasons}. Draft it with the "
+                       f"`kueue-release-notes` skill (`{RELEASE_NOTES_SKILL}`)."]
+    details = _details(rep)
+
+    def visible(lines):
+        return sum(1 for x in lines if x.strip())
+
+    fixed = visible([verdict, "| a |", "|---|"] + compat + release) + 1  # + <details> summary line
+    room = budget - fixed
+    # Fit the table: collapse the largest surfaces to one row each before dropping anything.
+    modes = {s: "full" for s in by_surface}
+    per = {s: _surface_rows(s, xs, "full") for s, xs in by_surface.items()}
+    while sum(map(len, per.values())) > room:
+        big = max((s for s in per if modes[s] == "full" and len(per[s]) > 1), key=lambda s: len(per[s]),
+                  default=None)
+        if big is None:
+            break
+        modes[big] = "one"
+        per[big] = _surface_rows(big, by_surface[big], "one")
+    rows = [r for s in by_surface for r in per[s]]
+    if len(rows) > room:
+        keep = max(room - 1, 1)

+                     for n in names if n.endswith(".go") and not n.endswith("_test.go")]
+            for _ in range(2):  # two passes resolve cross-file `A + "-x"` constants
+                for gf in files:
+                    consts.update({n: v for n, v, _ in string_consts(gf, consts)})
+            for gf in files:
+                sentinels.update(sentinel_errors(gf))
+            self._pkg[d] = (consts, sentinels)
+        return self._pkg[d]

+def _git(repo: str, *args: str) -> str:
+    try:
+        res = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, *args], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
+        raise GitError(f"git {' '.join(args[:2])}: {exc.stderr.strip()}") from exc
+    return res.stdout

+
+def changed_files(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> list[tuple[str | None, str | None]]:
+    """(base_path, head_path) per changed file of `git diff base...head` (renames kept)."""
+    out = _git(repo, "diff", "--name-status", "-z", "-M", "--no-ext-diff", f"{base}...{head}")
+    toks = out.split("\0")
+    res = []
+    i = 0
+    while i < len(toks) and toks[i]:

+        else:

+            i += 2
+    return res
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Report:
+    base: str
+    head: str
+    changes: list[Change] = field(default_factory=list)
+    compat: list[tuple[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)
+    actions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    files: int = 0
+
+    def to_json(self) -> dict:
+        return {"base": self.base, "head": self.head, "files_changed": self.files,
+                "surfaces_checked": list(SURFACES_CHECKED),
+                "changes": [c.to_json() for c in self.changes],
+                "compat": [{"check": a, "result": b} for a, b in self.compat],
+                "action_required": self.actions, "notes": self.notes}
+
+
+def collect(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> Report:
+    b, h = Rev(repo, base), Rev(repo, head)
+    files = changed_files(repo, b.sha, h.sha)
+    rep = Report(b.sha, h.sha, files=len(files))

+    metrics_touched = False

+"""Collect contract changes between two revisions of a kueue tree (read-only git)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations

+import posixpath

+CHART_CRD_PREFIX = "charts/kueue/templates/crd/"
+METRICS_DOC = "site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md"
+FEATURES_FILE = "pkg/features/kube_features.go"
+CONSTANTS_FILE = "pkg/constants/constants.go"
+
+SURFACES_CHECKED = ("CRD schema/defaults/validation", "CEL", "webhook messages",
+                    "condition/event reasons + API constants", "metrics (+ docs)", "feature gates",
+                    "log V-levels/keys", "kueuectl flags/columns/errors")
+
+
+class GitError(RuntimeError):
+    pass
+
+
+class Rev:
+    """Read-only view of one commit (git cat-file; never touches the worktree)."""
+
+    def __init__(self, repo: str, rev: str):
+        self.repo = repo
+        self.sha = _git(repo, "rev-parse", "--verify", f"{rev}^{{commit}}").strip()
+        self._blobs: dict = {}
+        self._pkg: dict = {}
+
+    def read(self, path: str | None) -> str | None:
+        if not path:
+            return None
+        if path not in self._blobs:
+            try:
+                self._blobs[path] = _git(self.repo, "show", f"{self.sha}:{path}")
+            except GitError:
+                self._blobs[path] = None
+        return self._blobs[path]
+
+    def gofile(self, path: str | None) -> GoFile | None:
+        src = self.read(path)
+        return GoFile(path, src) if src is not None else None
+
+    def package(self, d: str) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, str]]:
+        """(string consts, sentinel errors) across non-test .go files in dir d."""
+        if d not in self._pkg:
+            consts: dict[str, str] = {}
+            sentinels: dict[str, str] = {}
+            try:
+                names = _git(self.repo, "ls-tree", "--name-only", f"{self.sha}:{d}" if d else self.sha).split()
+            except GitError:
+                names = []
+            files = [GoFile(posixpath.join(d, n), self.read(posixpath.join(d, n)) or "")
+                     for n in names if n.endswith(".go") and not n.endswith("_test.go")]
+            for _ in range(2):  # two passes resolve cross-file `A + "-x"` constants
+                for gf in files:
+                    consts.update({n: v for n, v, _ in string_consts(gf, consts)})
+            for gf in files:
+                sentinels.update(sentinel_errors(gf))
+            self._pkg[d] = (consts, sentinels)
+        return self._pkg[d]
+
+
+def _git(repo: str, *args: str) -> str:
+    try:
+        res = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, *args], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
+        raise GitError(f"git {' '.join(args[:2])}: {exc.stderr.strip()}") from exc
+    return res.stdout
+
+
+def changed_files(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> list[tuple[str | None, str | None]]:
+    """(base_path, head_path) per changed file of `git diff base...head` (renames kept)."""
+    out = _git(repo, "diff", "--name-status", "-z", "-M", "--no-ext-diff", f"{base}...{head}")
+    toks = out.split("\0")
+    res = []
+    i = 0
+    while i < len(toks) and toks[i]:
+        st = toks[i]
+        if st[0] in "RC":
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], toks[i + 2]))
+            i += 3
+        elif st[0] == "A":
+            res.append((None, toks[i + 1]))
+            i += 2
+        elif st[0] == "D":
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], None))
+            i += 2
+        else:
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], toks[i + 1]))
+            i += 2
+    return res
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Report:
+    base: str
+    head: str
+    changes: list[Change] = field(default_factory=list)
+    compat: list[tuple[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)
+    actions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    files: int = 0
+
+    def to_json(self) -> dict:
+        return {"base": self.base, "head": self.head, "files_changed": self.files,
+                "surfaces_checked": list(SURFACES_CHECKED),
+                "changes": [c.to_json() for c in self.changes],
+                "compat": [{"check": a, "result": b} for a, b in self.compat],
+                "action_required": self.actions, "notes": self.notes}
+
+
+def collect(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> Report:
+    b, h = Rev(repo, base), Rev(repo, head)
+    files = changed_files(repo, b.sha, h.sha)
+    rep = Report(b.sha, h.sha, files=len(files))
+    changes: list[Change] = []
+    head_gates = set(gx.feature_gates(h.gofile(FEATURES_FILE)).keys()) if h.read(FEATURES_FILE) else set()
+
+    log_b, log_h, wh_b, wh_h, cli_b, cli_h = [], [], [], [], [], []
+    gofiles_b: dict = {}
+    gofiles_h: dict = {}
+    metrics_touched = False
+    chart_touched = set()
+    crd_touched = set()
+
+    for bp, hp in files:
+        path = hp or bp
+        if path.startswith(CHART_CRD_PREFIX):
+            chart_touched.add(path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1])
+            continue
+        if path.startswith(CRD_PREFIX) and path.endswith(".yaml"):
+            bc, hc = crd.parse(b.read(bp)), crd.parse(h.read(hp))
+            if bc or hc:
+                crd_touched.add(path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1])
+                changes += crd.diff(path, bc, hc, head_gates)
+            continue
+        if not gx.is_go_source(path):
+            continue
+        bgf, hgf = b.gofile(bp), h.gofile(hp)
+        if bgf:
+            gofiles_b[bp] = bgf
+        if hgf:
+            gofiles_h[hp] = hgf
+        empty = GoFile(path, "")
+        bgf_, hgf_ = bgf or empty, hgf or empty
+        benv, bsent = b.package(posixpath.dirname(bp)) if bp else ({}, {})
+        henv, hsent = h.package(posixpath.dirname(hp)) if hp else ({}, {})
+
+        if path.startswith(("pkg/", "cmd/")):
+            log_b += gx.log_calls(bgf_)
+            log_h += gx.log_calls(hgf_)
+        if path.startswith("pkg/"):
+            changes += gx.diff_events(gx.events(bgf_, _env(b, benv)), gx.events(hgf_, _env(h, henv)))
+        if path.startswith(("pkg/", "apis/")):
+            web = gx.is_webhook_file(path)
+            wh_b += gx.messages(bgf_, bsent, field_calls=True, errors_too=web)
+            wh_h += gx.messages(hgf_, hsent, field_calls=True, errors_too=web)
+        if path.startswith(("pkg/", "apis/")):
+            changes += gx.diff_consts(gx.contract_consts(bgf_, benv), gx.contract_consts(hgf_, henv))
+        if path.startswith("pkg/metrics/"):
+            metrics_touched = True
+            changes += gx.diff_metrics(gx.metrics(bgf_, _env(b, benv)), gx.metrics(hgf_, _env(h, henv)))

+CHART_CRD_PREFIX = "charts/kueue/templates/crd/"
+METRICS_DOC = "site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md"
+FEATURES_FILE = "pkg/features/kube_features.go"
+CONSTANTS_FILE = "pkg/constants/constants.go"
+
+SURFACES_CHECKED = ("CRD schema/defaults/validation", "CEL", "webhook messages",
+                    "condition/event reasons + API constants", "metrics (+ docs)", "feature gates",
+                    "log V-levels/keys", "kueuectl flags/columns/errors")
+
+
+class GitError(RuntimeError):
+    pass
+
+
+class Rev:
+    """Read-only view of one commit (git cat-file; never touches the worktree)."""
+
+    def __init__(self, repo: str, rev: str):
+        self.repo = repo
+        self.sha = _git(repo, "rev-parse", "--verify", f"{rev}^{{commit}}").strip()
+        self._blobs: dict = {}
+        self._pkg: dict = {}
+
+    def read(self, path: str | None) -> str | None:
+        if not path:
+            return None
+        if path not in self._blobs:
+            try:
+                self._blobs[path] = _git(self.repo, "show", f"{self.sha}:{path}")
+            except GitError:
+                self._blobs[path] = None
+        return self._blobs[path]
+
+    def gofile(self, path: str | None) -> GoFile | None:
+        src = self.read(path)
+        return GoFile(path, src) if src is not None else None
+
+    def package(self, d: str) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, str]]:
+        """(string consts, sentinel errors) across non-test .go files in dir d."""
+        if d not in self._pkg:
+            consts: dict[str, str] = {}
+            sentinels: dict[str, str] = {}
+            try:
+                names = _git(self.repo, "ls-tree", "--name-only", f"{self.sha}:{d}" if d else self.sha).split()
+            except GitError:
+                names = []
+            files = [GoFile(posixpath.join(d, n), self.read(posixpath.join(d, n)) or "")
+                     for n in names if n.endswith(".go") and not n.endswith("_test.go")]
+            for _ in range(2):  # two passes resolve cross-file `A + "-x"` constants
+                for gf in files:
+                    consts.update({n: v for n, v, _ in string_consts(gf, consts)})
+            for gf in files:
+                sentinels.update(sentinel_errors(gf))
+            self._pkg[d] = (consts, sentinels)
+        return self._pkg[d]
+
+
+def _git(repo: str, *args: str) -> str:
+    try:
+        res = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, *args], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
+        raise GitError(f"git {' '.join(args[:2])}: {exc.stderr.strip()}") from exc
+    return res.stdout
+
+
+def changed_files(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> list[tuple[str | None, str | None]]:
+    """(base_path, head_path) per changed file of `git diff base...head` (renames kept)."""
+    out = _git(repo, "diff", "--name-status", "-z", "-M", "--no-ext-diff", f"{base}...{head}")
+    toks = out.split("\0")
+    res = []
+    i = 0
+    while i < len(toks) and toks[i]:
+        st = toks[i]
+        if st[0] in "RC":
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], toks[i + 2]))
+            i += 3
+        elif st[0] == "A":
+            res.append((None, toks[i + 1]))
+            i += 2
+        elif st[0] == "D":
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], None))
+            i += 2
+        else:
+            res.append((toks[i + 1], toks[i + 1]))
+            i += 2
+    return res
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Report:
+    base: str
+    head: str
+    changes: list[Change] = field(default_factory=list)
+    compat: list[tuple[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)
+    actions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+    files: int = 0
+
+    def to_json(self) -> dict:
+        return {"base": self.base, "head": self.head, "files_changed": self.files,
+                "surfaces_checked": list(SURFACES_CHECKED),
+                "changes": [c.to_json() for c in self.changes],
+                "compat": [{"check": a, "result": b} for a, b in self.compat],
+                "action_required": self.actions, "notes": self.notes}
+
+
+def collect(repo: str, base: str, head: str) -> Report:
+    b, h = Rev(repo, base), Rev(repo, head)
+    files = changed_files(repo, b.sha, h.sha)
+    rep = Report(b.sha, h.sha, files=len(files))
+    changes: list[Change] = []
+    head_gates = set(gx.feature_gates(h.gofile(FEATURES_FILE)).keys()) if h.read(FEATURES_FILE) else set()
+
+    log_b, log_h, wh_b, wh_h, cli_b, cli_h = [], [], [], [], [], []
+    gofiles_b: dict = {}
+    gofiles_h: dict = {}
+    metrics_touched = False
+    chart_touched = set()
+    crd_touched = set()
+
+    for bp, hp in files:
+        path = hp or bp
+        if path.startswith(CHART_CRD_PREFIX):
+            chart_touched.add(path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1])
+            continue
+        if path.startswith(CRD_PREFIX) and path.endswith(".yaml"):
+            bc, hc = crd.parse(b.read(bp)), crd.parse(h.read(hp))
+            if bc or hc:
+                crd_touched.add(path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1])
+                changes += crd.diff(path, bc, hc, head_gates)
+            continue
+        if not gx.is_go_source(path):
+            continue
+        bgf, hgf = b.gofile(bp), h.gofile(hp)
+        if bgf:
+            gofiles_b[bp] = bgf
+        if hgf:
+            gofiles_h[hp] = hgf
+        empty = GoFile(path, "")
+        bgf_, hgf_ = bgf or empty, hgf or empty
+        benv, bsent = b.package(posixpath.dirname(bp)) if bp else ({}, {})
+        henv, hsent = h.package(posixpath.dirname(hp)) if hp else ({}, {})
+
+        if path.startswith(("pkg/", "cmd/")):
+            log_b += gx.log_calls(bgf_)
+            log_h += gx.log_calls(hgf_)
+        if path.startswith("pkg/"):
+            changes += gx.diff_events(gx.events(bgf_, _env(b, benv)), gx.events(hgf_, _env(h, henv)))
+        if path.startswith(("pkg/", "apis/")):
+            web = gx.is_webhook_file(path)
+            wh_b += gx.messages(bgf_, bsent, field_calls=True, errors_too=web)
+            wh_h += gx.messages(hgf_, hsent, field_calls=True, errors_too=web)
+        if path.startswith(("pkg/", "apis/")):
+            changes += gx.diff_consts(gx.contract_consts(bgf_, benv), gx.contract_consts(hgf_, henv))
+        if path.startswith("pkg/metrics/"):
+            metrics_touched = True
+            changes += gx.diff_metrics(gx.metrics(bgf_, _env(b, benv)), gx.metrics(hgf_, _env(h, henv)))
+        if path.startswith("pkg/features/"):
+            changes += gx.diff_gates(gx.feature_gates(bgf_), gx.feature_gates(hgf_))
+        if gx.is_kueuectl(path):
+            cli_b += gx.messages(bgf_, bsent, field_calls=False, errors_too=True)
+            cli_h += gx.messages(hgf_, hsent, field_calls=False, errors_too=True)
+            changes += gx.diff_flags(gx.kueuectl_flags(bgf_), gx.kueuectl_flags(hgf_), path)
+            changes += gx.diff_columns(gx.kueuectl_columns(bgf_), gx.kueuectl_columns(hgf_), path)
+
+    changes += gx.diff_logs(log_b, log_h)
+    changes += gx.diff_messages("Webhook", wh_b, wh_h, gofiles_b, gofiles_h)
+    changes += gx.diff_messages("kueuectl", cli_b, cli_h, gofiles_b, gofiles_h)
+
+    if metrics_touched:
+        rep.notes += _metrics_doc_check(changes, h)
```

+            "columns": cols, "fields": fields,
+        }

+
+
+def _walk(schema: dict, path: str, required: bool, out: dict) -> None:

+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)

+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:

+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs

+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")

+"""Go-source contract surfaces: logs, events, validation/CLI messages, API
+constants, metrics, feature gates, kueuectl flags and columns."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from collections import Counter
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+from .gosrc import GoFile, literals_in, string_consts, string_value
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, API, TIGHTEN, Change, pair, short
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Rec:
+    path: str
+    line: int
+    func: str
+    kind: str
+    text: str
+    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+
+    @property
+    def where(self) -> str:
+        return f"{self.path}:{self.line}"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
+
+def is_go_source(path: str) -> bool:
+    return (path.endswith(".go") and not path.endswith("_test.go") and "zz_generated" not in path
+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)
+
+
+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("pkg/webhooks/") or "webhook" in path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs
+
+LOG_RE = re.compile(
+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")
+LOGGER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|\.)(log\w*|\w*logger\w*|l|lg|klog)$|LoggerFrom\(")
+
+
+def log_calls(gf: GoFile) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(LOG_RE):
+        recv = m.group(1)
+        last = re.sub(r"\(.*\)$", "", recv)
+        if not (LOGGER_NAME_RE.search(last) or "LoggerFrom(" in recv):
+            continue
+        method = m.group(4)
+        is_err = method.startswith("Error")
+        if is_err:
+            if len(args) < 2:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[1], args[2:]
+            level = "Error"
+        else:
+            if not args:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[0], args[1:]
+            if m.group(3) is not None:
+                level = f"V({m.group(3).strip()})"
+            else:
+                level = _alias_level(gf, last, m.start()) or "V(0)"
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr)
+        keys = []
+        for i in range(0, len(kv), 2):
+            k = kv[i]
+            if k.endswith("..."):
+                keys.append("…")
+                break
+            keys.append(string_value(k) or f"<{short(k, 20)}>")
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "log",
+                       msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 40)}>",
+                       {"level": level, "keys": tuple(keys)}))
+    return out
+
+
+_V_IN_RHS_RE = re.compile(r"\.V\(([^()]*)\)")
+
+
+def _alias_level(gf: GoFile, ident: str, pos: int) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve `logV := log.V(5)` style aliases assigned earlier in the same func.
+
+    Walks the ident's assignments backwards: an RHS with `.V(n)` gives the level,
+    a self-derived RHS (`logV = logV.WithValues(...)`) keeps looking, anything
+    else ends the search (plain logger, V(0)).
+    """
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*", ident):
+        return None
+    region = gf.src[gf.func_start(pos):pos]
+    assign = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])" + re.escape(ident) + r"\s*:?=\s*([^\n;]+)")
+    for m in reversed(list(assign.finditer(region))):
+        rhs = m.group(1)
+        if rhs.startswith("="):  # comparison, not assignment
+            continue
+        v = _V_IN_RHS_RE.search(rhs)
+        if v:
+            return f"V({v.group(1).strip()})"

+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+"""Go-source contract surfaces: logs, events, validation/CLI messages, API
+constants, metrics, feature gates, kueuectl flags and columns."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from collections import Counter
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+from .gosrc import GoFile, literals_in, string_consts, string_value
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, API, TIGHTEN, Change, pair, short
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Rec:
+    path: str
+    line: int
+    func: str
+    kind: str
+    text: str
+    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+
+    @property
+    def where(self) -> str:
+        return f"{self.path}:{self.line}"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
+
+def is_go_source(path: str) -> bool:
+    return (path.endswith(".go") and not path.endswith("_test.go") and "zz_generated" not in path
+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)
+
+
+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("pkg/webhooks/") or "webhook" in path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs
+
+LOG_RE = re.compile(
+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")
+LOGGER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|\.)(log\w*|\w*logger\w*|l|lg|klog)$|LoggerFrom\(")
+
+
+def log_calls(gf: GoFile) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(LOG_RE):
+        recv = m.group(1)
+        last = re.sub(r"\(.*\)$", "", recv)
+        if not (LOGGER_NAME_RE.search(last) or "LoggerFrom(" in recv):
+            continue
+        method = m.group(4)
+        is_err = method.startswith("Error")
+        if is_err:
+            if len(args) < 2:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[1], args[2:]
+            level = "Error"
+        else:
+            if not args:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[0], args[1:]
+            if m.group(3) is not None:
+                level = f"V({m.group(3).strip()})"
+            else:
+                level = _alias_level(gf, last, m.start()) or "V(0)"
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr)
+        keys = []
+        for i in range(0, len(kv), 2):
+            k = kv[i]
+            if k.endswith("..."):
+                keys.append("…")
+                break
+            keys.append(string_value(k) or f"<{short(k, 20)}>")
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "log",
+                       msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 40)}>",
+                       {"level": level, "keys": tuple(keys)}))
+    return out
+
+
+_V_IN_RHS_RE = re.compile(r"\.V\(([^()]*)\)")
+
+
+def _alias_level(gf: GoFile, ident: str, pos: int) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve `logV := log.V(5)` style aliases assigned earlier in the same func.
+
+    Walks the ident's assignments backwards: an RHS with `.V(n)` gives the level,
+    a self-derived RHS (`logV = logV.WithValues(...)`) keeps looking, anything
+    else ends the search (plain logger, V(0)).
+    """
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*", ident):
+        return None
+    region = gf.src[gf.func_start(pos):pos]
+    assign = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])" + re.escape(ident) + r"\s*:?=\s*([^\n;]+)")
+    for m in reversed(list(assign.finditer(region))):
+        rhs = m.group(1)
+        if rhs.startswith("="):  # comparison, not assignment
+            continue
+        v = _V_IN_RHS_RE.search(rhs)
+        if v:
+            return f"V({v.group(1).strip()})"
+        if re.match(re.escape(ident) + r"\b", rhs.strip()):
+            continue
+        return None
```
