  **Preserve call-site identity when pairing logs.**
  
  If two calls with the same message and keys swap V-levels, the first pairing pass matches each call to the other site. Both changes disappear, and the report can say no contract change was detected. Pair calls by their identity before discarding equal records.

+    return (path.endswith(".go") and not path.endswith("_test.go") and "zz_generated" not in path
+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)
+
+
+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("pkg/webhooks/") or "webhook" in path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs
+
+LOG_RE = re.compile(
+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")
+LOGGER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|\.)(log\w*|\w*logger\w*|l|lg|klog)$|LoggerFrom\(")
+
+
+def log_calls(gf: GoFile) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(LOG_RE):
+        recv = m.group(1)
+        last = re.sub(r"\(.*\)$", "", recv)
+        if not (LOGGER_NAME_RE.search(last) or "LoggerFrom(" in recv):
+            continue
+        method = m.group(4)
+        is_err = method.startswith("Error")
+        if is_err:
+            if len(args) < 2:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[1], args[2:]
+            level = "Error"
+        else:
+            if not args:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[0], args[1:]
+            if m.group(3) is not None:
+                level = f"V({m.group(3).strip()})"
+            else:
+                level = _alias_level(gf, last, m.start()) or "V(0)"
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr)
+        keys = []
+        for i in range(0, len(kv), 2):
+            k = kv[i]
+            if k.endswith("..."):
+                keys.append("…")
+                break
+            keys.append(string_value(k) or f"<{short(k, 20)}>")
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "log",
+                       msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 40)}>",
+                       {"level": level, "keys": tuple(keys)}))
+    return out
+
+
+_V_IN_RHS_RE = re.compile(r"\.V\(([^()]*)\)")
+
+
+def _alias_level(gf: GoFile, ident: str, pos: int) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve `logV := log.V(5)` style aliases assigned earlier in the same func.
+
+    Walks the ident's assignments backwards: an RHS with `.V(n)` gives the level,
+    a self-derived RHS (`logV = logV.WithValues(...)`) keeps looking, anything
+    else ends the search (plain logger, V(0)).
+    """
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*", ident):
+        return None
+    region = gf.src[gf.func_start(pos):pos]
+    assign = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])" + re.escape(ident) + r"\s*:?=\s*([^\n;]+)")
+    for m in reversed(list(assign.finditer(region))):
+        rhs = m.group(1)
+        if rhs.startswith("="):  # comparison, not assignment
+            continue
+        v = _V_IN_RHS_RE.search(rhs)
+        if v:
+            return f"V({v.group(1).strip()})"
+        if re.match(re.escape(ident) + r"\b", rhs.strip()):
+            continue
+        return None
+    return None
+
+
+def diff_logs(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["level"], r.attrs["keys"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.6)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.8)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        bk, hk = set(b.attrs["keys"]), set(h.attrs["keys"])
+        gone, new = sorted(bk - hk), sorted(hk - bk)
+        notes = []
+        if gone and new:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"log key renamed ({', '.join(gone)} → {', '.join(new)})"
+            notes.append("key renamed")
+        elif gone:
+            notes.append("key removed")
+        elif new:
+            notes.append("key added")
+        if b.attrs["level"] != h.attrs["level"]:
+            notes.insert(0, f"{b.attrs['level']}→{h.attrs['level']}")
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            notes.append("reworded")
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), _log_cell(b), _log_cell(h), h.where, "changed", fl,
+                          "; ".join(notes), group=h.path,
+                          meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(b.text, 50), _log_cell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, "removed",
+                          group=b.path, meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": ABSENT}))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), ABSENT, _log_cell(h), h.where, "added", {}, "added",
+                          group=h.path, meta={"level_before": ABSENT, "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _log_cell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    keys = ", ".join(r.attrs["keys"])
+    return f'{r.attrs["level"]} "{r.text}"' + (f" [{keys}]" if keys else "")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- events
+
+EVENT_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Eventf|Event|AnnotatedEventf)\(")
+EVENT_TYPE_RE = re.compile(r'^(?:[\w.]+\.)?EventType(Normal|Warning)$|^"(Normal|Warning)"$')
+
+
+def events(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(EVENT_RE):
+        t = next((i for i, a in enumerate(args) if EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(a)), None)
+        if t is None or t + 1 >= len(args):
+            continue
+        tm = EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(args[t])
+        etype = tm.group(1) or tm.group(2)
+        reason_expr = args[t + 1]
+        reason = string_value(reason_expr, env) or reason_expr
+        new_api = t == 2 and len(args) >= t + 4
+        msg_expr = args[t + 3] if new_api else (args[t + 2] if len(args) > t + 2 else "")
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr, env)
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "event", reason,
+                       {"type": etype, "msg": msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 30)}>"}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_events(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["type"], r.attrs["msg"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.attrs["msg"], 0.6)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"event reason {b.text} → {h.text}"
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", _ev(b), _ev(h), h.where, "changed", fl, group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {b.text}", _ev(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", ABSENT, _ev(h), h.where, "added", {}, group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+def _ev(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f'{r.attrs["type"]} {r.text}: "{r.attrs["msg"]}"'
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- messages
+
+FIELD_RE = re.compile(r"\bfield\.(Invalid|Forbidden|Required|NotSupported|Duplicate|TooMany|TooLong|"
+                      r"TooLongMaxLength|NotFound)\(")
+ERR_RE = re.compile(r"\b(errors\.New|fmt\.Errorf)\(")
+VERB_RE = re.compile(r"%(?:%|[-+# 0]*(?:\*|\d+)?(?:\.(?:\*|\d+))?[a-zA-Z])")
+FIELD_MSG_ARG = {"Invalid": 2, "Forbidden": 1, "Required": 1, "NotSupported": 2}
+
+
+def messages(gf: GoFile, sentinels: dict[str, str], *, field_calls: bool, errors_too: bool) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    consumed: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()
+    if field_calls:
+        for m, args, _ in gf.calls(FIELD_RE):
+            fn = m.group(1)
+            k = FIELD_MSG_ARG.get(fn)
+            if k is None or len(args) <= k:
+                continue
+            if fn == "NotSupported":
+                vals = literals_in(args[k])
+                if not vals:
+                    continue
+                text = "supported: " + ", ".join(vals)
+            else:
+                text = string_value(args[k])
+                if text is None:
+                    continue
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), f"field.{fn}", text))
+    if errors_too:
+        for m, args, end in gf.calls(ERR_RE):
+            func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+            if not func or not args:
+                continue  # package-level sentinels are reported via their uses
+            text = string_value(args[0])
+            if text is None:
+                continue
+            if m.group(1) == "fmt.Errorf" and "%w" in text:
+                verbs = [v for v in VERB_RE.findall(text) if v != "%%"]
+                for idx, v in enumerate(verbs):
+                    if v == "%w" and 1 + idx < len(args) and args[1 + idx] in sentinels:
+                        text = text.replace("%w", sentinels[args[1 + idx]], 1)
+                        consumed.add((gf.line(m.start()), args[1 + idx]))
+                        break
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), func, m.group(1), text))
+        if sentinels:
+            names = "|".join(map(re.escape, sorted(sentinels, key=len, reverse=True)))
+            for m in re.finditer(rf"^[ \t]*return\b[^\n]*\b({names})\b", gf.src, re.M):
+                func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+                if not func:
+                    continue
+                ln = gf.line(m.start())
+                if (ln, m.group(1)) in consumed:
+                    continue
+                out.append(Rec(gf.path, ln, func, "sentinel", sentinels[m.group(1)], {"var": m.group(1)}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_messages(surface: str, base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec],
+                  base_files: dict, head_files: dict) -> list[Change]:
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.45)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.75)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+
+    def logic_note(func: str, bpath: str | None, hpath: str | None) -> str:
+        bf = base_files.get(bpath) if bpath else None
+        hf = head_files.get(hpath) if hpath else None
+        if not func:
+            return ""
+        bs = bf.logic_signature(func) if bf else None
+        hs = hf.logic_signature(func) if hf else None
+        if bs is None and hs is not None:
+            return f"new func `{func}`"
+        if bs is not None and hs is not None and bs != hs:
+            return f"logic in `{func}` also changed"
+        return ""
+
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if b.text == h.text:
+            continue
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", b.text, h.text, h.where, "changed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {},
+                          logic_note(h.func, b.path, h.path), group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{b.kind} in `{b.func or '?'}`", b.text, ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {}, logic_note(b.func, b.path, None),
+                          group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        fl = {API: 1, TIGHTEN: f"new validation in `{h.func}`"} if surface == "Webhook" else {}
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", ABSENT, h.text, h.where, "added", fl,
+                          logic_note(h.func, None, h.path), group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- constants
+
+CONTRACT_CONST_RE = re.compile(r"(Reason|Label|Annotation|Condition|Finalizer)")
+
+
+def contract_consts(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    """Exported string constants that are part of the user contract."""
+    api = gf.path.startswith("apis/")
+    events_file = gf.path.endswith("/events.go")
+    out = {}
+    for name, value, line in string_consts(gf, env):
+        if not name[:1].isupper():
+            continue
+        if not (api or events_file or CONTRACT_CONST_RE.search(name)):
+            continue
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, line, "", _const_kind(name, value), value)
+    return out
+
+
+def _const_kind(name: str, value: str) -> str:
+    if re.search(r"Label|Annotation|Finalizer", name) or ("/" in value and "." in value.split("/")[0]):
+        return "key"
+    if "Reason" in name:
+        return "reason"
+    if "Condition" in name:
+        return "condition"
+    return "const"
+
+
+def diff_consts(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    gone = {n: r for n, r in base.items() if n not in head}
+    new = {n: r for n, r in head.items() if n not in base}
+    by_val_new = {}
+    for n, r in new.items():
+        by_val_new.setdefault(r.text, []).append(n)
+    for n, r in sorted(gone.items()):
+        if by_val_new.get(r.text):
+            n2 = by_val_new[r.text].pop(0)
+            new.pop(n2)
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}` → `{n2}`", f'{n} = "{r.text}"', f'{n2} = "{r.text}"',
+                              head[n2].where, "changed", {API: 1} if r.path.startswith("apis/") else {},
+                              "Go identifier renamed; value unchanged"))
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if r.kind == "key":
+            fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(r.kind)} `{r.text}` removed"
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{r.text}"', ABSENT, r.where, "removed", fl,
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n, r in sorted(new.items()):
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, f'"{r.text}"', r.where, "added", {},
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n in sorted(set(base) & set(head)):
+        b, h = base[n], head[n]
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl = {}
+            if h.kind in ("key", "reason", "condition"):
+                fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(h.kind)} `{b.text}` renamed to `{h.text}`"
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{b.text}"', f'"{h.text}"', h.where, "changed", fl,
+                              _kind_word(h.kind)))
+    return out
+
+
+def _kind_word(k: str) -> str:
+    return {"key": "label/annotation key", "reason": "reason", "condition": "condition type"}.get(k, "constant")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- metrics
+
+METRIC_RE = re.compile(r"\bprometheus\.New(Counter|Gauge|Histogram|Summary)(Vec)?\(")
+
+
+def metrics(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    out = {}
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(METRIC_RE):
+        if not args:
+            continue
+        opts = _composite(args[0])
+        name = string_value(opts.get("Name", ""), env)
+        if not name:
+            continue
+        ns = string_value(opts.get("Namespace", '""'), env) or ""
+        sub = string_value(opts.get("Subsystem", '""'), env) or ""
+        full = "_".join(x for x in (ns, sub, name) if x)
+        labels: list[str] = []
+        dynamic = False
+        if m.group(2) and len(args) > 1:
+            labels = literals_in(args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r'"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`', "", args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r"\[\]string|[{},\s]", "", rest)
+            dynamic = bool(rest)
+        help_text = " ".join((string_value(opts.get("Help", '""'), env) or "").split())
+        out[full] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), m.group(1).lower(), full,
+                        {"labels": labels, "dynamic": dynamic, "help": help_text,
```

+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+from .gosrc import GoFile, literals_in, string_consts, string_value
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, API, TIGHTEN, Change, pair, short
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Rec:
+    path: str
+    line: int
+    func: str
+    kind: str
+    text: str
+    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+
+    @property
+    def where(self) -> str:
+        return f"{self.path}:{self.line}"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
+
+def is_go_source(path: str) -> bool:
+    return (path.endswith(".go") and not path.endswith("_test.go") and "zz_generated" not in path
+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)
+
+
+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("pkg/webhooks/") or "webhook" in path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs
+
+LOG_RE = re.compile(
+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")
+LOGGER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|\.)(log\w*|\w*logger\w*|l|lg|klog)$|LoggerFrom\(")
+
+
+def log_calls(gf: GoFile) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(LOG_RE):
+        recv = m.group(1)
+        last = re.sub(r"\(.*\)$", "", recv)
+        if not (LOGGER_NAME_RE.search(last) or "LoggerFrom(" in recv):
+            continue
+        method = m.group(4)
+        is_err = method.startswith("Error")
+        if is_err:
+            if len(args) < 2:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[1], args[2:]
+            level = "Error"
+        else:
+            if not args:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[0], args[1:]
+            if m.group(3) is not None:
+                level = f"V({m.group(3).strip()})"
+            else:
+                level = _alias_level(gf, last, m.start()) or "V(0)"
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr)
+        keys = []
+        for i in range(0, len(kv), 2):
+            k = kv[i]
+            if k.endswith("..."):
+                keys.append("…")
+                break
+            keys.append(string_value(k) or f"<{short(k, 20)}>")
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "log",
+                       msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 40)}>",
+                       {"level": level, "keys": tuple(keys)}))
+    return out
+
+
+_V_IN_RHS_RE = re.compile(r"\.V\(([^()]*)\)")
+
+
+def _alias_level(gf: GoFile, ident: str, pos: int) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve `logV := log.V(5)` style aliases assigned earlier in the same func.
+
+    Walks the ident's assignments backwards: an RHS with `.V(n)` gives the level,
+    a self-derived RHS (`logV = logV.WithValues(...)`) keeps looking, anything
+    else ends the search (plain logger, V(0)).
+    """
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*", ident):
+        return None
+    region = gf.src[gf.func_start(pos):pos]
+    assign = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])" + re.escape(ident) + r"\s*:?=\s*([^\n;]+)")
+    for m in reversed(list(assign.finditer(region))):
+        rhs = m.group(1)
+        if rhs.startswith("="):  # comparison, not assignment
+            continue
+        v = _V_IN_RHS_RE.search(rhs)
+        if v:
+            return f"V({v.group(1).strip()})"
+        if re.match(re.escape(ident) + r"\b", rhs.strip()):
+            continue
+        return None
+    return None
+
+
+def diff_logs(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["level"], r.attrs["keys"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.6)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.8)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        bk, hk = set(b.attrs["keys"]), set(h.attrs["keys"])
+        gone, new = sorted(bk - hk), sorted(hk - bk)
+        notes = []
+        if gone and new:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"log key renamed ({', '.join(gone)} → {', '.join(new)})"
+            notes.append("key renamed")
+        elif gone:
+            notes.append("key removed")
+        elif new:
+            notes.append("key added")
+        if b.attrs["level"] != h.attrs["level"]:
+            notes.insert(0, f"{b.attrs['level']}→{h.attrs['level']}")
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            notes.append("reworded")
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), _log_cell(b), _log_cell(h), h.where, "changed", fl,
+                          "; ".join(notes), group=h.path,
+                          meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(b.text, 50), _log_cell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, "removed",
+                          group=b.path, meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": ABSENT}))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), ABSENT, _log_cell(h), h.where, "added", {}, "added",
+                          group=h.path, meta={"level_before": ABSENT, "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _log_cell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    keys = ", ".join(r.attrs["keys"])
+    return f'{r.attrs["level"]} "{r.text}"' + (f" [{keys}]" if keys else "")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- events
+
+EVENT_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Eventf|Event|AnnotatedEventf)\(")
+EVENT_TYPE_RE = re.compile(r'^(?:[\w.]+\.)?EventType(Normal|Warning)$|^"(Normal|Warning)"$')
+
+
+def events(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(EVENT_RE):
+        t = next((i for i, a in enumerate(args) if EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(a)), None)
+        if t is None or t + 1 >= len(args):
+            continue
+        tm = EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(args[t])
+        etype = tm.group(1) or tm.group(2)
+        reason_expr = args[t + 1]
+        reason = string_value(reason_expr, env) or reason_expr
+        new_api = t == 2 and len(args) >= t + 4
+        msg_expr = args[t + 3] if new_api else (args[t + 2] if len(args) > t + 2 else "")
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr, env)
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "event", reason,
+                       {"type": etype, "msg": msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 30)}>"}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_events(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["type"], r.attrs["msg"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.attrs["msg"], 0.6)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"event reason {b.text} → {h.text}"
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", _ev(b), _ev(h), h.where, "changed", fl, group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {b.text}", _ev(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", ABSENT, _ev(h), h.where, "added", {}, group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+def _ev(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f'{r.attrs["type"]} {r.text}: "{r.attrs["msg"]}"'
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- messages
+
+FIELD_RE = re.compile(r"\bfield\.(Invalid|Forbidden|Required|NotSupported|Duplicate|TooMany|TooLong|"
+                      r"TooLongMaxLength|NotFound)\(")
+ERR_RE = re.compile(r"\b(errors\.New|fmt\.Errorf)\(")
+VERB_RE = re.compile(r"%(?:%|[-+# 0]*(?:\*|\d+)?(?:\.(?:\*|\d+))?[a-zA-Z])")
+FIELD_MSG_ARG = {"Invalid": 2, "Forbidden": 1, "Required": 1, "NotSupported": 2}
+
+
+def messages(gf: GoFile, sentinels: dict[str, str], *, field_calls: bool, errors_too: bool) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    consumed: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()
+    if field_calls:
+        for m, args, _ in gf.calls(FIELD_RE):
+            fn = m.group(1)
+            k = FIELD_MSG_ARG.get(fn)
+            if k is None or len(args) <= k:
+                continue
+            if fn == "NotSupported":
+                vals = literals_in(args[k])
+                if not vals:
+                    continue
+                text = "supported: " + ", ".join(vals)
+            else:
+                text = string_value(args[k])
+                if text is None:
+                    continue
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), f"field.{fn}", text))
+    if errors_too:
+        for m, args, end in gf.calls(ERR_RE):
+            func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+            if not func or not args:
+                continue  # package-level sentinels are reported via their uses
+            text = string_value(args[0])
+            if text is None:
+                continue
+            if m.group(1) == "fmt.Errorf" and "%w" in text:
+                verbs = [v for v in VERB_RE.findall(text) if v != "%%"]
+                for idx, v in enumerate(verbs):
+                    if v == "%w" and 1 + idx < len(args) and args[1 + idx] in sentinels:
+                        text = text.replace("%w", sentinels[args[1 + idx]], 1)
+                        consumed.add((gf.line(m.start()), args[1 + idx]))
+                        break
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), func, m.group(1), text))
+        if sentinels:
+            names = "|".join(map(re.escape, sorted(sentinels, key=len, reverse=True)))
+            for m in re.finditer(rf"^[ \t]*return\b[^\n]*\b({names})\b", gf.src, re.M):
+                func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+                if not func:
+                    continue
+                ln = gf.line(m.start())
+                if (ln, m.group(1)) in consumed:
+                    continue
+                out.append(Rec(gf.path, ln, func, "sentinel", sentinels[m.group(1)], {"var": m.group(1)}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_messages(surface: str, base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec],
+                  base_files: dict, head_files: dict) -> list[Change]:
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.45)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.75)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+
+    def logic_note(func: str, bpath: str | None, hpath: str | None) -> str:
+        bf = base_files.get(bpath) if bpath else None
+        hf = head_files.get(hpath) if hpath else None
+        if not func:
+            return ""
+        bs = bf.logic_signature(func) if bf else None
+        hs = hf.logic_signature(func) if hf else None
+        if bs is None and hs is not None:
+            return f"new func `{func}`"
+        if bs is not None and hs is not None and bs != hs:
+            return f"logic in `{func}` also changed"
+        return ""
+
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if b.text == h.text:
+            continue
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", b.text, h.text, h.where, "changed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {},
+                          logic_note(h.func, b.path, h.path), group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{b.kind} in `{b.func or '?'}`", b.text, ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {}, logic_note(b.func, b.path, None),
+                          group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        fl = {API: 1, TIGHTEN: f"new validation in `{h.func}`"} if surface == "Webhook" else {}
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", ABSENT, h.text, h.where, "added", fl,
+                          logic_note(h.func, None, h.path), group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- constants
+
+CONTRACT_CONST_RE = re.compile(r"(Reason|Label|Annotation|Condition|Finalizer)")
+
+
+def contract_consts(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    """Exported string constants that are part of the user contract."""
+    api = gf.path.startswith("apis/")
+    events_file = gf.path.endswith("/events.go")
+    out = {}
+    for name, value, line in string_consts(gf, env):
+        if not name[:1].isupper():
+            continue
+        if not (api or events_file or CONTRACT_CONST_RE.search(name)):
+            continue
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, line, "", _const_kind(name, value), value)
+    return out
+
+
+def _const_kind(name: str, value: str) -> str:
+    if re.search(r"Label|Annotation|Finalizer", name) or ("/" in value and "." in value.split("/")[0]):
+        return "key"
+    if "Reason" in name:
+        return "reason"
+    if "Condition" in name:
+        return "condition"
+    return "const"
+
+
+def diff_consts(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    gone = {n: r for n, r in base.items() if n not in head}
+    new = {n: r for n, r in head.items() if n not in base}
+    by_val_new = {}
+    for n, r in new.items():
+        by_val_new.setdefault(r.text, []).append(n)
+    for n, r in sorted(gone.items()):
+        if by_val_new.get(r.text):
+            n2 = by_val_new[r.text].pop(0)
+            new.pop(n2)
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}` → `{n2}`", f'{n} = "{r.text}"', f'{n2} = "{r.text}"',
+                              head[n2].where, "changed", {API: 1} if r.path.startswith("apis/") else {},
+                              "Go identifier renamed; value unchanged"))
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if r.kind == "key":
+            fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(r.kind)} `{r.text}` removed"
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{r.text}"', ABSENT, r.where, "removed", fl,
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n, r in sorted(new.items()):
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, f'"{r.text}"', r.where, "added", {},
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n in sorted(set(base) & set(head)):
+        b, h = base[n], head[n]
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl = {}
+            if h.kind in ("key", "reason", "condition"):
+                fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(h.kind)} `{b.text}` renamed to `{h.text}`"
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{b.text}"', f'"{h.text}"', h.where, "changed", fl,
+                              _kind_word(h.kind)))
+    return out
+
+
+def _kind_word(k: str) -> str:
+    return {"key": "label/annotation key", "reason": "reason", "condition": "condition type"}.get(k, "constant")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- metrics
+
+METRIC_RE = re.compile(r"\bprometheus\.New(Counter|Gauge|Histogram|Summary)(Vec)?\(")
+
+
+def metrics(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    out = {}
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(METRIC_RE):
+        if not args:
+            continue
+        opts = _composite(args[0])
+        name = string_value(opts.get("Name", ""), env)
+        if not name:
+            continue
+        ns = string_value(opts.get("Namespace", '""'), env) or ""
+        sub = string_value(opts.get("Subsystem", '""'), env) or ""
+        full = "_".join(x for x in (ns, sub, name) if x)
+        labels: list[str] = []
+        dynamic = False
+        if m.group(2) and len(args) > 1:
+            labels = literals_in(args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r'"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`', "", args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r"\[\]string|[{},\s]", "", rest)
+            dynamic = bool(rest)
+        help_text = " ".join((string_value(opts.get("Help", '""'), env) or "").split())
+        out[full] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), m.group(1).lower(), full,
+                        {"labels": labels, "dynamic": dynamic, "help": help_text,
+                         "buckets": " ".join(opts.get("Buckets", "").split())})
+    return out
+
+
+def _composite(expr: str) -> dict[str, str]:
+    from .gosrc import composite_fields
+    return composite_fields(expr)
+
+
+def diff_metrics(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", _mcell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` removed"}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, _mcell(h), h.where, "added", {}))
+            continue
+        if b.kind != h.kind:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` type", b.kind, h.kind, h.where, "changed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` type changed"}))
+        bl, hl = b.attrs["labels"], h.attrs["labels"]
+        if bl != hl or b.attrs["dynamic"] != h.attrs["dynamic"]:
+            gone = [x for x in bl if x not in hl]
+            fl = {}
+            if gone:
+                new = [x for x in hl if x not in bl]
+                what = "renamed" if new else "removed"
+                fl[ACTION] = f"metric `{n}` label {what} ({', '.join(gone)}" + (
+                    f" → {', '.join(new)})" if new else ")")
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` labels", _labels(b), _labels(h), h.where, "changed", fl))
+        if b.attrs["help"] != h.attrs["help"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` help", b.attrs["help"], h.attrs["help"], h.where, "changed", {}))
+        if b.attrs["buckets"] != h.attrs["buckets"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` buckets", b.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT,
+                              h.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT, h.where, "changed", {}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _labels(r: Rec) -> str:
+    s = ", ".join(r.attrs["labels"]) or "(none)"
+    return s + (" + dynamic" if r.attrs["dynamic"] else "")
+
+
+def _mcell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f"{r.kind} [{_labels(r)}]"
+
+
+DOC_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\|\s*`([a-z0-9_:]+)`\s*\|\s*([^|]*)\|(.*)\|\s*(.*?)\s*\|\s*$", re.M)
+
+
+def metrics_doc(text: str | None) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
+    out = {}
+    for m in DOC_ROW_RE.finditer(text or ""):
+        labels = re.findall(r"(?:^|<br>)\s*`([a-z0-9_]+)`", m.group(4))
+        out[m.group(1)] = labels
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- feature gates
+
+SPEC_RE = re.compile(r'\{\s*Version:\s*version\.MustParse\("([^"]+)"\)\s*,\s*Default:\s*(true|false)\s*,'
+                     r'\s*PreRelease:\s*(?:featuregate\.)?(\w+)(?:\s*,\s*LockToDefault:\s*(true|false))?\s*\}')
+
+
+def feature_gates(gf: GoFile) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    m = re.search(r"defaultVersionedFeatureGates\s*=\s*map\[[^\]]+\][\w.]+\{", gf.src)
+    if not m:
+        return {}
+    items, _ = _group_from(gf.src, m.end() - 1)
+    out = {}
+    for it in items:
+        km = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_][\w.]*)\s*:\s*(\{.*\})\s*\Z", it, re.S)
+        if not km:
+            continue
+        specs = [(v, d == "true", pre, lock == "true") for v, d, pre, lock in SPEC_RE.findall(km.group(2))]
+        if not specs:
+            continue
+        name = km.group(1).rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
+        pos = m.end() + gf.src[m.end():].find(km.group(1) + ":")
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(pos), "", "gate", name, {"specs": specs})
+    return out
+
+
+def _group_from(src: str, i: int):
+    from .gosrc import group_args
+    return group_args(src, i)
+
+
+def gate_cell(specs) -> str:
+    v, d, pre, lock = specs[-1]
+    return f"{pre} {'on' if d else 'off'} @v{v}" + (" (locked)" if lock else "")
+
+
+def diff_gates(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", gate_cell(b.attrs["specs"]), "removed", b.where,
+                              "removed", {API: 1, ACTION: f"feature gate `{n}` removed"} if not
+                              b.attrs["specs"][-1][3] else {API: 1}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, gate_cell(h.attrs["specs"]), h.where,
+                              "added", {API: 1}, meta={"gate": n, "specs": h.attrs["specs"]}))
+            continue
+        bs, hs = b.attrs["specs"][-1], h.attrs["specs"][-1]
+        if (bs[1], bs[2], bs[3]) == (hs[1], hs[2], hs[3]) and b.attrs["specs"] == h.attrs["specs"]:
+            continue
+        if (bs[1], bs[2], bs[3]) == (hs[1], hs[2], hs[3]):
+            continue  # only historical entries changed
+        fl = {API: 1}
+        if bs[1] != hs[1]:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"feature gate `{n}` default {'on' if hs[1] else 'off'}"
+        out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", gate_cell(b.attrs["specs"]), gate_cell(h.attrs["specs"]),
+                          h.where, "changed", fl, meta={"gate": n, "specs": h.attrs["specs"],
+                                                        "default_before": bs[1], "default_after": hs[1],
+                                                        "locked_now": bool(hs[3] and not bs[3])}))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- kueuectl
+
+FLAG_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Flags|PersistentFlags)\(\)\.([A-Z]\w*)\(")
+COLS_RE = re.compile(r"\[\]metav1\.TableColumnDefinition\{")
+
+
+def kueuectl_flags(gf: GoFile) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    out = {}
+    marks = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(FLAG_RE):
+        meth = m.group(2)
+        if meth.startswith("Mark"):
+            if args:
+                marks.append((string_value(args[0]), meth, string_value(args[1]) if len(args) > 1 else ""))
+            continue
+        has_var = "Var" in meth
+        a = args[1:] if has_var else args
+        if not a:
+            continue
+        name = string_value(a[0])
```

  **Extract only flag registration calls.**
  
  A later getter overwrites the declaration record. For example, a change to the `--status` default can disappear from the report. Lookup-only calls can also appear as added flags. Restrict extraction to registration methods and add a regression test for a declaration followed by a getter.

@@ -0,0 +1,648 @@
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");

+"""Go-source contract surfaces: logs, events, validation/CLI messages, API
+constants, metrics, feature gates, kueuectl flags and columns."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from collections import Counter
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+
+from .gosrc import GoFile, literals_in, string_consts, string_value
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, API, TIGHTEN, Change, pair, short
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Rec:
+    path: str
+    line: int
+    func: str
+    kind: str
+    text: str
+    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
+
+    @property
+    def where(self) -> str:
+        return f"{self.path}:{self.line}"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
+
+def is_go_source(path: str) -> bool:
+    return (path.endswith(".go") and not path.endswith("_test.go") and "zz_generated" not in path
+            and not path.startswith(("test/", "vendor/", "hack/", "site/", "client-go/"))
+            and "/testing" not in path and "/testingjobs/" not in path and "/test/" not in path)
+
+
+def is_webhook_file(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("pkg/webhooks/") or "webhook" in path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+
+
+def is_kueuectl(path: str) -> bool:
+    return path.startswith("cmd/kueuectl/")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- logs
+
+LOG_RE = re.compile(
+    r"(?<![\w.])((?:[A-Za-z_]\w*\.)*?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\([^()]*\))?)"
+    r"((?:\.(?:WithValues|WithName)\([^()]*\))*)(?:\.V\(([^()]*)\))?\.(Info|Error|InfoS|ErrorS)\(")
+LOGGER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|\.)(log\w*|\w*logger\w*|l|lg|klog)$|LoggerFrom\(")
+
+
+def log_calls(gf: GoFile) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(LOG_RE):
+        recv = m.group(1)
+        last = re.sub(r"\(.*\)$", "", recv)
+        if not (LOGGER_NAME_RE.search(last) or "LoggerFrom(" in recv):
+            continue
+        method = m.group(4)
+        is_err = method.startswith("Error")
+        if is_err:
+            if len(args) < 2:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[1], args[2:]
+            level = "Error"
+        else:
+            if not args:
+                continue
+            msg_expr, kv = args[0], args[1:]
+            if m.group(3) is not None:
+                level = f"V({m.group(3).strip()})"
+            else:
+                level = _alias_level(gf, last, m.start()) or "V(0)"
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr)
+        keys = []
+        for i in range(0, len(kv), 2):
+            k = kv[i]
+            if k.endswith("..."):
+                keys.append("…")
+                break
+            keys.append(string_value(k) or f"<{short(k, 20)}>")
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "log",
+                       msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 40)}>",
+                       {"level": level, "keys": tuple(keys)}))
+    return out
+
+
+_V_IN_RHS_RE = re.compile(r"\.V\(([^()]*)\)")
+
+
+def _alias_level(gf: GoFile, ident: str, pos: int) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve `logV := log.V(5)` style aliases assigned earlier in the same func.
+
+    Walks the ident's assignments backwards: an RHS with `.V(n)` gives the level,
+    a self-derived RHS (`logV = logV.WithValues(...)`) keeps looking, anything
+    else ends the search (plain logger, V(0)).
+    """
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*", ident):
+        return None
+    region = gf.src[gf.func_start(pos):pos]
+    assign = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])" + re.escape(ident) + r"\s*:?=\s*([^\n;]+)")
+    for m in reversed(list(assign.finditer(region))):
+        rhs = m.group(1)
+        if rhs.startswith("="):  # comparison, not assignment
+            continue
+        v = _V_IN_RHS_RE.search(rhs)
+        if v:
+            return f"V({v.group(1).strip()})"
+        if re.match(re.escape(ident) + r"\b", rhs.strip()):
+            continue
+        return None
+    return None
+
+
+def diff_logs(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["level"], r.attrs["keys"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.6)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.8)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        bk, hk = set(b.attrs["keys"]), set(h.attrs["keys"])
+        gone, new = sorted(bk - hk), sorted(hk - bk)
+        notes = []
+        if gone and new:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"log key renamed ({', '.join(gone)} → {', '.join(new)})"
+            notes.append("key renamed")
+        elif gone:
+            notes.append("key removed")
+        elif new:
+            notes.append("key added")
+        if b.attrs["level"] != h.attrs["level"]:
+            notes.insert(0, f"{b.attrs['level']}→{h.attrs['level']}")
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            notes.append("reworded")
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), _log_cell(b), _log_cell(h), h.where, "changed", fl,
+                          "; ".join(notes), group=h.path,
+                          meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(b.text, 50), _log_cell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, "removed",
+                          group=b.path, meta={"level_before": b.attrs["level"], "level_after": ABSENT}))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Log", short(h.text, 50), ABSENT, _log_cell(h), h.where, "added", {}, "added",
+                          group=h.path, meta={"level_before": ABSENT, "level_after": h.attrs["level"]}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _log_cell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    keys = ", ".join(r.attrs["keys"])
+    return f'{r.attrs["level"]} "{r.text}"' + (f" [{keys}]" if keys else "")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- events
+
+EVENT_RE = re.compile(r"\.(Eventf|Event|AnnotatedEventf)\(")
+EVENT_TYPE_RE = re.compile(r'^(?:[\w.]+\.)?EventType(Normal|Warning)$|^"(Normal|Warning)"$')
+
+
+def events(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(EVENT_RE):
+        t = next((i for i, a in enumerate(args) if EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(a)), None)
+        if t is None or t + 1 >= len(args):
+            continue
+        tm = EVENT_TYPE_RE.match(args[t])
+        etype = tm.group(1) or tm.group(2)
+        reason_expr = args[t + 1]
+        reason = string_value(reason_expr, env) or reason_expr
+        new_api = t == 2 and len(args) >= t + 4
+        msg_expr = args[t + 3] if new_api else (args[t + 2] if len(args) > t + 2 else "")
+        msg = string_value(msg_expr, env)
+        out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), "event", reason,
+                       {"type": etype, "msg": msg if msg is not None else f"<{short(msg_expr, 30)}>"}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_events(base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    def full(r):
+        return (r.text, r.attrs["type"], r.attrs["msg"])
+
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (full, None),
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.attrs["msg"], 0.6)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if full(b) == full(h):
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl[ACTION] = f"event reason {b.text} → {h.text}"
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", _ev(b), _ev(h), h.where, "changed", fl, group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {b.text}", _ev(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed", {}, group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        out.append(Change("Event", f"reason {h.text}", ABSENT, _ev(h), h.where, "added", {}, group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+def _ev(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f'{r.attrs["type"]} {r.text}: "{r.attrs["msg"]}"'
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- messages
+
+FIELD_RE = re.compile(r"\bfield\.(Invalid|Forbidden|Required|NotSupported|Duplicate|TooMany|TooLong|"
+                      r"TooLongMaxLength|NotFound)\(")
+ERR_RE = re.compile(r"\b(errors\.New|fmt\.Errorf)\(")
+VERB_RE = re.compile(r"%(?:%|[-+# 0]*(?:\*|\d+)?(?:\.(?:\*|\d+))?[a-zA-Z])")
+FIELD_MSG_ARG = {"Invalid": 2, "Forbidden": 1, "Required": 1, "NotSupported": 2}
+
+
+def messages(gf: GoFile, sentinels: dict[str, str], *, field_calls: bool, errors_too: bool) -> list[Rec]:
+    out = []
+    consumed: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()
+    if field_calls:
+        for m, args, _ in gf.calls(FIELD_RE):
+            fn = m.group(1)
+            k = FIELD_MSG_ARG.get(fn)
+            if k is None or len(args) <= k:
+                continue
+            if fn == "NotSupported":
+                vals = literals_in(args[k])
+                if not vals:
+                    continue
+                text = "supported: " + ", ".join(vals)
+            else:
+                text = string_value(args[k])
+                if text is None:
+                    continue
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), f"field.{fn}", text))
+    if errors_too:
+        for m, args, end in gf.calls(ERR_RE):
+            func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+            if not func or not args:
+                continue  # package-level sentinels are reported via their uses
+            text = string_value(args[0])
+            if text is None:
+                continue
+            if m.group(1) == "fmt.Errorf" and "%w" in text:
+                verbs = [v for v in VERB_RE.findall(text) if v != "%%"]
+                for idx, v in enumerate(verbs):
+                    if v == "%w" and 1 + idx < len(args) and args[1 + idx] in sentinels:
+                        text = text.replace("%w", sentinels[args[1 + idx]], 1)
+                        consumed.add((gf.line(m.start()), args[1 + idx]))
+                        break
+            out.append(Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), func, m.group(1), text))
+        if sentinels:
+            names = "|".join(map(re.escape, sorted(sentinels, key=len, reverse=True)))
+            for m in re.finditer(rf"^[ \t]*return\b[^\n]*\b({names})\b", gf.src, re.M):
+                func = gf.func_at(m.start())
+                if not func:
+                    continue
+                ln = gf.line(m.start())
+                if (ln, m.group(1)) in consumed:
+                    continue
+                out.append(Rec(gf.path, ln, func, "sentinel", sentinels[m.group(1)], {"var": m.group(1)}))
+    return out
+
+
+def diff_messages(surface: str, base: list[Rec], head: list[Rec],
+                  base_files: dict, head_files: dict) -> list[Change]:
+    pairs, removed, added = pair(base, head, [
+        (lambda r: (r.func, r.text), None),
+        (lambda r: r.text, None),
+        (lambda r: r.func, (lambda r: r.text, 0.45)),
+        (lambda r: r.path, (lambda r: r.text, 0.75)),
+    ])
+    out = []
+
+    def logic_note(func: str, bpath: str | None, hpath: str | None) -> str:
+        bf = base_files.get(bpath) if bpath else None
+        hf = head_files.get(hpath) if hpath else None
+        if not func:
+            return ""
+        bs = bf.logic_signature(func) if bf else None
+        hs = hf.logic_signature(func) if hf else None
+        if bs is None and hs is not None:
+            return f"new func `{func}`"
+        if bs is not None and hs is not None and bs != hs:
+            return f"logic in `{func}` also changed"
+        return ""
+
+    for b, h in pairs:
+        if b.text == h.text:
+            continue
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", b.text, h.text, h.where, "changed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {},
+                          logic_note(h.func, b.path, h.path), group=h.path))
+    for b in removed:
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{b.kind} in `{b.func or '?'}`", b.text, ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                          {API: 1} if surface == "Webhook" else {}, logic_note(b.func, b.path, None),
+                          group=b.path))
+    for h in added:
+        fl = {API: 1, TIGHTEN: f"new validation in `{h.func}`"} if surface == "Webhook" else {}
+        out.append(Change(surface, f"{h.kind} in `{h.func or '?'}`", ABSENT, h.text, h.where, "added", fl,
+                          logic_note(h.func, None, h.path), group=h.path))
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- constants
+
+CONTRACT_CONST_RE = re.compile(r"(Reason|Label|Annotation|Condition|Finalizer)")
+
+
+def contract_consts(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    """Exported string constants that are part of the user contract."""
+    api = gf.path.startswith("apis/")
+    events_file = gf.path.endswith("/events.go")
+    out = {}
+    for name, value, line in string_consts(gf, env):
+        if not name[:1].isupper():
+            continue
+        if not (api or events_file or CONTRACT_CONST_RE.search(name)):
+            continue
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, line, "", _const_kind(name, value), value)
+    return out
+
+
+def _const_kind(name: str, value: str) -> str:
+    if re.search(r"Label|Annotation|Finalizer", name) or ("/" in value and "." in value.split("/")[0]):
+        return "key"
+    if "Reason" in name:
+        return "reason"
+    if "Condition" in name:
+        return "condition"
+    return "const"
+
+
+def diff_consts(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    gone = {n: r for n, r in base.items() if n not in head}
+    new = {n: r for n, r in head.items() if n not in base}
+    by_val_new = {}
+    for n, r in new.items():
+        by_val_new.setdefault(r.text, []).append(n)
+    for n, r in sorted(gone.items()):
+        if by_val_new.get(r.text):
+            n2 = by_val_new[r.text].pop(0)
+            new.pop(n2)
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}` → `{n2}`", f'{n} = "{r.text}"', f'{n2} = "{r.text}"',
+                              head[n2].where, "changed", {API: 1} if r.path.startswith("apis/") else {},
+                              "Go identifier renamed; value unchanged"))
+            continue
+        fl = {}
+        if r.kind == "key":
+            fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(r.kind)} `{r.text}` removed"
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{r.text}"', ABSENT, r.where, "removed", fl,
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n, r in sorted(new.items()):
+        out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, f'"{r.text}"', r.where, "added", {},
+                          _kind_word(r.kind)))
+    for n in sorted(set(base) & set(head)):
+        b, h = base[n], head[n]
+        if b.text != h.text:
+            fl = {}
+            if h.kind in ("key", "reason", "condition"):
+                fl[ACTION] = f"{_kind_word(h.kind)} `{b.text}` renamed to `{h.text}`"
+            out.append(Change("Constant", f"`{n}`", f'"{b.text}"', f'"{h.text}"', h.where, "changed", fl,
+                              _kind_word(h.kind)))
+    return out
+
+
+def _kind_word(k: str) -> str:
+    return {"key": "label/annotation key", "reason": "reason", "condition": "condition type"}.get(k, "constant")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- metrics
+
+METRIC_RE = re.compile(r"\bprometheus\.New(Counter|Gauge|Histogram|Summary)(Vec)?\(")
+
+
+def metrics(gf: GoFile, env: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    out = {}
+    for m, args, _ in gf.calls(METRIC_RE):
+        if not args:
+            continue
+        opts = _composite(args[0])
+        name = string_value(opts.get("Name", ""), env)
+        if not name:
+            continue
+        ns = string_value(opts.get("Namespace", '""'), env) or ""
+        sub = string_value(opts.get("Subsystem", '""'), env) or ""
+        full = "_".join(x for x in (ns, sub, name) if x)
+        labels: list[str] = []
+        dynamic = False
+        if m.group(2) and len(args) > 1:
+            labels = literals_in(args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r'"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`', "", args[1])
+            rest = re.sub(r"\[\]string|[{},\s]", "", rest)
+            dynamic = bool(rest)
+        help_text = " ".join((string_value(opts.get("Help", '""'), env) or "").split())
+        out[full] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(m.start()), gf.func_at(m.start()), m.group(1).lower(), full,
+                        {"labels": labels, "dynamic": dynamic, "help": help_text,
+                         "buckets": " ".join(opts.get("Buckets", "").split())})
+    return out
+
+
+def _composite(expr: str) -> dict[str, str]:
+    from .gosrc import composite_fields
+    return composite_fields(expr)
+
+
+def diff_metrics(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", _mcell(b), ABSENT, b.where, "removed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` removed"}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, _mcell(h), h.where, "added", {}))
+            continue
+        if b.kind != h.kind:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` type", b.kind, h.kind, h.where, "changed",
+                              {ACTION: f"metric `{n}` type changed"}))
+        bl, hl = b.attrs["labels"], h.attrs["labels"]
+        if bl != hl or b.attrs["dynamic"] != h.attrs["dynamic"]:
+            gone = [x for x in bl if x not in hl]
+            fl = {}
+            if gone:
+                new = [x for x in hl if x not in bl]
+                what = "renamed" if new else "removed"
+                fl[ACTION] = f"metric `{n}` label {what} ({', '.join(gone)}" + (
+                    f" → {', '.join(new)})" if new else ")")
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` labels", _labels(b), _labels(h), h.where, "changed", fl))
+        if b.attrs["help"] != h.attrs["help"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` help", b.attrs["help"], h.attrs["help"], h.where, "changed", {}))
+        if b.attrs["buckets"] != h.attrs["buckets"]:
+            out.append(Change("Metric", f"`{n}` buckets", b.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT,
+                              h.attrs["buckets"] or ABSENT, h.where, "changed", {}))
+    return out
+
+
+def _labels(r: Rec) -> str:
+    s = ", ".join(r.attrs["labels"]) or "(none)"
+    return s + (" + dynamic" if r.attrs["dynamic"] else "")
+
+
+def _mcell(r: Rec) -> str:
+    return f"{r.kind} [{_labels(r)}]"
+
+
+DOC_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\|\s*`([a-z0-9_:]+)`\s*\|\s*([^|]*)\|(.*)\|\s*(.*?)\s*\|\s*$", re.M)
+
+
+def metrics_doc(text: str | None) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
+    out = {}
+    for m in DOC_ROW_RE.finditer(text or ""):
+        labels = re.findall(r"(?:^|<br>)\s*`([a-z0-9_]+)`", m.group(4))
+        out[m.group(1)] = labels
+    return out
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- feature gates
+
+SPEC_RE = re.compile(r'\{\s*Version:\s*version\.MustParse\("([^"]+)"\)\s*,\s*Default:\s*(true|false)\s*,'
+                     r'\s*PreRelease:\s*(?:featuregate\.)?(\w+)(?:\s*,\s*LockToDefault:\s*(true|false))?\s*\}')
+
+
+def feature_gates(gf: GoFile) -> dict[str, Rec]:
+    m = re.search(r"defaultVersionedFeatureGates\s*=\s*map\[[^\]]+\][\w.]+\{", gf.src)
+    if not m:
+        return {}
+    items, _ = _group_from(gf.src, m.end() - 1)
+    out = {}
+    for it in items:
+        km = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_][\w.]*)\s*:\s*(\{.*\})\s*\Z", it, re.S)
+        if not km:

+        specs = [(v, d == "true", pre, lock == "true") for v, d, pre, lock in SPEC_RE.findall(km.group(2))]
+        if not specs:
+            continue
+        name = km.group(1).rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
+        pos = m.end() + gf.src[m.end():].find(km.group(1) + ":")
+        out[name] = Rec(gf.path, gf.line(pos), "", "gate", name, {"specs": specs})
+    return out
+
+
+def _group_from(src: str, i: int):
+    from .gosrc import group_args
+    return group_args(src, i)
+
+
+def gate_cell(specs) -> str:
+    v, d, pre, lock = specs[-1]
+    return f"{pre} {'on' if d else 'off'} @v{v}" + (" (locked)" if lock else "")
+
+
+def diff_gates(base: dict[str, Rec], head: dict[str, Rec]) -> list[Change]:
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(set(base) | set(head)):
+        b, h = base.get(n), head.get(n)
+        if b and not h:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", gate_cell(b.attrs["specs"]), "removed", b.where,
+                              "removed", {API: 1, ACTION: f"feature gate `{n}` removed"} if not
+                              b.attrs["specs"][-1][3] else {API: 1}))
+            continue
+        if h and not b:
+            out.append(Change("Feature gate", f"`{n}`", ABSENT, gate_cell(h.attrs["specs"]), h.where,
+                              "added", {API: 1}, meta={"gate": n, "specs": h.attrs["specs"]}))
+            continue
+        bs, hs = b.attrs["specs"][-1], h.attrs["specs"][-1]

+"""Minimal scanning helpers for gofmt'd Go source (no Go toolchain needed).
+
+Everything works on the output of ``blank_comments`` so offsets stay aligned
+with the original text while comments can never be mistaken for code.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations

+import bisect
+import re

+LIT_RE = re.compile(r'"(?:[^"\\\n]|\\.)*"|`[^`]*`')
+IDENT_RE = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\.[A-Za-z_]\w*)*")
+FUNC_RE = re.compile(r"^func\s+(?:\(\s*(?:\w+\s+)?\*?([\w.\[\], ]+?)\s*\)\s*)?([A-Za-z_]\w*)", re.M)
+_ESCAPES = {'"': '"', "\\": "\\", "'": "'"}
+_OPEN = {"(": ")", "{": "}", "[": "]"}
+
+
+def skip_literal(src: str, i: int) -> int:
+    """Return the index just past the string/rune literal starting at src[i]."""
+    q = src[i]
+    n = len(src)
+    if q == "`":
+        k = src.find("`", i + 1)
+        return n if k < 0 else k + 1
+    j = i + 1
+    while j < n:
+        ch = src[j]
+        if ch == "\\":
+            j += 2
+            continue
+        if ch == q:
+            return j + 1
+        if ch == "\n":
+            return j
+        j += 1
+    return n
+
+
+def blank_comments(src: str) -> str:
+    """Replace // and /* */ comments with spaces (newlines kept)."""
+    out = list(src)
+    i, n = 0, len(src)
+    while i < n:
+        c = src[i]
+        if c == "/" and i + 1 < n and src[i + 1] == "/":
+            j = src.find("\n", i)
+            j = n if j < 0 else j
+            out[i:j] = " " * (j - i)
+            i = j
+        elif c == "/" and i + 1 < n and src[i + 1] == "*":
+            j = src.find("*/", i + 2)
+            j = n if j < 0 else j + 2
+            for k in range(i, j):
+                if out[k] != "\n":
+                    out[k] = " "
+            i = j
+        elif c in "\"`'":
+            i = skip_literal(src, i)
+        else:
+            i += 1

+

+    """Decode a Go string literal for display (only quote/backslash escapes)."""

+    body = lit[1:-1]
+    return re.sub(r"\\(.)", lambda m: _ESCAPES.get(m.group(1), m.group(0)), body)

+        c = expr[i]
+        if c in "\"`'":

+        elif c in ")}]":
+            depth -= 1
+        elif c == sep and depth == 0:
+            parts.append(expr[start:i])
+            start = i + 1
+        i += 1
+    parts.append(expr[start:])
+    return [p.strip() for p in parts]
+

+def string_value(expr: str, env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> str | None:
+    """Value of a string literal, a `+` concatenation of literals, or (with env)
+    identifiers resolving to string constants. None when not static."""
+    expr = (expr or "").strip()
+    if not expr:
+        return None
+    out = []
+    for tok in split_top(expr, "+"):
+        m = LIT_RE.fullmatch(tok)
+        if m:
+            out.append(decode(tok))
+            continue

+            name = tok.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
+            if tok in env:
+                out.append(env[tok])
+                continue
+            if name in env:
+                out.append(env[name])
+                continue
+        return None
+    return "".join(out)
+
+
+def literals_in(expr: str) -> list[str]:
+    return [decode(m.group(0)) for m in LIT_RE.finditer(expr or "")]
+
+
+def group_args(src: str, i: int) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
+    """src[i] is an opening ( or {; return top-level comma-separated items and
+    the index just past the matching closer."""
+    closer = _OPEN[src[i]]
+    depth, start, j, n = 0, i + 1, i, len(src)
+    args: list[str] = []
+    while j < n:
+        c = src[j]
+        if c in "\"`'":
+            j = skip_literal(src, j)
+            continue
+        if c in _OPEN:
+            depth += 1
+        elif c in ")}]":
+            depth -= 1
+            if depth == 0:
+                last = src[start:j].strip()
+                if last:
+                    args.append(last)
+                return args, j + 1
+        elif c == "," and depth == 1:
+            args.append(src[start:j].strip())
+            start = j + 1
+        j += 1
+    return args, n
+
+
+def composite_fields(expr: str) -> dict[str, str]:
+    """Key: value fields of a composite literal such as `prometheus.GaugeOpts{...}`."""
+    k = expr.find("{")
+    if k < 0:
+        return {}
+    items, _ = group_args(expr, k)
+    out = {}
+    for it in items:
+        m = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*:\s*(.*)\Z", it, re.S)
+        if m:
+            out[m.group(1)] = m.group(2).strip()
+    return out

+
+class GoFile:
+    """A parsed view of one Go source file at one revision."""

+    def __init__(self, path: str, src: str):
+        self.path = path
+        self.raw = src

+            end = len(self.src) if end < 0 else end + 2

  **End function spans at the matching brace.**
  
  When a one-line function precedes a package sentinel, the scanner treats the sentinel as function-local. A newly added webhook or kueuectl return of that sentinel can disappear from the report. Match the function’s closing brace while skipping literals.

+"""Tiny YAML subset loader for controller-gen CRD output (python3 stdlib only).
+
+Supports block mappings and sequences (including `- key: v` items and
+sequences at the parent key's indent), plain multi-line scalars, single and
+double quoted (multi-line) scalars, literal/folded block scalars (|, |-, |+,
+>, >-), empty flow collections and simple flow sequences, comments on their
+own line, and multiple documents. That is everything controller-gen and
+sigs.k8s.io/yaml emit; anchors, tags and complex keys are out of scope.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+
+_INT_RE = re.compile(r"^[-+]?(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*)$")
+_FLOAT_RE = re.compile(r"^[-+]?(?:\.[0-9]+|[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]*)?)(?:[eE][-+]?[0-9]+)?$")
+
+
+class YAMLError(ValueError):
+    pass
+
+
+def _indent(line: str) -> int:
+    return len(line) - len(line.lstrip(" "))
+
+
+def _is_blank(line: str) -> bool:
+    s = line.strip()
+    return not s or s.startswith("#")
+
+
+def _strip_comment(s: str) -> str:
+    return re.sub(r"\s+#.*$", "", s)
+
+
+def _plain(s: str):
+    if s in ("", "~", "null", "Null", "NULL"):
+        return None
+    if s in ("true", "True", "TRUE"):
+        return True
+    if s in ("false", "False", "FALSE"):
+        return False
+    if _INT_RE.match(s):
+        return int(s)
+    if _FLOAT_RE.match(s) and any(c.isdigit() for c in s):
+        return float(s)
+    return s
+
+
+def _split_key(text: str):
+    """Return (key, rest) if text is `key: rest` / `key:`; else None."""
+    if text[:1] in ("'", '"'):
+        q = text[0]
+        i = 1
+        while i < len(text):
+            if text[i] == q:
+                if q == "'" and text[i + 1:i + 2] == "'":
+                    i += 2
+                    continue
+                break
+            if q == '"' and text[i] == "\\":
+                i += 1
+            i += 1
+        key = _unquote(text[:i + 1])
+        rest = text[i + 1:]
+        if rest.startswith(":") and (len(rest) == 1 or rest[1] == " "):
+            return key, rest[1:].strip()
+        return None
+    m = re.match(r"^([^#'\"{\[][^:]*?|[^:#\s][^:]*?):(?: (.*)|$)", text)
+    if not m:
+        return None
+    return m.group(1).strip(), (m.group(2) or "").strip()
+
+
+def _unquote(s: str) -> str:
+    if s.startswith("'"):
+        return s[1:-1].replace("''", "'")
+    body = s[1:-1]
+    out, i = [], 0
+    esc = {"n": "\n", "t": "\t", '"': '"', "\\": "\\", "/": "/", "r": "\r", "0": "\0", " ": " "}
+    while i < len(body):
+        c = body[i]
+        if c == "\\" and i + 1 < len(body):
+            n = body[i + 1]
+            if n == "u":
+                out.append(chr(int(body[i + 2:i + 6], 16)))
+                i += 6
+                continue
+            if n == "x":
+                out.append(chr(int(body[i + 2:i + 4], 16)))
+                i += 4
+                continue
+            out.append(esc.get(n, n))
+            i += 2
+            continue
+        out.append(c)
+        i += 1
+    return "".join(out)
+
+
+def _fold(parts: list[str]) -> str:
+    """Join flow-scalar continuation lines: single break -> space, blank -> \\n."""
+    out = ""
+    pending_nl = 0
+    for k, p in enumerate(parts):
+        if p == "":
+            pending_nl += 1
+            continue
+        if k == 0 or not out:
+            out += ("\n" * pending_nl) + p if out else p
+        elif pending_nl:
+            out += "\n" * pending_nl + p
+        else:
+            out += " " + p
+        pending_nl = 0
+    return out
+
+
+class _Parser:
+    def __init__(self, text: str):
+        self.lines = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n")
+        self.n = len(self.lines)
+
+    def _next(self, i: int) -> int:
+        while i < self.n and _is_blank(self.lines[i]):
+            i += 1
+        return i
+
+    def documents(self):
+        docs, i = [], 0
+        while True:
+            i = self._next(i)
+            if i >= self.n:
+                break
+            if self.lines[i].startswith("---"):
+                i += 1
+                continue
+            if self.lines[i].startswith("..."):
+                i += 1
+                continue
+            val, i = self.node(i, _indent(self.lines[i]))
+            docs.append(val)
+        return docs
+
+    def node(self, i: int, indent: int):
+        i = self._next(i)
+        if i >= self.n:
+            return None, i
+        line = self.lines[i]
+        text = line[indent:]
+        if text == "-" or text.startswith("- "):
+            return self.seq(i, indent)
+        return self.mapping(i, indent)
+
+    def seq(self, i: int, indent: int):
+        out = []
+        while True:
+            i = self._next(i)
+            if i >= self.n:
+                break
+            line = self.lines[i]
+            if _indent(line) != indent or line.startswith("---"):
+                break
+            text = line[indent:]
+            if not (text == "-" or text.startswith("- ")):
+                break
+            rest = text[1:].lstrip(" ")
+            if not rest:
+                val, i = self.node(i + 1, self._child_indent(i + 1, indent))
+                out.append(val)
+                continue
+            inner = indent + (len(text) - len(rest))
+            if _split_key(rest) is not None and not rest.startswith(("'", '"')) or (
+                    rest[:1] in ("'", '"') and _split_key(rest) is not None):
+                self.lines[i] = " " * inner + rest
+                val, i = self.mapping(i, inner)
+                out.append(val)
+            else:

+    def _child_indent(self, i: int, parent: int) -> int:
+        j = self._next(i)
+        if j >= self.n:
+            return parent + 2
+        return _indent(self.lines[j])
+
+    def mapping(self, i: int, indent: int):
+        out = {}
+        while True:
+            i = self._next(i)
+            if i >= self.n:
+                break
+            line = self.lines[i]
+            if _indent(line) != indent or line.startswith(("---", "...")):
+                if _indent(line) > indent:
+                    raise YAMLError(f"line {i + 1}: unexpected indentation")

+    def block(self, header: str, i: int, indent: int):

+        chomp = m.group(2) or m.group(4)
+        explicit = m.group(3)
+        j = i + 1
+        body: list[str] = []
+        block_indent = indent + int(explicit) if explicit else None
+        while j < self.n:
+            ln = self.lines[j]
+            if ln.strip() == "":
+                body.append("")
+                j += 1
+                continue
+            ind = _indent(ln)
+            if block_indent is None:
+                if ind <= indent:
+                    break
+                block_indent = ind
+            if ind < block_indent:
+                break
+            body.append(ln[block_indent:])
+            j += 1
+        trailing = 0
+        while body and body[-1] == "":
+            body.pop()
+            trailing += 1
+        if style == "|":
+            text = "\n".join(body)
+        else:
+            text = ""
+            prev_more = False
+            for k, ln in enumerate(body):
+                more = ln.startswith((" ", "\t"))
+                if k == 0:
+                    text = ln
+                elif ln == "":
+                    text += "\n"
+                elif more or prev_more or text.endswith("\n"):
+                    text += ("\n" if not text.endswith("\n") else "") + ln
+                else:
+                    text += " " + ln
+                prev_more = more
+        if chomp == "-":
+            pass
+        elif chomp == "+":
+            text += "\n" * (trailing + 1) if body else "\n" * trailing
+        elif body:

+        return text, j
+
+    def scalar(self, rest: str, i: int, indent: int):
+        """Plain or quoted scalar starting on line i (possibly multi-line)."""
+        if rest[0] in ("'", '"'):
+            q = rest[0]
+            buf = [rest]
+            j = i
+            while not self._closed(" ".join(buf), q):
+                j += 1
+                if j >= self.n:
+                    raise YAMLError(f"line {i + 1}: unterminated quoted scalar")
+                buf.append(self.lines[j].strip())

+            for p in buf[1:]:

+            end = self._quote_end(raw, q)
+            return _unquote(raw[:end + 1]), j + 1

+            return self.flow(rest, i)
+        parts = [_strip_comment(rest)]
+        j = i + 1
+        while j < self.n:
+            ln = self.lines[j]
+            if ln.strip() == "":
+                parts.append("")
+                j += 1
+                continue
+            if _indent(ln) <= indent or ln.lstrip().startswith("#"):
+                break
+            parts.append(_strip_comment(ln.strip()))
+            j += 1
+        while parts and parts[-1] == "":
+            parts.pop()
+            j -= 1
+        while j > i + 1 and self.lines[j - 1].strip() == "":
+            j -= 1
+        text = _fold(parts)
+        return (_plain(text) if len(parts) == 1 else text), j
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _quote_end(s: str, q: str) -> int:
+        i = 1
+        while i < len(s):
+            if s[i] == q:
+                if q == "'" and s[i + 1:i + 2] == "'":
+                    i += 2
+                    continue

+            if q == '"' and s[i] == "\\":
+                i += 1
+            i += 1

+
+    def _closed(self, s: str, q: str) -> bool:
+        return self._quote_end(s, q) >= 0

+    def flow(self, rest: str, i: int):
+        s = rest.strip()
+        if s in ("[]", "{}"):
+            return ([] if s == "[]" else {}), i + 1
+        if s.startswith("[") and s.endswith("]"):

+        if s.startswith("{") and s.endswith("}"):

+                if x.strip():
+                    kv = _split_key(x.strip())
+                    if kv:
