```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,168 @@
+# Copyright 2026 The Kubernetes Authors.
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+"""Markdown block for inject-pr-notes (content only: no markers, no header)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections import OrderedDict
+
+from .core import SURFACES_CHECKED, Report
+from .goextract import level_histogram
+from .model import ABSENT, ACTION, BREAKING, SURFACE_ORDER, Change, short
+
+RELEASE_NOTES_SKILL = "cmd/experimental/skills/kueue-release-notes"
+# Content budget: the injector adds one header line; the family budget is 25.
+DEFAULT_BUDGET = 24
+MAX_DETAIL_ROWS = 120
+COLLAPSE_AT = 3
+WIDE = 110  # before/after cell width when a surface is shown row by row
+
+
+def empty_line() -> str:
+    return f"No user-facing contract change detected (surfaces checked: {', '.join(SURFACES_CHECKED)})."
+
+
+def cell(s: str, n: int = 60) -> str:
+    s = short(s, n) if n else " ".join(str(s).split())
+    s = s.replace("|", "\\|")
+    if s in ("", ABSENT):
+        return ABSENT
+    return s
+
+
+def code(s: str, n: int = 60) -> str:
+    """Inline-code cell (backticks stripped from content to keep markdown sane)."""
+    c = cell(str(s).replace("`", "'"), n)
+    return c if c == ABSENT else f"`{c}`"
+
+
+def _counts(items: list[Change]) -> str:
+    n_add = sum(c.kind == "added" for c in items)
+    n_rm = sum(c.kind == "removed" for c in items)
+    bits = [(len(items) - n_add - n_rm, "changed"), (n_add, "added"), (n_rm, "removed")]
+    return ", ".join(f"{n} {w}" for n, w in bits if n)
+
+
+def _files(items: list[Change]) -> str:
+    return ", ".join(sorted({(c.group or c.where.split(":")[0]).rsplit("/", 1)[-1] for c in items}))
+
+
+def _important_first(items: list[Change]) -> list[Change]:
+    return sorted(items, key=lambda c: (0 if c.flags.get(BREAKING) else 1, 0 if c.flags.get(ACTION) else 1))
+
+
+def _log_row(items: list[Change]) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
+    moved = sum(1 for c in items if c.kind == "changed" and c.meta.get("level_before") != c.meta.get("level_after"))
+    renamed = sum(1 for c in items if c.flags.get(ACTION))
+    reworded = sum(1 for c in items if "reworded" in c.note)
+    n_add = sum(c.kind == "added" for c in items)
+    n_rm = sum(c.kind == "removed" for c in items)
+    what = (f"{len(items)} log calls in {short(_files(items), 40)}: {moved} V-level moves, {reworded} reworded, "
+            f"{n_add} added, {n_rm} removed" + (f", {renamed} key rename{'s' if renamed != 1 else ''}"
+                                                 if renamed else ""))
+    return ("Log", cell(what, 0), level_histogram(items, "level_before"), level_histogram(items, "level_after"))
+
+
+def _surface_rows(surface: str, items: list[Change], mode: str) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, str]]:
+    """mode: "full" (row per change up to COLLAPSE_AT, else 2 + summary) or "one" (single row)."""
+    if surface == "Log" and (len(items) > COLLAPSE_AT or mode == "one"):
+        return [_log_row(items)]
+    if len(items) <= COLLAPSE_AT and mode == "full":
+        return [(surface, cell(c.item + (f" ({c.note})" if c.note and surface != "Log" else ""), WIDE),
+                 code(c.before, WIDE), code(c.after, WIDE)) for c in items]
+    first = _important_first(items)
+    if mode == "one":
+        c = first[0]
+        more = f" (+{len(items) - 1} more; {len(items)} total: {_counts(items)})" if len(items) > 1 else ""
+        return [(surface, cell(c.item, 60) + more, code(c.before), code(c.after))]
+    rows = [(surface, cell(c.item, 70), code(c.before), code(c.after)) for c in first[:2]]
+    rows.append((surface, cell(f"… {len(items) - 2} more (of {len(items)}: {_counts(items)}) in {_files(items)}",
+                               0), "see details", ""))
+    return rows
+
+
+def render(rep: Report, budget: int = DEFAULT_BUDGET) -> str:
+    if not rep.changes:
+        return empty_line() + "\n"
```

  **Include notes in the no-change output.**
  
  When a CRD description changes without an update to its Helm copy, the collector adds a warning but produces no changes. Markdown drops the warning, although JSON retains it. Include notes in the no-change line so reviewers see the warning while the block remains one line.

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,150 @@
+---
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta
+description: Show reviewers a before/after table of user-facing contract changes. The PR author's coding agent compares the merge base and the head and chooses which surfaces matter for this PR. Use when a PR changes an API, CRD, flag, config, metric, or other user-facing contract.
+license: Apache-2.0
+metadata:
+  copyright: The Kubernetes Authors
+---
+
+# Skill: Before/after user-facing contract for a PR
+
+The PR author's coding agent produces a before/after table of user-facing
+contract changes for reviewers to read. The comparison is a static read of
+the merge base and the head. The PR's tests remain the regression tests.
+
+Publishing follows
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+Start from the PR diff and choose the contract surfaces this change can
+affect. The rows depend on the PR. Pick them case by case. Use this skill
+when the diff touches a user-facing contract, or when a reviewer asks what
+changes for users or whether the release note is ACTION REQUIRED.
+
+For runtime behavior, use
+[kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff](../kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff/SKILL.md).
+For the release-note text, use
+[kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md). This skill flags
+that a note is likely ACTION REQUIRED. The author writes the note. When
+every surface you checked is unchanged, publish one line that names those
+surfaces.
+
+## What to compare
+
+Read each chosen item at the merge base and at the head. Keep a row only
+when the user-facing value differs. Choose from:
+
+- API and CRD. Fields, types, required markers, defaults, enums, bounds,
+  validation, CEL, printer columns, and the Configuration API. Read `apis/`
+  and `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+- Install config. The Helm CRD copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  plus controller flags and config files the PR edits.
+- Webhook admission messages.
+- Condition reasons, event reasons, and constants that users or other
+  controllers match on.
+- Metrics. Name, help text, and labels, plus
+  `site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md`.
+- Feature gates. New gates, default flips, and gates that become locked.
+- Logs. V-level moves and key renames. A reworded line counts when users
+  or alerts match the text.
+- `cmd/kueuectl/`. Flags, output columns, and error text.
+
+Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it.
```

```diff
+# Skill: Before/after user-facing contract for a PR
+
+The PR author's coding agent produces a before/after table of user-facing
+contract changes for reviewers to read. The comparison is a static read of
+the merge base and the head. The PR's tests remain the regression tests.
+
+Publishing follows
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+Start from the PR diff and choose the contract surfaces this change can
+affect. The rows depend on the PR. Pick them case by case. Use this skill
+when the diff touches a user-facing contract, or when a reviewer asks what
+changes for users or whether the release note is ACTION REQUIRED.
+
+For runtime behavior, use
+[kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff](../kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff/SKILL.md).
+For the release-note text, use
+[kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md). This skill flags
+that a note is likely ACTION REQUIRED. The author writes the note. When
+every surface you checked is unchanged, publish one line that names those
+surfaces.
+
+## What to compare
+
+Read each chosen item at the merge base and at the head. Keep a row only
+when the user-facing value differs. Choose from:
+
+- API and CRD. Fields, types, required markers, defaults, enums, bounds,
+  validation, CEL, printer columns, and the Configuration API. Read `apis/`
+  and `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+- Install config. The Helm CRD copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  plus controller flags and config files the PR edits.
+- Webhook admission messages.
+- Condition reasons, event reasons, and constants that users or other
+  controllers match on.
+- Metrics. Name, help text, and labels, plus
+  `site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md`.
+- Feature gates. New gates, default flips, and gates that become locked.
+- Logs. V-level moves and key renames. A reworded line counts when users
+  or alerts match the text.
+- `cmd/kueuectl/`. Flags, output columns, and error text.
+
+Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it.
+
+## How to compare
+
+Resolve the merge base and the base worktree as in
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md). Read the
+base files in that worktree and the head files at the PR head. Treat a
+rename as the same file.
+
+Pair the same field path, metric, flag, gate, reason, or message. A
+reworded message you still recognize is one changed row. Otherwise record
+one removal and one addition. Keep a format string as written in the
+source. When the surrounding webhook or CEL code also changed, say the
+behavior changed with the string.
+
+For an API or CRD change, judge compatibility from the files:
+
+- A removed field, a new required field, a narrower enum, a tighter bound,
+  or a changed type or default means existing manifests or clients break.
```

  **Distinguish changed defaults from broken manifests.**
  
  A default change affects requests that omit the field; manifests that set it explicitly can remain valid and retain their behavior. This rule instead reports every default change as a break. Record the affected cases separately from manifest acceptance, and mark compatibility unverified when the static evidence is insufficient. +---
```diff
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta
+description: Show reviewers a before/after table of user-facing contract changes. The PR author's coding agent compares the merge base and the head and chooses which surfaces matter for this PR. Use when a PR changes an API, CRD, flag, config, metric, or other user-facing contract.

+---
+
+# Skill: Before/after user-facing contract for a PR
+
+The PR author's coding agent produces a before/after table of user-facing
+contract changes for reviewers to read. The comparison is a static read of
+the merge base and the head. The PR's tests remain the regression tests.
+
+Publishing follows
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+Start from the PR diff and choose the contract surfaces this change can
+affect. The rows depend on the PR. Pick them case by case. Use this skill
+when the diff touches a user-facing contract, or when a reviewer asks what
+changes for users or whether the release note is ACTION REQUIRED.
+
+For runtime behavior, use
+[kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff](../kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff/SKILL.md).
+For the release-note text, use
+[kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md). This skill flags
+that a note is likely ACTION REQUIRED. The author writes the note. When
+every surface you checked is unchanged, publish one line that names those
+surfaces.
+
+## What to compare
+
+Read each chosen item at the merge base and at the head. Keep a row only
+when the user-facing value differs. Choose from:
+
+- API and CRD. Fields, types, required markers, defaults, enums, bounds,
+  validation, CEL, printer columns, and the Configuration API. Read `apis/`
+  and `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+- Install config. The Helm CRD copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  plus controller flags and config files the PR edits.
+- Webhook admission messages.
+- Condition reasons, event reasons, and constants that users or other
+  controllers match on.
+- Metrics. Name, help text, and labels, plus
+  `site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md`.
+- Feature gates. New gates, default flips, and gates that become locked.
+- Logs. V-level moves and key renames. A reworded line counts when users
+  or alerts match the text.
+- `cmd/kueuectl/`. Flags, output columns, and error text.
+
+Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it.
+
+## How to compare
+
+Resolve the merge base and the base worktree as in
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md). Read the
+base files in that worktree and the head files at the PR head. Treat a
+rename as the same file.
+
+Pair the same field path, metric, flag, gate, reason, or message. A
+reworded message you still recognize is one changed row. Otherwise record
+one removal and one addition. Keep a format string as written in the
+source. When the surrounding webhook or CEL code also changed, say the
+behavior changed with the string.
+
+For an API or CRD change, judge compatibility from the files:
+
+- A removed field, a new required field, a narrower enum, a tighter bound,
+  or a changed type or default means existing manifests or clients break.
+- A new optional field keeps the old behavior when omitting it still means
+  the same thing. Mark a default, or an "If unspecified" sentence,
+  unverified unless the code shows that omitted behavior.
+- Mark CEL rules, webhook branches, and gate-off behavior unverified.
+- A gate default flip changes behavior with no opt-in. A locked gate
+  rejects the opposite `--feature-gates` value at startup.
+
+When metrics change, compare names and labels with
+`site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md` and note a mismatch. When CRDs
+change, note a Helm copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/` that differs
+from `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+
+## Block shape
+
+One block, inside the 25 visible-line budget from the common skill. Show at
+most 12 changed rows. Columns are surface, change, base, and head. Omit
+rows that match.
+
+Visible part, in this order:
+
+1. One factual verdict sentence with the change count.
+2. One `Runner: static · surfaces: ...` line. `surfaces` names what you
+   compared for this PR.
+3. The changed-rows table. When one surface has many items, keep one
+   summary row here. For a large log move, that row counts V-level moves,
+   reworded lines, additions, removals, and key renames.
+4. A **Compatibility** table when an API or CRD change can affect existing
+   manifests or clients. Each result is a static finding, or `unverified`.
+5. One ACTION REQUIRED line when a default, label, required field, removed
+   field, flag, column, or gate lock forces a user change on upgrade.
```

### Review by [@vladikkuzn](https://github.com/vladikkuzn) (commented) — 2026-11-13T14:48:44Z

- **cmd/experimental/skills/kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta/SKILL.md:4** by [@vladikkuzn](https://github.com/vladikkuzn) — 2026-11-13T14:48:44Z
```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,95 @@
+---
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta
+description: Lists the user-facing contract changes in a Kueue PR as a before/after table built from the merge base and the head, with no cluster and no checkout. Covers CRD fields, defaults and validation, CEL and webhook messages, condition and event reasons, metrics, feature gates, log verbosity and keys, and kueuectl flags, columns and errors. Adds a compatibility check for API changes and flags a likely ACTION REQUIRED release note. Use when a reviewer asks what a PR changes for users, or before marking an API or UX change ready for review.
+license: Apache-2.0
```

  > [!NOTE]
  > 🤖 **Grok 4.7 responding on behalf of vladikkuzn**
  
  Declined. This describes the removed cdelta collector and its export filter. The current skill is guidance only: the agent includes constants that users or other controllers match on, choosing the surfaces for the PR in front of it. There is no collector to change, and naming `lwsNameLabel` would pin a fixed scenario.

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,155 @@
+---
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta
+description: Show reviewers a before/after table of user-facing contract changes. The PR author's coding agent compares the merge base and the head and chooses which surfaces matter for this PR. Use when a PR changes an API, CRD, flag, config, metric, or other user-facing contract.
+license: Apache-2.0
+metadata:
+  copyright: The Kubernetes Authors
+---
+
+# Skill: Before/after user-facing contract for a PR
+
+The PR author's coding agent produces a before/after table of user-facing
+contract changes for reviewers to read. The comparison is a static read of
+the merge base and the head. The PR's tests remain the regression tests.
+
+Publishing follows
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+Start from the PR diff and choose the contract surfaces this change can
+affect. The rows depend on the PR. Pick them case by case. Use this skill
+when the diff touches a user-facing contract, or when a reviewer asks what
+changes for users or whether the release note is ACTION REQUIRED.
+
+For runtime behavior, use
+[kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff](../kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff/SKILL.md).
+For the release-note text, use
+[kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md). This skill flags
+that a note is likely ACTION REQUIRED. The author writes the note. When
+every surface you checked is unchanged, publish one line that names those
+surfaces.
+
+## What to compare
+
+Read each chosen item at the merge base and at the head. Keep a row only
+when the user-facing value differs. Choose from:
+
+- API and CRD. Fields, types, required markers, defaults, enums, bounds,
+  validation, CEL, printer columns, and the Configuration API. Read `apis/`
+  and `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+- Install config. The Helm CRD copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  plus controller flags and config files the PR edits.
+- Webhook admission messages.
+- Condition reasons, event reasons, and constants that users or other
+  controllers match on.
+- Metrics. Name, help text, and labels, plus
+  `site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md`.
+- Feature gates. New gates, default flips, and gates that become locked.
+- Logs. V-level moves and key renames. A reworded line counts when users
+  or alerts match the text.
```

  If a log message is reworded but the agent cannot establish that a user or alert matches it, this rule omits the change. That can produce an incorrect log-change count. Count a recognized rewording as one change.
  
  The linked issue requires reworded messages to count as one change.

```diff
+# Skill: Before/after user-facing contract for a PR
+
+The PR author's coding agent produces a before/after table of user-facing
+contract changes for reviewers to read. The comparison is a static read of
+the merge base and the head. The PR's tests remain the regression tests.
+
+Publishing follows
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+Start from the PR diff and choose the contract surfaces this change can
+affect. The rows depend on the PR. Pick them case by case. Use this skill
+when the diff touches a user-facing contract, or when a reviewer asks what
+changes for users or whether the release note is ACTION REQUIRED.
+
+For runtime behavior, use
+[kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff](../kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff/SKILL.md).
+For the release-note text, use
+[kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md). This skill flags
+that a note is likely ACTION REQUIRED. The author writes the note. When
+every surface you checked is unchanged, publish one line that names those
+surfaces.
+
+## What to compare
+
+Read each chosen item at the merge base and at the head. Keep a row only
+when the user-facing value differs. Choose from:
+
+- API and CRD. Fields, types, required markers, defaults, enums, bounds,
+  validation, CEL, printer columns, and the Configuration API. Read `apis/`
+  and `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+- Install config. The Helm CRD copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  plus controller flags and config files the PR edits.
+- Webhook admission messages.
+- Condition reasons, event reasons, and constants that users or other
+  controllers match on.
+- Metrics. Name, help text, and labels, plus
+  `site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md`.
+- Feature gates. New gates, default flips, and gates that become locked.
+- Logs. V-level moves and key renames. A reworded line counts when users
+  or alerts match the text.
+- `cmd/kueuectl/`. Flags, output columns, and error text.
+
+Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it.
+
+## How to compare
+
+Resolve the merge base and the base worktree as in
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md). Read the
+base files in that worktree and the head files at the PR head. Treat a
+rename as the same file.
+
+Pair the same field path, metric, flag, gate, reason, or message. A
+reworded message you still recognize is one changed row. Otherwise record
+one removal and one addition. Keep a format string as written in the
+source. When the surrounding webhook or CEL code also changed, say the
+behavior changed with the string.
+
+For an API or CRD change, judge compatibility from the files:
+
+- A removed field, a new required field, a narrower enum, a tighter bound,
+  or a changed type means existing manifests or clients break.
```

  **Do not classify every type change as a break.**
  
  A type change does not by itself prove that existing manifests or clients break. For example, changing a schema from integer to number can continue to accept existing integer values. Compare the accepted values and client contract, or mark compatibility as unverified.

```diff
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md). Read the
+base files in that worktree and the head files at the PR head. Treat a
+rename as the same file.
+
+Pair the same field path, metric, flag, gate, reason, or message. A
+reworded message you still recognize is one changed row. Otherwise record
+one removal and one addition. Keep a format string as written in the
+source. When the surrounding webhook or CEL code also changed, say the
+behavior changed with the string.
+
+For an API or CRD change, judge compatibility from the files:
+
+- A removed field, a new required field, a narrower enum, a tighter bound,
+  or a changed type means existing manifests or clients break.
```

  > [!NOTE]
  > 🤖 **Claude Opus 5.5 responding on behalf of vladikkuzn**
  
  Declined. Kueue APIs are Go types, so a field type change breaks typed Go clients even when existing manifest values stay valid (for example `int32` to `int64`, or an integer schema widened to a number). Keeping "a changed type means existing manifests or clients break" covers the client side; the manifest side is still judged from the schema.

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,155 @@
+---
+name: kueue-pr-evidence-contract-delta
+description: Show reviewers a before/after table of user-facing contract changes. The PR author's coding agent compares the merge base and the head and chooses which surfaces matter for this PR. Use when a PR changes an API, CRD, flag, config, metric, or other user-facing contract.
+license: Apache-2.0
+metadata:
+  copyright: The Kubernetes Authors
+---
+
+# Skill: Before/after user-facing contract for a PR
+
+The PR author's coding agent produces a before/after table of user-facing
+contract changes for reviewers to read. The comparison is a static read of
+the merge base and the head. The PR's tests remain the regression tests.
+
+Publishing follows
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+Start from the PR diff and choose the contract surfaces this change can
+affect. The rows depend on the PR. Pick them case by case. Use this skill
+when the diff touches a user-facing contract, or when a reviewer asks what
+changes for users or whether the release note is ACTION REQUIRED.
+
+For runtime behavior, use
+[kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff](../kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff/SKILL.md).
+For the release-note text, use
+[kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md). This skill flags
+that a note is likely ACTION REQUIRED. The author writes the note. When
+every surface you checked is unchanged, publish one line that names those
+surfaces.
+
+## What to compare
+
+Read each chosen item at the merge base and at the head. Keep a row only
+when the user-facing value differs. Choose from:
+
+- API and CRD. Fields, types, required markers, defaults, enums, bounds,
+  validation, CEL, printer columns, and the Configuration API. Read `apis/`
+  and `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+- Install config. The Helm CRD copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  plus controller flags and config files the PR edits.
+- Webhook admission messages.
+- Condition reasons, event reasons, and constants that users or other
+  controllers match on.
+- Metrics. Name, help text, and labels, plus
+  `site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md`.
+- Feature gates. New gates, default flips, and gates that become locked.
+- Logs. V-level moves and key renames. A reworded line counts when users
+  or alerts match the text.
+- `cmd/kueuectl/`. Flags, output columns, and error text.
+
+Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it.
+
+## How to compare
+
+Resolve the merge base and the base worktree as in
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md). Read the
+base files in that worktree and the head files at the PR head. Treat a
+rename as the same file.
+
+Pair the same field path, metric, flag, gate, reason, or message. A
+reworded message you still recognize is one changed row. Otherwise record
+one removal and one addition. Keep a format string as written in the
+source. When the surrounding webhook or CEL code also changed, say the
+behavior changed with the string.
+
+For an API or CRD change, judge compatibility from the files:
+
+- A removed field, a new required field, a narrower enum, a tighter bound,
+  or a changed type means existing manifests or clients break.
```

```diff
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md).
+
+## When to use
+
+Start from the PR diff and choose the contract surfaces this change can
+affect. The rows depend on the PR. Pick them case by case. Use this skill
+when the diff touches a user-facing contract, or when a reviewer asks what
+changes for users or whether the release note is ACTION REQUIRED.
+
+For runtime behavior, use
+[kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff](../kueue-pr-evidence-state-diff/SKILL.md).
+For the release-note text, use
+[kueue-release-notes](../kueue-release-notes/SKILL.md). This skill flags
+that a note is likely ACTION REQUIRED. The author writes the note. When
+every surface you checked is unchanged, publish one line that names those
+surfaces.
+
+## What to compare
+
+Read each chosen item at the merge base and at the head. Keep a row only
+when the user-facing value differs. Choose from:
+
+- API and CRD. Fields, types, required markers, defaults, enums, bounds,
+  validation, CEL, printer columns, and the Configuration API. Read `apis/`
+  and `config/components/crd/bases/`.
+- Install config. The Helm CRD copy under `charts/kueue/templates/crd/`,
+  plus controller flags and config files the PR edits.
+- Webhook admission messages.
+- Condition reasons, event reasons, and constants that users or other
+  controllers match on.
+- Metrics. Name, help text, and labels, plus
+  `site/content/en/docs/reference/metrics.md`.
+- Feature gates. New gates, default flips, and gates that become locked.
```
```diff
+- Logs. V-level moves and key renames. A recognized reworded log line
+  counts as one change.
+- `cmd/kueuectl/`. Flags, output columns, and error text.
+
+Choose a surface only when the diff can affect it.
+
+## How to compare
+
+Resolve the merge base as in
+[kueue-pr-evidence-common](../kueue-pr-evidence-common/SKILL.md). Read both
+sides with `git show <merge-base>:<path>` and `git show <head>:<path>`.
```
```diff
+No worktree is needed. Treat a rename as the same file.
+
+Pair the same field path, metric, flag, gate, reason, or message. A
+reworded message you still recognize is one changed row. Otherwise record
+one removal and one addition. Keep a format string as written in the
+source. When the surrounding webhook or CEL code also changed, say the
+behavior changed with the string.
+
+For an API or CRD change, judge compatibility from the files:
+
+- A removed field, a new required field, a narrower enum, a tighter bound,
+  or a changed type means existing manifests or clients break.
```
